ואין בו סימן טהרה (no token of cleanness applies to it) – whereas with the spreading of a leprous spot there is a token of cleanness that applies to it, for if it spread all over, it is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:1:4)


**מְטַמֵּא בְכָל שֶׁהוּא.** אֲבָל שֵׂעָר לָבָן אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא בְּפָחוֹת מִשְּׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת, דְּמִעוּט שֵׂעָר שְׁתַּיִם:


מטמא בכל שהוא – but the white hair does not defile with less than two hairs, for the least of hair is two.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:1:5)


**חוּץ מִן הַנֶּגַע.** כְּשֶׁפָּשָׂה חוּץ לַנֶּגַע, טָמֵא. אֲבָל שֵׂעָר לָבָן בָּעִינַן שֶׁיְּהֵא בַנֶּגַע, כְּדִכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) וְשֵׂעָר בַּנֶּגַע הָפַךְ לָבָן:


חוץ מן הנגע – that when it spread outside of the plague, it is impure But regarding the white hair we require that it be in the plague, as it is written (Leviticus 13:3): "ושער בנגע הפך לבן"/“if hair in the affected patch turned white.”



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:2:1)


**בְּכָל מַרְאֶה.** הַיְנוּ כְּמַרְאֵה הַבָּשָׂר, בֵּין כּוּשִׁי בֵּין לָבָן בֵּין אָדֹם:


בכל מראה – that is like the appearance of the flesh whether abnormally dark-complexioned whether white or whether red.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:2:2)


**מְטַמֵּא בְכָל שֶׁהוּא.** אֲבָל בְּמִחְיָה בַּעְיָא כָעֲדָשָׁה:


מטמא בכל שהוא – but with quick flesh it requires like a lentil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:2:3)


**חוּץ מִן הַנֶּגַע, מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן בַּמִּחְיָה.** שֶׁהַמִּחְיָה צְרִיכָה לִהְיוֹת בְּתוֹךְ הַנֶּגַע:


חוץ מן הנגע מה שאין כן במחיה – for the quick flesh needs to be within the plague.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:3:1)


**וּבִמְכֻנָּס.** שֶׁהַשְּׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד בַּנֶּגַע:


ובמכונס (are close together) – that they two hairs are in one place in the plague.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:3:2)


**בִּמְפֻזָּר.** זֶה בְּמִזְרַח הַנֶּגַע וְזֶה בַמַּעֲרָב:


במפורד – this one on the east side of the plague and that one on the west side.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:3:3)


**בִּמְבֻצָּר.** שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת בְּאֶמְצַע הַנֶּגַע, כְּמִבְצָר שֶׁבְּאֶמְצַע הָעִיר:


מבוצר (encompassed) – two hairs in the middle of the plague, like a fortress that is in the middle of the city.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:3:4)


**שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְבֻצָּר.** בְּחוּט הַיּוֹצֵא מִן הַנֶּגַע, וְיֵשׁ בְּרָחְבּוֹ שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת. מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן בְּמִחְיָה דְּבָעִינַן מְבֻצֶּרֶת וּמְכֻנֶּסֶת:


אינו מבוצר – like a thread that comes out from the plague, and there is in its midst two hairs, what is not the case with the burning spot that we require [both] encompassed and close together.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:3:5)


**הֲפוּכָה וְשֶׁלֹּא הֲפוּכָה.** בֵּין שֶׁהַבַּהֶרֶת קָדְמָה לַמִּחְיָה, בֵּין שֶׁהַמִּחְיָה קָדְמָה לַבַּהֶרֶת. מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן בְּשֵׂעָר לָבָן, שֶׁאִם קָדַם לַבַּהֶרֶת, טָהוֹר:


הפוכה ושלא הפוכה (whether it was changed or not changed) – whether the bright white spot on the skin preceded the quick flesh , or whether the quick flesh preceded the bright white spot on the skin/בהרת. What is not the case concerning the white hair, for if it (i.e., the white hair) preceded the bright white spot on the skin, he is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:3:6)


**וּמְעַכֶּבֶת אֶת הַהוֹפֵךְ כֻּלּוֹ לָבָן.** דְּאִם הֻחְלַט בְּמִחְיָה, וּפָרְחָה הַצָּרַעַת בְּכֻלּוֹ חוּץ מִמְּקוֹם הַמִּחְיָה, עֲדַיִן טָמֵא הוּא. אֲבָל אִם פָּרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ, אַף עַל גַּב דְּכָל גּוּפוֹ מָלֵא שֵׂעָר לָבָן, טָהוֹר, שֶׁאֵין שֵׂעָר לָבָן מְעַכֵּב:


מעכבת את ההופך כולו לבן (hinders [uncleanness] in him who has turned entirely white) – if he was declared a definite leper with quick flesh, and the leprosy blossomed throughout him except for the place of the quick flesh, he is still impure. But if it blossomed throughout even though all of his hair is filled with white hair, he is pure, for the white hair does not hinder (i.e., is not indispensable).



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:4:1)


**עִקָּרָן מַשְׁחִיר וְרֹאשָׁן מַלְבִּין טָהוֹר.** דְּאָמַר קְרָא (ויקרא יג) וְשֵׂעָר בַּנֶּגַע הָפַךְ לָבָן, הָעִקָּר הַסָּמוּךְ לַנֶּגַע הָפַךְ לָבָן:


עיקרן משחיר וראשן מלבין טהור – as Scripture states (Leviticus 13:3): “if hair in the affected patch has turned white”/"ושער בנגע הפך לבן", the root hat is adjacent to/near the plague has turned white. -


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:4:2)


**כְּדֵי לִקְרֹץ בְּזוּג.** כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּנָּטֵל בְּפִי הַזּוּג שֶׁל מִסְפָּרַיִם. וַהֲלָכָה, דְּשִׁעוּר שֵׂעָר לָבָן, אָרְכּוֹ אֵין פָּחוֹת מִכְּדֵי שֶׁיִּנָּטֵל בְּפִי הַזּוּג, אֲבָל הַלַּבְנוּנִית שֶׁבּוֹ בְּכָל שֶׁהוּא:


כדי לקרוץ בזוג (sufficient to cut with scissors) – sufficient that he should take with the mouth of a pair of scissors. And the Halakha the measure of white hair, its length is not less than that he should take with the mouth of a scissors, but the whiteness that is in it is any amount at all.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:4:3)


**וְנִרְאֵית כִּשְׁתַּיִם טָהוֹר.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ שְׂעָרוֹת הַמְחֻלָּקוֹת בְּרֹאשָׁן לִשְׁתַּיִם וּבְעִקָּרָן אֵינָן אֶלָּא אֶחָת:


ונראית כשתים טהור – that thee are hairs that are divided at their top into two but at their root they are not other than one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:4:4)


**הָכִי גָרְסִינַן, בַּהֶרֶת וּבָהּ שֵׂעָר לָבָן וְשֵׂעָר שָׁחוֹר טָמֵא.** בַּהֶרֶת שֶׁהִיא כַגְּרִיס מְצֻמְצָם וּבָהּ שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת לְבָנוֹת, וְהַשְּׁאָר מְלֵאָה שְׂעָרוֹת שְׁחֹרוֹת, טָמֵא. וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן שֶׁמְּקוֹם הַשְּׂעָרוֹת שְׁחֹרוֹת מְמַעֵט הַבַּהֶרֶת מִכַּגְּרִיס:


בהרת ובה שער לבן ושער שחור טמא – a bright white spot on the skin that is like the size of a pressed [Cilician] bean and it has two white hairs, but the rest is filled with black hairs, it is impure. But we don’t say that the place of the black hairs limits the bright white spot on the skin from being like a split [Cilician] bean.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:5:1)


**זוֹקְקָהּ לְשֵׂעָר לָבָן וּלְפִסְיוֹן.** דְּאִי אִיכָּא בְאוֹתוֹ הַחוּט שֵׂעָר לָבָן, מַחְלִיטוֹ. אוֹ אִם פָּשָׂה אֵצֶל הַחוּט בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ, טָמֵא. אֲבָל אִם אֵין בְּרֹחַב הַחוּט שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת וְאִית בֵּיהּ שֵׂעָר לָבָן, לֹא מְטַמֵּא, דְּלָא חֲשִׁיב מִן הַנֶּגַע. וְכֵן אִם פָּשָׂה אֵצֶל הַחוּט שֶׁאֵין בְּרָחְבּוֹ שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת, כְּפִשְׂיוֹן רָחוֹק מִן הַנֶּגַע הוּא חָשׁוּב וּבָעֵי כַּגְּרִיס:


זוקקה לשער לבן ולפסיון (if [the streak/thread] subjects it [the bright spot] to [restrictions in respect to] white hair and spreading) – if there is in that thread a white hair, he is declared to be a definitive leper. Or if it spread near the thread at the end of a week, he is impure. But if there isn’t in the width of the thread two hairs but it has white hair he does not defile, for it is not considered from the plague. But if it spread near the thread and there isn’t in its width two hairs, it is considered like spreading far from the plague, and it requires like a split [Cilician] bean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:5:2)


**אֲבָל לֹא לְמִחְיָה.** דַּאֲפִלּוּ יֵשׁ בְּרֹחַב הַחוּט שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת, אֵין מִחְיָה שֶׁבּוֹ מְטַמְּאָה. שֶׁהַמִּחְיָה צְרִיכָה שֶׁתְּהֵא מְבֻצֶּרֶת בְּאֶמְצַע הַנֶּגַע כְּמִבְצָר שֶׁהוּא בְאֶמְצַע הָעִיר:


אבל לא למחיה – that even if here is in the width of the thread of two hairs, the quick flesh that is on it does not defile. For the quick flesh needs that will be encompassed in the middle of the plague like a fortress which is in the middle of the city.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:5:3)


**מְצָרְפָן.** וְנִדּוֹנִים כְּנֶגַע אֶחָד. וְאִם פָּשָׂה אַחַת מֵהֶן מַשֶּׁהוּ, אוֹ נוֹלַד שֵׂעָר לָבָן בְּאַחַת מֵהֶן, מַחְלִיט עַל שְׁתֵּיהֶן:


מצרפן – and they are judged as one plague. But if one of them spread a little bit or a white hair was forthcoming on one of them, he is declared to be a definitive leper on both of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:5:4)


**וְאִם לָאו אֵינוֹ מְצָרְפָן.** וְנִדּוֹנִים כִּשְׁנֵי נְגָעִים, וְאִם פָּשְׂתָה הָאַחַת וְהָאַחֶרֶת לֹא פָשְׂתָה, מַחְלִיט בְּזוֹ וּמַסְגִּיר בְּזוֹ. וְנַפְקָא מִנַּהּ, דִּלְכִי מִטְּהַר בָּעֵי לַאֲתוֹיֵי שְׁנֵי קָרְבָּנוֹת:


ואם לאו אימנן מצרפן – and they are judged as two plagues, but if one of them spread but the other one did not spread, he is declared to be a definitive leper on the one, and is shut up/isolated on the other. And the practical difference is that when he is declared to be pure, he is required to bring two sacrifices.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:6:1)


**בַּהֶרֶת כַּגְּרִיס.** בְּלֹא מְקוֹם מִחְיָה. דְּהַיְנוּ תֵּשַׁע עֲדָשׁוֹת בַּהֶרֶת, וַעֲדָשָׁה אַחַת מִחְיָה. עֶשֶׂר עֲדָשׁוֹת בֵּין הַכֹּל:


בהרת כגריס – but not the place of the quick flesh, that is nine lentils of the bright white spot on the skin, and one lentil of quick flesh, ten lentils among them all.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:6:2)


**גְּרִיס.** הֵן תֵּשַׁע עֲדָשׁוֹת, וּמִחְיָה אֵין פָּחוֹת מֵעֲדָשָׁה. וְכָל הֵיכָא דִתְנִינַן בַּהֶרֶת, הוּא הַדִּין לִשְׁאָר מַרְאוֹת חֲלָקוֹת וּפְתוּכוֹת:


גריס – they are nine lentils, and the quick flesh is not less than a lentil. Wherever that we teach בהרת/the bright white spot on the skin (i.e., eventually one of the symptoms of leprosy), the same law applies to the rest of the appearances that are smooth or variegated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:6:3)


**וְהָלְכָה הַמִּחְיָה.** שֶׁבָּא הַנֶּגַע עַל מְקוֹמָהּ:


והלכה המחיה – that the plague comes upon its place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:6:4)


**הָלַךְ שֵׂעָר לָבָן.** שֶׁנָּשַׁר, אוֹ הָפַךְ לְשָׁחֹר:


הלך שער לבן – that it fell off or changed to black/dark.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:6:5)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מְטַהֵר.** אַהָלְכָה מִחְיָה קָאֵי:


ר' שמעון מטהר – it refers to the quick flesh that disappeared.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:6:6)


**שֶׁלֹּא הֲפָכַתּוּ בַהֶרֶת.** שֶׁשֵּׂעָר לָבָן קָדַם לַבַּהֶרֶת שֶׁל מְקוֹם הַמִּחְיָה:


שלא הפכתו בהרת – that the white hair preceded the bright white spot on the skin of the place of the quick flesh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:6:7)


**שֶׁלֹּא הֲפָכַתּוּ בַהֶרֶת כַּגְּרִיס.** שֶׁשֵּׂעָר לָבָן קָדַם לַבַּהֶרֶת כַּגְּרִיס, דִּמְקוֹם הַמִּחְיָה הָיָה חָסֵר מִכַּגְּרִיס. וְכָאן נַמִּי לֹא פָלֵיג רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אֶלָּא אַהָלְכָה מִחְיָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


שלא הפכתו בהרת כגריס – that the white hair preceded the bright white spot on the skin like a split Cilician bean, for the place of the quick flesh was missing that of a Cilician bean, and here also, Rabbi Shimon does not dispute other than when the quick flesh disappears. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:1)


**בַּהֶרֶת וּבָהּ מִחְיָה וּפִשְׂיוֹן.** בַּהֶרֶת כַּגְּרִיס וְהִסְגִּירוֹ, וּלְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ מָצָא בוֹ מִחְיָה וּפִשְׂיוֹן וְהֶחְלִיטוֹ:


בהרת ובה מחיה ופשיון – a bright white spot on the skin (i.e., eventually one of the symptoms of leprosy) like a split Cilician bean and he has been shut in/put in isolation, and at the end of the week, he found in it the quick flesh and spreading and they declared him to be a certified leper.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:2)


**הָלְכָה הַמִּחְיָה.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁבָּא עָלֶיהָ הַנֶּגַע, טְמֵאָה הַבַּהֶרֶת מִפְּנֵי הַפִּשְׂיוֹן:


הלכה המחיה (the quick flesh disappeared – after a week of quarantine)– as for example, that the plague came upon, the bright white spot on the skin is impure because of the spreading.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:3)


**הָלַךְ הַפִּשְׂיוֹן.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁכָּנְסָה הַבַּהֶרֶת וְחָזְרָה לְכַגְּרִיס:


הלך הפשיון – as for example, that the bright white spot on the skin/בהרת came in and it returned to the size of a Cilician bean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:4)


**וְכֵן שֵׂעָר לָבָן וּפִשְׂיוֹן.** אִם הָלַךְ הָאֶחָד, טָמֵא מִפְּנֵי הַשֵּׁנִי:


וכן שער לבן ופשיון – if one of them disappeared, he is impure because of the second.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:5)


**הָלְכָה וְחָזְרָה בְסוֹף הַשָּׁבוּעַ.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁהִסְגִּירוֹ עַל בַּהֶרֶת כַּגְּרִיס, וְהָלְכָה הַבַּהֶרֶת בְּאֶמְצַע הַשָּׁבוּעַ, וּבְסוֹף הַשָּׁבוּעַ חָזְרָה:


הלכה וחזרה בסוף השבוע – as for example, that he was shut up/isolated on account of the bright white spot on the skin like the size of a split Cilician bean, and the bright white spot on the skin went away in the middle of the week, but at the end of the week it returned.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:6)


**הֲרֵי הִיא כְמוֹת שֶׁהָיְתָה.** וּמַסְגִּירוֹ הֶסְגֵּר שֵׁנִי:


הרי היא כמות שהיתה – and they shut him up/isolated him a second time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:7)


**לְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר.** דְּרָאָה אוֹתָהּ הַכֹּהֵן בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ כְּשֶׁהָלְכָה לָהּ וּפְטָרוֹ, אִם אַחֲרֵי כֵן חָזְרָה, תֵּרָאֶה בַתְּחִלָּה. וְהַאי חָזְרָה הַיְנוּ בִּמְקוֹמָהּ הָרִאשׁוֹן, דְּאִי בְמָקוֹם אַחֵר, אִם כֵּן הֲרֵי זוֹ בַּהֶרֶת אַחֶרֶת:


לאחר הפטור – that the Kohen saw it (i.e., the bright white spot on the skin) at the end of the week and when it disappeared, he released him, if after that it returned, it would appear at the beginning. ( And this return – that is in its first (i.e., original) place, for if it was in in another place, if so this is another bright spot on the skin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:8)


**הָיְתָה עַזָּה.** כַּשֶּׁלֶג, כְּשֶׁהִסְגִּירוֹ: **וְנַעֲשֵׂית כֵּהָה.**


היתה עזה – like snow when he shut him up/isolated him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:9)


**בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן, כְּגוֹן כְּסִיד אוֹ כִקְרוּם.** אוֹ שֶׁהָיְתָה כִקְרוּם בִּשְׁעַת הֶסְגֵּר, וְנַעֲשֵׂית עַזָּה כַשֶּׁלֶג בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן:


ונעשית כהה – at the end of the first week, as for example, like lime or with a membrane, or that it was like a membrane at the time of his being shut up/put in isolation, and it became strong like snow at the end of the first week.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:10)


**הֲרֵי הִיא כְמוֹת שֶׁהָיְתָה.** וּטְעוּנָה הֶסְגֵּר שֵׁנִי, כְּמוֹ עָמַד בְּעֵינָיו:


הרי היא כמות שהיתה – and it requires a second isolation, like “it has remained unchanged”/"עמד בעיניו" (Leviticus 13:5).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:11)


**וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא תִתְמַעֵט מֵאַרְבָּעָה מַרְאוֹת.** שֶׁאִם נִתְמַעֲטָה מֵאַרְבָּעָה מַרְאוֹת, פּוֹטְרוֹ מִיָּד וְטָהוֹר:


ובלבד שלא תתמעט מארבע מראות (on condition that it did not diminish [to a shade less] than the four appearances – if it diminished from the four appearances, he (i.e., the Kohen) releases him immediately and he is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:12)


**כָּנְסָה וּפָשְׂתָה.** הֵיכָא דְהִסְגִּירָהּ בְּבַהֶרֶת כַּגְּרִיס, וּבְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ כָּנְסָה, וְאַחַר כָּךְ פָּשְׂתָה אֶת מְקוֹם הַכִּנּוּס וְלֹא יוֹתֵר. אוֹ שֶׁפָּשְׂתָה בְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ, וְאַחַר כָּךְ כָּנְסָה אֶת מְקוֹם הַפִּשְׂיוֹן וְלֹא יוֹתֵר:


כנסה ופשתה (if it contracted and spread) – where he was shut up/placed in isolation with the bright white spot on the skin like a split Cilician bean, and at the end of he week, it contracted, but after that it spread the place in the place of the contraction but not more. Or that it spread at the end of the week, and afterwards contracted the place of the spreading and not more.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:13)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא מְטַמֵּא.** שֶׁהֲרֵי פָשָׂה:


ר' עקיבא מטמא – for it certainly spread.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:7:14)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִים.** שֶׁאֵין זֶה פִשְׂיוֹן, אֶלָּא הֲרֵי הִיא כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיְתָה. וְאִי קָאֵי בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן, מַסְגִּירוֹ הֶסְגֵּר שֵׁנִי. וְאִי קָאֵי בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי, פּוֹטְרוֹ וְטָהוֹר:


וחכמים מטהרים – that this is not spreading, but it is as it was. But if it refers to the end of the first week, they shut him up/isolate him a second time. But if it refers to the end of the second week, they release him and he is ritually pure.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:8:1)


**וּפָשְׂתָה כַחֲצִי גְרִיס.** לְצַד הַמִּזְרָח:


ופשתה כחצי גריס – to the eastern side.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:8:2)


**וְהָלַךְ מִן הָאוֹם כַּחֲצִי גְרִיס.** לְצַד הַמַּעֲרָב:


והלך מן האום כחצי גריס – to the western side.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:8:3)


**תֵּרָאֶה בַתְּחִלָּה.** דַּחֲשִׁיב כְּנֶגַע אַחֵר, וְנֶגַע חָדָשׁ הוּא זֶה, וְיַסְגִּיר:


תיראה בתחלה (it is inspected anew) – for it is considered like another plague, and it is a new plague and he should be shut up/isolated [for it].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:8:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִים.** דְּלָא חֲשִׁיב פִּשְׂיוֹן אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן מוֹסִיף עַל שִׁעוּר הַנֶּגַע שֶׁהָיָה בַתְּחִלָּה, וְזֶה שֶׁאֵינוֹ עַכְשָׁיו אֶלָּא כַגְּרִיס, נֶחְשָׁב כָּרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁעָמַד בְּעֵינָיו, וְטָהוֹר:


וחכמים מאהרין – that it is not considered spreading unless it adds on the measurement of the plaque that it was at the outset, but this now is none other than like a split Cilician bean, it is considered like the first that remained unchanged and is pure.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:9:1)


**וּפָשְׂתָה כַחֲצִי גְרִיס וָעוֹד כוּ'‏ רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא מְטַמֵּא.** מִשּׁוּם וְעוֹד דְּאִשְׁתַּיַּר בָּהּ מִן הַפִּשְׂיוֹן. וּבְפָשְׂתָה לְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר אַיְרֵי:


ופשתה כחצי גריס ועוד וכו'. רבי עקיבא מטמא – because the “and more”/"ועוד" that remains there from the spreading. And we are speaking about that it spread after the clearance/release.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:9:2)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִין.** דְּכֵיוָן דַּאֲזַל לֵיהּ חֲצִי גְּרִיס מִן הָאוֹם, לֵיכָּא מִנֶּגַע חָדָשׁ אֶלָּא חֲצִי גְרִיס וְעוֹד:


וחכמים מטהרין – for since it went one-half of a split Cilician bean from the starting point of leprosy, it is not from a new plague but rather one-half of a split Cilician bean and more.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:9:3)


**וּפָשְׂתָה כַגְּרִיס וָעוֹד.** לְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר, וְאַחַר כָּךְ הָלְכָה לָהּ כָּל הָאוֹם וְלֹא נִשְׁתַּיֵּר אֶלָּא הַגְּרִיס וָעוֹד הֶחָדָשׁ:


ופשתה כגריס ועוד – after the release [from uncleanness], and afterwards, the starting point of leprosy disappeared and there didn’t remain anything other than the new split Cilician bean and [a bit] more.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:9:4)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא מְטַמֵּא.** דְּהָא פָשָׂה לֵיהּ מִשִּׁעוּר קַמָּא. וְרַבָּנָן סָבְרֵי, כֵּיוָן דַּאֲזַל לֵיהּ קַמָּא כֻּלֵּיהּ, וְהַאי נֶגַע דְּקָאֵי חָדָשׁ, תֵּרָאֶה בַתְּחִלָּה וְיַסְגִּיר:


רבי עקיבא מטמא – for it spread for him from the first measurement. But the Rabbis hold, since it went on [further], the first is all of it, but this plague that is referred to is new, that it should be inspected anew and he should be shut up/isolated.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:10:1)


**בַּהֶרֶת כַּגְּרִיס וּפָשְׂתָה כַגְּרִיס.** בְּשֶׁפָּשְׂתָה לְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר אַיְרֵי:


בהרת כגריס ופשתה כגריס – we are speaking that it spread after the clearance/release [from impurity].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:10:2)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא מְטַמֵּא.** דְּיֵשׁ כָּאן נֶגַע וְסִימַן טֻמְאָה עִמּוֹ:


ר' עקיבא מטמא – for there is here a plague and the sign of defilement with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:10:3)


**תֵּרָאֶה בַתְּחִלָּה.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּכְשֶׁתֵּרָאֶה בַתְּחִלָּה נַמִּי יְטַמְּאֶנָּה הַכֹּהֵן מִיָּד, דְּמִחְיָה וְשֵׂעָר לָבָן מְטַמְּאִים בַּתְּחִלָּה, מִכָּל מָקוֹם נַפְקָא מִנַּהּ דִּלְרַבָּנָן בָּעֵי אָתוֹיֵי קָרְבָּן אַכָּל חַד וְחַד. וּלְרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא כְּנֶגַע רִאשׁוֹן חֲשׁוּב, וְכִי מִתַּסֵּי מֵהַאי מַיְתֵי חַד קָרְבָּן. וּבְכָל הָנָךְ דִּפְלִיגֵי רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא וַחֲכָמִים בְּמַתְנִיתִין, הֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


תיראה בתחלה – for even though that it should be examined anew, the Kohen should also declare it impure immediately. And the quick flesh and white hair defile at the beginning, nevertheless, the practical difference is that according to the Rabbis, he needs to bring a sacrifice for each and every one, but according to Rabbi Akiba it is considered like the first plague, and when it (i.e., the plague) falls away, he brings one sacrifice. And in all of these where Rabbi Akiba and the Sages disagree in our Mishnah, the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:10:4)


**וְאֵין בָּהּ כְּלוּם.** אֵין בָּהּ שֵׂעָר לָבָן:


ואין בה כלום – it has no white hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:10:5)


**וּבָהּ שַׂעֲרָה אַחַת וְנוֹלְדָה בַהֶרֶת כוּ'‏ הֲרֵי זוֹ לְהַסְגִּיר.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁקָּדַם כַּגְּרִיס בַּהֶרֶת לַשֵּׂעָר הָאַחֲרוֹן, כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא קָדְמָה אֶת הָרִאשׁוֹן, לֹא מַהֲנֵי, דְּבָעִינַן שֶׁתַּקְדִּים כַּגְּרִיס בַּהֶרֶת לִשְׁתֵּי הַשְּׂעָרוֹת:


ובה שערה אחת ונולדה בהרת וכו' הרי זו להסגיר – and even though the bright white spot in the flesh like a Cilician bean came first to the last hair, since it didn’t come before the first [hair], it has no effect, for we require that the bright white spot in the flesh precedes like a split Ciliican bean to the two hairs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:10:6)


**בַּהֶרֶת כַּחֲצִי גְרִיס וּבָהּ שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת כוּ'‏ הֲרֵי זוֹ לְהַסְגִּיר.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דַּחֲצִי גְרִיס הָרִאשׁוֹן קָדַם לִשְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת, לֹא מַהֲנֵי, עַד שֶׁיַּקְדִּים הַגְּרִיס שָׁלֵם לִשְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת:


בהרת כחצי גריס ובה שתי שערות וכו' הרי זו להסגיר – and even though the one-half Cilician bean preceded the two hairs, it has no effect until the full split Cilician bean precedes the two hairs.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:11:1)


**וְנוֹלְדָה בַהֶרֶת כַּחֲצִי גְרִיס וּבָהּ שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת הֲרֵי זוֹ לְהַחְלִיט.** כָּל הֵיכָא דִתְנַן וּבָהּ שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת אוֹ וּבָהּ שַׂעֲרָה אַחַת, לָאו דַּאֲתוּ שְׂעָרוֹת וּבַהֶרֶת בַּהֲדֵי הֲדָדֵי, אֶלָּא דְּבַהֶרֶת קָדְמָה וְאַחַר כָּךְ בָּאוּ שְׂעָרוֹת:


ונולד בהרת כחצי גריס ובה שתי שערות כו' הרי זו להחלט – wherever it teaches [in the Mishnah]: "ובה שתי שערות"/ “and in it are two hairs” or "ובה שערה אחת" / “and in it is one hair”, it is not that the hairs and ""the bright white spot in the flesh/בהרת that come together, but rather, that the bright white spot in the flesh came first and afterwards came the hairs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:11:2)


**וְאִם שֵׂעָר לָבָן קוֹדֵם לְבַהֶרֶת טָהוֹר.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) וְהִיא הָפְכָה שֵׂעָר לָבָן, הַבַּהֶרֶת הִיא שֶׁגָּרְמָה לַשֵּׂעָר שֶׁיֵּהָפֵךְ לְלָבָן:


ואם שער לבן קדמה לבהרת טהור – as it is written (Leviticus 13:10): "[וראה הכהן והנה שאת-לבנה בעור] והיא הפכה שער לבן [ומחית בשר חי בשאת]."/ “[If the priest finds on the skin a white swelling] which has turned some hair white, [with a patch of un-discolored flesh in the swelling],” it is the bright white spot in the flesh which caused the hair to turn to a white color.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:11:3)


**סָפֵק טָמֵא.** סָפֵק אִם שֵׂעָר לָבָן קָדַם לַבַּהֶרֶת, אוֹ בַהֶרֶת קָדְמָה לְשֵׂעָר לָבָן, טָמֵא:


ספק טמא – there is doubt if the white hair preceded the bright white spot in the flesh/בהרת or that the bright white spot in the flesh preceded the white hair, it is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 4:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/4:11:4)


**וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ קִהָה.** גָּרְסִינַן. מִלְּשׁוֹן תִּקְהֶינָה שִׁנָּיו. כְּלוֹמַר, הָיוּ שִׁנָּיו קֵהוֹת מִדִּבְרֵי הָאוֹמֵר סָפֵק טָמֵא. דְּאִיהוּ סָבַר סָפֵק טָהוֹר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


ורבי יהושע קהה – we have the reading. From the language of (Jeremiah 31:29): “[In those days, they shall no longer say, ‘Parents have eaten sour grapes] and children’s teeth are blunted.”/"ושני בנים תקהינה". Meaning to say, that his teeth were blunted from stating the words that it is doubtfully impure, for he held that it is doubtfully pure. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:1:1)


**כָּל סְפֵק נְגָעִים.** חוּץ מִזֶּה. דְּסָפֵק בַּהֶרֶת קָדְמָה סָפֵק שֵׂעָר לָבָן קָדַם:


כל ספק נגעים חוץ מזה – [this relates to Chapter 4, Mishnah 11 towards the end of the Mishnah] – that the doubt regarding the bright white spot in the flesh preceded [or] the doubt about whether the white hair preceded.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:1:2)


**הֲרֵי הִיא כַסֶּלַע.** נַעֲשֵׂית רְחָבָה וּגְדוֹלָה כַסֶּלַע:


הרי היא כסלע – it became as wide and as large as a Sela.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:1:3)


**סָפֵק שֶׁהִיא הִיא.** וּרְאוּיָה לְהַחְלִיט שֶׁהֲרֵי פָשָׂה:


ספק היא היא – and it is worthy/fit [for the person] to be declared a certified leper, for it spread.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:1:4)


**סָפֵק שֶׁאַחֶרֶת בָּאָה תַחְתֶּיהָ.** וְהָרִאשׁוֹנָה הָלְכָה וּרְאוּיָה לְהַסְגִּיר, דְּאֵין פִּשְׂיוֹן מְטַמֵּא בַתְּחִלָּה:


ספק שאחרת באה תחתיה – that the first went away/disappeared and he is fit to be shut up/isolated, for spreading does not defile at the outset.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:1:5)


**טָמֵא.** וּמַחְלִיטוֹ:


ומחליטו – and he declares him to be a certified leper.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:2:1)


**הָלַךְ שֵׂעָר לָבָן.** שֶׁהֻחְלַט בַּעֲבוּרוֹ:


הלך שער לבן – that he had been certified as a leper for its sake [of the white hair].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:2:2)


**וְחָזַר שֵׂעָר לָבָן.** אַחֵר תַּחְתָּיו:


וחזר שער לבן – other in its place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:2:3)


**וְכֵן בְּמִחְיָה וְכֵן בְּפִשְׂיוֹן.** אוֹ שֶׁלֹּא חָזַר שֵׂעָר לָבָן, אֶלָּא שֶׁנּוֹלְדָה בוֹ מִחְיָה שֶׁהִיא גַם כֵּן סִימַן טֻמְאָה, אוֹ שֶׁפָּשָׂה הַנֶּגַע:


וכן במחיה וכן בפשיון – or that the white hair did not return, but rather that quick flesh resulted which is also a sign of defilement, or that the plague spread.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:2:4)


**בַּתְּחִלָּה בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן.** כְּלוֹמַר, בֵּין שֶׁהַהֶחְלֵט הָיָה בַתְּחִלָּה כְּשֶׁהוּבָא אֶל הַכֹּהֵן, בֵּין בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן, בֵּין בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי, בֵּין לְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר כְּגוֹן שֶׁעָמַד בְּעֵינָיו שְׁנֵי שָׁבוּעוֹת וּפְטָרוֹ וְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר בָּא שֵׂעָר לָבָן וְהֶחְלִיטוֹ, בְּכָל הָנֵי גַּוְנֵי אִם הָלַךְ לוֹ שֵׂעָר לָבָן וְחָזַר שֵׂעָר לָבָן, אוֹ מִחְיָה אוֹ פִשְׂיוֹן, הֲרֵי הִיא כְּמוֹת שֶׁהָיְתָה. כְּלוֹמַר נִשְׁאַר בְּטֻמְאָה כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה, הוֹאִיל וְיֵשׁ בּוֹ סִימַן טֻמְאָה, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין זֶה אוֹתוֹ סִימָן שֶׁהָיָה בִשְׁעַת הֶחְלֵט:


בתחילה בסוף שבוע ראשון – that is to say, whether the declaration of being a certified leper had been at the outset when he was brought to the Kohen, whether at the end of the first week, whether at the end of the second week, whether after the clearance/release, as for example, that it remained unchanged/עמד בעיניו for two weeks and he released him, and after the release, the white hair came and he (i.e., the Kohen) declared him to be a certified leper, in all of these ways if the white hair disappeared and then the white hair returned, or the quick flesh or the spreading. הרי היא כמות שהיתה – that is to say, the defilement remained like it was, for since he has a sign of defilement, even though this is not the same sign that he had at the time of the declaration as a certified leper.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:2:5)


**וְחָזַר הַפִּשְׂיוֹן וְכֵן בְּשֵׂעָר לָבָן.** הָא דְלָא קָתָנֵי הָכָא וְכֵן בְּמִחְיָה, לְפִי שֶׁכְּשֶׁהָלַךְ הַפִּשְׂיוֹן וּבָאת הַמִּחְיָה, פְּעָמִים שֶׁיִּהְיֶה טָהוֹר, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהַמִּחְיָה מִעֲטָה הַנֶּגַע מִכַּגְּרִיס:


וחזר הפשיון וכן בשער לבן – but that it (i.e., the Mishnah) doesn’t teach, "וכן המחיה"/and similarly the quick flesh, because when the spreading disappeared and the quick flesh came, sometimes he would be pure, as for example, that the quick flesh diminished the plague from being the size of a split Cilician bean.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:3:1)


**שְׂעַר פְּקֻדָּה.** לְשׁוֹן פִּקָּדוֹן, שֶׁהִפְקִידָה הַבַּהֶרֶת אֶת הַשֵּׂעָר בְּעוֹר הַבָּשָׂר וְהָלְכָה לָהּ:


שער פקודה (hair of a leprous spot which remains after the inflammation has partly receded) – it is language of פקדון/deposit (see Leviticus 5:21 and 5:23), that the bright white spot in the flesh/בהרת deposited the hair in the skin of the flesh and left it/disappeared.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:3:2)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִים.** וְטַעֲמַיְהוּ, דִּכְתִיב וְהִיא הָפְכָה, שֶׁהֲפָכַתּוּ הִיא וְלֹא הֲפָכַתּוּ חֲבֶרְתָּהּ:


וחכמים מטהרים – and their reasoning, as it is written (Leviticus 13:10): “[If the priest finds on the skin a white swelling] which has turned some hair white, [with a patch of un-discolored flesh in the swelling].”/"והיא הפכה [שער לבן ומחית בשר חי בשאת]., that it has turned [some hair white], but it did not turn its neighboring [hair white].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:3:3)


**אַף דְּבָרֶיךָ אֵין מְקֻיָּמִין.** דְּהַאי נַמִּי טָהוֹר. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


אף דבריך אין מקויימין – for this also is pure. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:4:1)


**בֵּין בְּאִישׁ אֶחָד.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּמַה נַּפְשָׁךְ יֵשׁ בּוֹ בַהֶרֶת אַחַת שֶׁפָּשְׂתָה, אוֹתָהּ שֶׁתְּחִלָּתָהּ כַּגְּרִיס וְעַכְשָׁיו כַּסֶּלַע, וְהָיָה רָאוּי לְהַחְלִיטוֹ, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי הוֹאִיל וְאֵינוֹ יוֹדֵעַ עַל אֵיזֶה נֶגַע מַחְלִיט, טָהוֹר מִגְּזֵרַת הַכָּתוּב דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) וְאִם פָּשֹׂה תִפְשֶׂה בָּעוֹר וְטִמֵּא הַכֹּהֵן אֹתוֹ, אֶת הַנֶּגַע הַוַּדַּאי הוּא מְטַמֵּא, וְאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא אֶת הַנֶּגַע שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ סָפֵק:


בין אישר אחד – even though that which ever you turn, he has one bright white spot in the flesh that has spread, that which at its outset was like a split Cilician bean and now is like a Sela, he is fit to be declared a certified leper, nevertheless, since he (i.e., the Kohen) doesn’t know on which plague is declaring him to be a certified leper, he (i.e., the person) is pure, from the decree of the Scriptural verse, as it is written (Leviticus 13:22): “If it should spread in the skin, the priest shall pronounce him impure; [it is an affection],” the definitive plague he declares impure, but he doesn’t declare impure but he doesn’t defile the plague where he has doubt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:4:2)


**בֵּין בִּשְׁנֵי אֲנָשִׁים.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֶחָד מֵהֶם טָעוּן הֶסְגֵּר שִׁבְעַת יָמִים שֵׁנִית בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן, וְהָאַחֵר מֻחְלָט, הוֹאִיל וְאֵינוֹ יוֹדֵעַ אֵיזֶה לְהַסְגִּיר וְאֵיזֶה לְהַחְלִיט, שְׁנֵיהֶם טְהוֹרִים מִגְּזֵרַת הַכָּתוּב:


בין בשני אנשים – even though that one of them requires being shut up/isolated for seven days a second time at the end of the first week, and the other is a certified leper, but sine he doesn’t know which to shut up and which to declare a certified leper, both of them are pure from the decree from the Scriptural verse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:4:3)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


ר' עקיבא אומר כו' – but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Akiba.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:5:1)


**שְׁנֵיהֶם טְמֵאִין.** דְּבִשְׁנֵיהֶם פָּשָׂה:


שניהם טמאים – since it spread in both of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:5:2)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁחָזְרוּ לִהְיוֹת כַּסֶּלַע.** דְּהַשְׁתָּא אֶחָד מֵהֶם טָהוֹר, הוֹאִיל וְהֻזְקְקוּ לַטֻּמְאָה, שְׁנֵיהֶם טְמֵאִים. דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְטִהֲרוֹ הַכֹּהֵן, אֶת הַוַּדַּאי הוּא מְטַהֵר, וְאֵינוֹ מְטַהֵר אֶת הַסָּפֵק:


אף על פי שחזרו להיות כסלע – for now one of them is ritually pure, but since they were obliged to regard themselves/obligate themselves for defilement, both of them are impure, as it is written (Leviticus 13:23): “[But if the discoloration remains stationary, not having spread, it is the scar of the inflammation] the priest shall declare him pure”/"וטהרו הכהן" - that which is definitive, he (i.e., the Kohen) declares pure, but he does not declare pure the doubtful (see also Sifra on his verse in Leviticus).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/5:5:3)


**עַד שֶׁיַּחְזְרוּ לִהְיוֹת כַּגְּרִיס.** דְּהַשְׁתָּא וַדַּאי שְׁנֵיהֶם טְהוֹרִים, שֶׁהַפִּשְׂיוֹן שֶׁבַּעֲבוּרוֹ הֻחְלַט הָלַךְ מִשְּׁנֵיהֶם:


עד שיחזרו להיות גריס – that now, certainly both of them are ritually pure, that the spreading that on whose account they were declared to be certified lepers has disappeared from both of them.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:1:1)


**גּוּפָהּ שֶׁל בַּהֶרֶת.** כַּגְּרִיס. חֲצִי פוֹל:


גופה של בהרת – a split bean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:1:2)


**הַקִּלְקִי.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם. וּפוֹלִין שֶׁבּוֹ גְּדוֹלִים:


הקלקי (Cilician) – the name of a place. And he beans that are there are large.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:1:3)


**מְרֻבָּע.** וְזֶהוּ שִׁעוּר מְצֻמְצָם שֶׁבְּבַהֶרֶת. וְאִם הָיָה הַנֶּגַע עָגֹל אוֹ עַל צוּרָה אַחֶרֶת, אִם יֵשׁ בּוֹ כְדֵי שֶׁיִּכָּנֵס בְּתוֹךְ הַנֶּגַע כַּגְּרִיס הַקִּלְקִי מְרֻבָּע, טָמֵא, וְאִם לָאו, טָהוֹר:


מרובע – this is the measure that is defined exactly that is in the bright white spot in the skin. But if the plague was round or in another form, if it has within it in order that the plague like the split Cilician squared bean can enter, it is impure, and if it not, it is pure.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:2:1)


**רָבְתָה הַבַּהֶרֶת.** מִבַּחוּץ:


רבתה הבהרת – from the outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:2:2)


**טְמֵאָה.** מִשּׁוּם פִּשְׂיוֹן:


טמאה – because of spreading.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:2:3)


**נִתְמַעֲטָה טְהוֹרָה.** דְּלֵיכָּא כַגְּרִיס:


נתמעטה טהורה – for it lacks like [the size of] a split bean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:2:4)


**רָבְתָה הַמִּחְיָה.** וְכָנְסָה הַבַּהֶרֶת:


רבתה הבהרת – and the bright white spot in the flesh/בהרת entered in.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:2:5)


**טְהוֹרָה.** דְּאֵין הַמִּחְיָה מְבֻצֶּרֶת, דְּבָעִינַן בַּהֶרֶת כָּעֲדָשָׁה מִכָּל צַד סָבִיב לַמִּחְיָה:


טהורה – for the quick flesh does not diminish. For we require that the bright white spot in the flesh is like a lentil on each side surrounding the quick flesh.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:3:1)


**רָבְתָה הַבַּהֶרֶת.** מִבַּחוּץ:


רבתה הבהרת – from the outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:3:2)


**טְמֵאָה.** שֶׁהֲרֵי פָשְׂתָה בָעוֹר:


טמאה – for it (i.e., the bright white spot in the flesh) spread in the flesh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:3:3)


**נִתְמַעֲטָה טְהוֹרָה.** וַאֲפִלּוּ הָיְתָה הַמִּחְיָה כָּעֲדָשָׁה:


נתמעטה טהורה – and even if the quick flesh was like a lentil [in size].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:3:4)


**רָבְתָה הַמִּחְיָה.** וְנַעֲשֵׂית כָּעֲדָשָׁה, טְמֵאָה:


רבתה המחיה – and it became like a lentil, it is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:3:5)


**נִתְמַעֲטָה.** שֶׁפָּשְׂתָה הַבַּהֶרֶת עָלֶיהָ:


מתמעטה – that the bright white spot in the flesh spread upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:3:6)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר מְטַמֵּא.** דַּחֲשִׁיב לֵיהּ פִּשְׂיוֹן מִבִּפְנִים כְּמוֹ מִבַּחוּץ:


ר' מאיר מטמא – for he considers spreading from the inside like from the outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:3:7)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִים.** דְּפִשְׂיוֹן שֶׁבִּפְנִים לָא חֲשִׁיב פִּשְׂיוֹן. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מטהרים – for spreading that is on the inside is not considered spreading. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:4:1)


**שֶׁלֹּא יִתְמַעֲטוּ מִכַּשִּׁעוּר.** בַּהֶרֶת מִכַּגְּרִיס, וּמִחְיָה מִכָּעֲדָשָׁה:


שלא יתמעטו מכשיעור – a bright white spot on the skin/בהרת from like a split Cilician bean [in size] and quick flesh/מחיה from like a lentil.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:1)


**בַּהֶרֶת כַּגְּרִיס.** מְרֻבָּע וּרְצוּעָה שֶׁל בָּשָׂר חַי בְּרֹחַב כָּעֲדָשָׁה מַקֶּפֶת אוֹתָהּ מִסָּבִיב, וּלְחוּץ לְאוֹתוֹ בָשָׂר חַי מַקֶּפֶת בַּהֶרֶת כַּגְּרִיס מִסָּבִיב, וַהֲרֵי יֵשׁ כָּאן שְׁתֵּי בֶהָרוֹת, אַחַת פְּנִימִית וְאַחַת חִיצוֹנָה, וּמִחְיָה מַפְסֶקֶת בֵּינֵיהֶן סָבִיב:


בהרת כגריס – squared and a strip of living flesh in the width of like a lentil surrounding it from around, and except for that living flesh that surrounds the bright white spot on the skin like a split Cilician bean from around. For here there are two bright white spots on the skin, one inner and one outer, and the quick flesh interrupts between them all around.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:2)


**הַפְּנִימִית לְהַסְגִּיר.** דְּאֵין לָהּ מִחְיָה בְּאֶמְצָעָהּ:


הפנימית להסגיר – for there is no quick flesh in its middle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:3)


**וְהַחִיצוֹנָה לְהַחְלִיט.** דְּמִחְיָה הַמַּקֶּפֶת לַפְּנִימִית סִימַן טֻמְאָה לַחִיצוֹנָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי הִיא מְבֻצֶּרֶת בְּאֶמְצָעָהּ:


והחיצונה להחליט – for the quick flesh that surrounds the inner [bright white spot on the skin] is a sign/token of defilement for the outer [bright white spot on the skin] for it is surrounded in its middle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:4)


**שֶׁהַבַּהֶרֶת בְּתוֹכָהּ.** לְפִי שֶׁהַבַּהֶרֶת לְתוֹכָהּ אֵינָהּ חֲשׁוּבָה סִימַן טֻמְאָה לַחִיצוֹנָה, דְּבָעִינַן מְבֻצֶּרֶת, נֶגַע מִכָּאן וְנֶגַע מִכָּאן וּמִחְיָה בָאֶמְצַע, [בְּלִי הֶפְסֵק דָּבָר אַחֵר בְּתוֹךְ הַמִּחְיָה], וְהָכָא יֵשׁ כָּאן נֶגַע וּמִחְיָה וְנֶגַע וּמִחְיָה וְנֶגַע. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


שהבהרת בתוכה – because the bright white spot on the skin is within it, it is not considered a sign/token of defilement to the outer [bright white spot on the skin], since we require it being surrounded, a plague from here and a plague from there with quick flesh in the middle [without an interruption of something else within the quick flesh), but here, there is a plague and quick flesh and a plague and quick flesh and a plague. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:5)


**נִתְמַעֲטָה הַמִּחְיָה.** שֶׁפָּשְׂתָה הַבַּהֶרֶת עָלֶיהָ:


נתמעטה המחיה – that the bright white spot on the skin spread upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:6)


**וְהָלְכָה לָהּ.** אוֹ שֶׁהָלְכָה לָהּ כֻּלָּהּ:


והלכה לה – or it went away/disappeared completely.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:7)


**אִם מִבִּפְנִים הִיא כָלְתָה.** שֶׁבַּהֶרֶת הַפְּנִימִית כִּסְּתָה הַמִּחְיָה:


אם מבפנים היא כלתה – that the bright white spot on the skin covered the quick flesh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:8)


**סִימַן פִּשְׂיוֹן לַפְּנִימִית.** וּמַחְלִיטָהּ, שֶׁהֲרֵי פָשְׂתָה מִבַּחוּץ:


סימן פשיון לפנימית – and it is declared definitive leprosy, for it spread from the outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:9)


**וְהַחִיצוֹנָה טְהוֹרָה.** שֶׁהָלְכָה מִחְיָתָהּ אוֹ נִתְמַעֲטָה מִכָּעֲדָשָׁה:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:10)


**אִם מִבַּחוּץ.** שֶׁהַבַּהֶרֶת הַחִיצוֹנָה כִּסְּתָה הַמִּחְיָה:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:11)


**הַחִיצוֹנָה טְהוֹרָה.** שֶׁנִּתְמַעֲטָה מִחְיָתָהּ אוֹ הָלְכָה, וּפִשְׂיוֹן לֵיכָּא, שֶׁאֵין הַנֶּגַע פּוֹשֶׂה לְתוֹכָהּ:


והחיצונה טהורה – that its quick flesh diminished or went away, and there is no spreading for the plague does not spread within it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:12)


**וְהַפְּנִימִית לְהַסְגִּיר.** שֶׁהַנֶּגַע עָמַד בְּעֵינָיו:


והפנימית להסגיר – for the plague remained unchanged.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:13)


**בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ.** בֵּין כָּלְתָה מִבִּפְנִים בֵּין כָּלְתָה מִבַּחוּץ:


בין כך ובין כך – whether it ceased/went away from the inside or whether it ceased/disappeared from the outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:5:14)


**טְהוֹרָה.** שֶׁכְּשֵׁם שֶׁאִם פָּשְׂתָה הַחִיצוֹנָה לַמִּחְיָה שֶׁבְּתוֹכָהּ אֵינוֹ פִשְׂיוֹן, כָּךְ כְּשֶׁפָּשְׂתָה הַפְּנִימִית לַמִּחְיָה שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הַבַּהֶרֶת הַחִיצוֹנָה אֵינוֹ פִשְׂיוֹן. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


טהור – that just as if it the outer part [of the bright white spot on the skin] had spread to the quick flesh that is within it, this is not spreading, so when the inner [bright white spot on the skin] spreads to the quick flesh that is within the outer bright white spot on the skin, it is not spreading. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiba.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:6:1)


**אֵימָתַי.** הַחִיצוֹנָה טְהוֹרָה כְּשֶׁפָּשְׂתָה הַפְּנִימִית אוֹ הַחִיצוֹנָה עַל מִחְיָה, דַּאֲמַרַן לְעֵיל שֶׁהַחִיצוֹנָה טְהוֹרָה, הָנֵי מִלֵּי בִּזְמַן שֶׁהִיא כָעֲדָשָׁה מוּבֵאת, שֶׁהַמִּחְיָה כָעֲדָשָׁה מְצֻמְצֶמֶת לֹא פָחוֹת וְלֹא יוֹתֵר. אֲבָל אִם הָיְתָה הַמִּחְיָה יְתֵרָה מִכָּעֲדָשָׁה:


אימתי – the outer [bright white spot on the skin] is pure when the inner [bright white spot on the skin] or the outer [bright white spot on the skin] has spread on the quick flesh, for we stated above that the outer [bright white spot on the skin] is pure (see the previous Mishnah), these words apply when it is exactly the size of a lentil, that the quick flesh is like an exactly defined lentil, not less and not more. But if the quick flesh is greater [in size] than that of a lentil, [the excess is a token/sign of spreading for the inner one (i.e., the bright white spot on the skin) and the outer one (i.e., the bright white spot on the skin) is unclean].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:6:2)


**הַמּוֹתָר סִימַן פִּשְׂיוֹן לַפְּנִימִית, וְהַחִיצוֹנָה טְמֵאָה.** כְּלוֹמַר, אִם פָּשְׂתָה הַפְּנִימִית עַל אוֹתוֹ מוֹתָר, שְׁתֵּיהֶן לְהַחְלִיט, פְּנִימִית מִשּׁוּם פִּשְׂיוֹן וְחִיצוֹנָה מִשּׁוּם מִחְיָה. וְאִם פָּשְׂתָה הַחִיצוֹנָה עַל אוֹתוֹ מוֹתָר, מִכָּל מָקוֹם הַחִיצוֹנָה לְהַחְלִיט מִשּׁוּם מִחְיָה, וְהַפְּנִימִית לְהַסְגִּיר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


המותר סימן פסיון לפנימית והחיצונה טמאה – that is to say, if the inner [bright white spot on the skin] spread over that excess, both of them are to be declared to be definitive leprosy. The inner one because of the spreading and the outer one because of the quick flesh. But if the outer one (i.e., the bright white spot on the skin) spread over that excess, nevertheless, the outer one is to be declared definitive leprosy because of the quick flesh and the inner one is to [cause] to be shut up/isolated. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:6:3)


**הָיָה בֹהַק פָּחוֹת מִכָּעֲדָשָׁה.** סָמוּךְ לַפְּנִימִית, וְהַחִיצוֹנָה מַקֶּפֶת לַבֹּהַק:


היה בוהק פחות מכעדשה (if the white scurf/tetter was less than a lentil) – near the inner [bright white spot on the skin], and the outer [bright white spot on the skin] surrounds the white scurf/tetter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:6:4)


**סִימַן פִּשְׂיוֹן לַפְּנִימִית.** אִם פָּשְׂתָה הַפְּנִימִית וְכִסְּתָה אֶת הַבֹּהַק, הַפְּנִימִית לְהַחְלִיט, שֶׁהֲרֵי פָשְׂתָה מִבַּחוּץ. וְאִם הַחִיצוֹנָה פָשְׂתָה וְכִסְּתָה אֶת הַבֹּהַק, אֵינוֹ סִימַן פִּשְׂיוֹן לַחִיצוֹנָה, שֶׁאֵין הַנֶּגַע פּוֹשֶׂה לְתוֹכוֹ. וְהַאי דְּנָקַט בֹּהַק פָּחוֹת מִכָּעֲדָשָׁה, דְּאִם הָיָה כָעֲדָשָׁה הָיָה מְטַמֵּא מִשּׁוּם מִחְיָה, דְּקַיְמָא לָן מִחְיָה מְטַמְּאָה בְּכָל הַמַּרְאוֹת. אֲבָל פָּחוֹת מִכָּעֲדָשָׁה אֵין כָּאן מִחְיָה, דְּאֵין מִחְיָה פְחוּתָה מִכָּעֲדָשָׁה:


סימן פשיון לפנימית – if the inner [bright white spot on the skin] spread and covered the white scurf/tetter, the inner [bright white spot on the skin] is to be declared a definitive leprosy , for it has spread from the outside. But if the outer [bright white spot on the skin] spread and covered the white scurf/tetter, it is not a sign/token of spreading for the outer [bright white spot on the skin] for the plague does not spread within. And that it (i.e., the Mishnah) used the phrase: "היה בוהק פחות מכעדשה"/it was a white scurf/tetter less than a lentil -, for if it was like a lentil, it would defile because of the quick flesh for we hold that quick flesh defiles in all of the appearances [of plagues], but less than a lentil, there is no quick flesh, for there is no quick flesh that is less than a lentil.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:7:1)


**שֶׁאֵינָן מִטַּמְּאִין מִשּׁוּם מִחְיָה.** דִּכְתִיב וְרָאָהוּ הַכֹּהֵן, בָּעִינַן שֶׁיִּרְאֶה אֶת הַנֶּגַע עִם הַמִּחְיָה כֻּלּוֹ כְאֶחָד, וּכְשֶׁיֵּשׁ כַּגְּרִיס בְּרֹאשׁ הַחֹטֶם אוֹ בְרָאשֵׁי אֶצְבָּעוֹת הוּא שׁוֹפֵעַ אֵילָךְ וָאֵילָךְ וְאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִרְאוֹת כֻּלּוֹ כְאֶחָד:


שאינן מיטמאין משום מחיה – as it is written (Leviticus 13:5): “[On the seventh day] the priest shall examine him”/וראהו הכהן [ביום השביעי], we require that he should examine the plague with all of the quick flesh as one, and when there is like a [Cilician] split bean at the tip of the nose or at the tip of the joints [of the hands and feet] it inclines/slants this way and that way and he is not able to examine it all as one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:7:2)


**רָאשֵׁי אָזְנַיִם.** נֶחְשָׁבִים כְּאֶחָד. וְכֵן רָאשֵׁי דַדִּין. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה שֶׁמָּנָה רָאשֵׁי דַדִּין שֶׁבָּאִישׁ:


ראשי אזנים – [the tips of the ears] are considered as one. And similarly, the tips of the breasts. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda who counted the tips of the breast that are on a man.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:7:3)


**וְרֹאשׁ הַגְּוִיָּה.** הוּא גִּיד הָאַמָּה:


וראש הגויה – which is the membrum virile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:7:4)


**וְיַבָּלוֹת.** לְשׁוֹן [אוֹ חָרוּץ אוֹ יַבֶּלֶת] (ויקרא כב). פורו"ש בְּלַעַ"ז:


יבלות (warts on the skin) – it is the language of (Leviticus 22:22): ["או חרוץ או יבלת"]/ “or maimed or with a wen” PURUSH in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:7:5)


**וְדִלְדּוּלִין.** בָּשָׂר שֶׁפֵּרֵשׁ וְנִדַּלְדֵּל. וְאִית דְּגָרְסֵי וְתִלְתּוּלִין, בָּשָׂר שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה כְמוֹ תֵל גָּבוֹהַּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


ודלדולין (a wart with a thin neck) – flesh that separated and was torn loose/detached. But there are those who have the reading of "ותלתולין"/warts, flesh that became like a high mound. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:1)


**שֶׁאֵינָן מִטַּמְּאִין בְּבַהֶרֶת.** בְּאַרְבָּעָה מַרְאוֹת נְגָעִים, שְׂאֵת וְתוֹלַדְתָּהּ בַּהֶרֶת וְתוֹלַדְתָּהּ:


שאינן מיטמאין בבהרת – in the four appearances of plagues, שאת/swelling and its subcategories/derivatives, בהרת/the bright white spot on the skin and its subcategories/derivatives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:2)


**תּוֹךְ הָעַיִן תּוֹךְ הָאֹזֶן.** שֶׁכָּל אֵלּוּ אֵינָן חֲשׁוּבִים כְּעוֹר בָּשָׂר, וְהַכָּתוּב אוֹמֵר (שם יג) וְהָיָה בְעוֹר בְּשָׂרוֹ:


תוך העין תוך האוזן – that all of these are not considered like the skin of the body, as Scripture states (Leviticus 13:2): "והיה בעור-בשרו"/ “on the skin of his body.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:3)


**הַקְּמָטִין.** שֶׁבִּשְׁאָר הַגּוּף:


הקמטין (the wrinkles) – that are in the rest of the body.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:4)


**וְהַקְּמָטִין שֶׁבַּצַּוָּאר.** תָּנָא סֵיפָא, לְגַלּוֹיֵי דִּקְמָטִין דְּרֵישָׁא קְמָטִין שֶׁל שְׁאָר הַגּוּף הֵן. דְּלֹא תֵימָא קְמָטִין דְּצַוָּאר דַּוְקָא דְּלֹא מִתְחֲזֵי כְאַחֲרִינֵי:


והקמטין שבצואר (the wrinkles/folds that are on the neck) – the Mishnah teaches the concluding clause to reveal that the wrinkles/folds of the first clause are the folds/wrinkles of the rest of the body , that doesn’t state the wrinkles of the neck especially, that don’t appear as other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:5)


**תַּחַת הַדַּד.** כְּשֶׁנִּרְאֵית כְּמֵנִיקָה אֶת בְּנָהּ, מַה שֶּׁאֵינוֹ נִרְאֶה בְאוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה אֵינוֹ מִטַּמֵּא:


תחת הדד – when it appears like she is nursing her child, what it doesn’t appear at that time does not defile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:6)


**וּבֵית הַשֶּׁחִי.** כְּשֶׁנִּרְאֶה כְמוֹסֵק זֵיתִים:


ובית השחי (the arm-pit) – when it appears like he is harvesting olives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:7)


**כַּף הָרֶגֶל.** מַה שֶּׁתַּחַת [הַקַּעֲרוּרִית] שֶׁל הָרֶגֶל. דְּכָל אֵלּוּ אֵינָן עוֹר בָּשָׂר הַנִּרְאֶה:


כף הרגל (the sole of the foot) – what is underneath [the bottom] of the foot. And all of these are not the skin of the body that appears.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:8)


**וְהַצִּפֹּרֶן.** לָא חֲשִׁיבָא עוֹר בָּשָׂר:


והצפורן – it is not considered the skin of the body.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:9)


**הָרֹאשׁ וְהַזָּקָן.** אֵינָן מִטַּמְּאִין בְּבַהֶרֶת, דִּכְתִיב (שם) נֶתֶק הוּא צָרַעַת הָרֹאשׁ אוֹ הַזָּקָן הוּא, אֵין לָרֹאשׁ וְלַזָּקָן טֻמְאָה אֶלָּא טֻמְאַת נְתָקִין בִּלְבַד:


הראש והזקן – are not defiled through the bright white spot on the skin/בהרת, as it is written (Leviticus 13:30): “it is a scall, a scaly eruption in the hair or beard,” the head nor the beard have defilement other than the defilement of the bald (blanched) spots on the head or in the beard/נתקין.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:10)


**הַשְּׁחִין וְהַמִּכְוָה וְהַקֶּדַח הַמּוֹרְדִין.** שֶׁלֹּא נִתְרַפְּאוּ יָפֶה וְלֹא נִקְרַם הָעוֹר שֶׁלָּהֶם כִּקְלִפַּת הַשּׁוּם:


השחין והמכוה והקדח המורדים (the boils and the burning and the blisters/inflammation that are festering – not covered by a scab – running wounds from contusions, burns or inflammation -Jastrow) – that had not healed properly, and a cover/crust did not form over their skin like the husk of garlic.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:11)


**אֵין מִטַּמְּאִין מִשּׁוּם נֶגַע.** דְּבִשְׁחִין כְּתִיב (שם) שְׁחִין וְנִרְפָּא, וּבְמִכְוָה נַמִּי כְתִיב (שם) וְהָיְתָה מִחְיַת הַמִּכְוָה:


אין מיטמאין משום נגע – that regarding boils it is written (Leviticus 13:18): “When an inflammation ]appears on the skin of one’s body[ and it heals”/["ובשר כי-יהיה בו-בערו] שחין ונרפא", and with regard to burning it is also written (Leviticus 13:24): “[When the skin of one’s body sustains a burn by fire,] and the patch from the burn is a discoloration.”/"[או בשר כי-יהיה בערו מכות-אש] והיתה מחית המכוה".


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:12)


**קֶדַח.** הוּא שְׁחִין שֶׁבָּא מֵחֲמַת דָּבָר אַחֵר, כְּגוֹן מֵחֲמַת מַכַּת אֶבֶן אוֹ עֵץ אוֹ גֶפֶת אוֹ סִיד, וּמִכְוָה נַמִּי שֶׁלֹּא בָאָה מֵחֲמַת הָאֵשׁ עַצְמוֹ אֶלָּא מֵחֲמַת תּוֹלְדוֹת הָאֵשׁ, כְּגוֹן רֶמֶץ חַם וְגִפְסִית חַם, כָּל אֵלּוּ קְרוּיִין קֶדַח. לְשׁוֹן כִּקְדֹחַ אֵשׁ הֲמָסִים. וְקֶדַח הַמּוֹרֵד נַמִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִתְרַפָּא וְלֹא הֶעֱלָה קְרוּם כִּקְלִפַּת הַשּׁוּם, אֵינוֹ מִטַּמֵּא מִשּׁוּם נֶגַע:


קדח (blister, inflammation) – it is a boil that comes on account of something else, as for example, on account of being struck b a stone or wood or peat or plaster/lime, and the burning also does not come on account of the fire itself, but on account of the derivatives/subcategories of fire, such as hot ashes/embers and hot plastering material, all of these are called blisters. It is the language of (Isaiah 64:1): “As when fire kindles brushwood/כקדח אש המסים”, and the running wounds not covered by a scab also, that did not heal, and did not raise up a scab like the husk of garlic, does not defile because of the plague.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:13)


**הַמּוֹרְדִים.** כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא נִתְרַפְּאוּ שֶׁלֹּא הֶעֱלוּ קְרוּם, קְרוּיִין מוֹרְדִים, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁעֲדַיִן עוֹמְדִים בְּמִרְדָּם. וְאִית דְּגָרְסֵי מוֹרְרִים בִּשְׁנֵי רֵישִׁי"ן, לְשׁוֹן רָר בְּשָׂרוֹ (שם טו):


המורדים – all the time that it didn’t heal that it didn’t raise up a scab is called, "מורדים"/wounds that are festering, meaning to say, that they still stand in their rebellion. But there are those who have the reading "מוררים"/bitter, afflicting with two [letters] "ר". It is the language of (Leviticus 15:3): “[The impurity from his discharge shall mean the following -] whether his member runs with the discharge”/"רר בשרו".


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:14)


**וְאֵין מִצְטָרְפִים בַּנְּגָעִים.** אִם הָיָה חֲצִי גְרִיס נֶגַע בְּעוֹר בְּשָׂרוֹ וַחֲצִי גְרִיס בְּאֶחָד מִן הַמְּקוֹמוֹת הַלָּלוּ, אֵין מִצְטָרְפִים לְכַגְּרִיס:


ואין מצטרפין בנגעים – there was a half split bean plague in the skin of his flesh or a half split bean plague in one of these places, they do not combine to make a [Cilician] bean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:15)


**וְאֵין הַנֶּגַע פּוֹשֶׂה לְתוֹכָן.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּלֹא מִטַּמְּאוּ בָאוֹם לֹא מִטַּמְּאוּ נַמִּי בַפִּשְׂיוֹן:


ואין הנגע פושה לתוכן (and the plague does not spread into them) – for since hey don’t become defiled at the starting point of leprosy, they don’t also become defiled through spreading.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:16)


**וְאֵינָם מִטַּמְּאִין מִשּׁוּם מִחְיָה.** דְּאִם פָּרַח בְּכֻלּוֹ וְיֵשׁ בְּרֹאשׁוֹ אוֹ בְכַף רַגְלוֹ אוֹ בַקְּמָטִין בָּשָׂר חַי, אֵין מִטַּמְּאִין מִשּׁוּם מִחְיָה. אִי נַמִּי, אִם הָיָה אֶחָד מִן הַמְּקוֹמוֹת הַלָּלוּ מְבֻצָּר בְּתוֹךְ הַנֶּגַע, אֵינוֹ מִטַּמֵּא מִשּׁוּם מִחְיָה:


ואינן מיטמאין משום מחיה – that if blossomed/spread throughout, and there is at his head or on the sole of his foot or in the folds of his living flesh, they don’t become defiled because of quick flesh. Alternatively, if one of these places was surrounded within the plague, it doesn’t become defiled through quick flesh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:17)


**וְאֵינָם מְעַכְּבִים אֶת הַהוֹפֵךְ כֻּלּוֹ לָבָן.** אִם פָּרְחָה הַצָּרַעַת בְּכֻלּוֹ חוּץ מֵאֶחָד מֵהַמְּקוֹמוֹת הַלָּלוּ, אֵין מְעַכְּבִין, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) וְכִסְּתָה הַצָּרַעַת אֵת כָּל עוֹר הַנֶּגַע, עוֹר הָרָאוּי לִהְיוֹת בּוֹ נֶגַע, פְּרָט לְאֵלּוּ שֶׁאֵין מִטַּמְּאִין מִשּׁוּם נֶגַע:


ואינם מעכבים את ההופך כולו לבן (and they don’t hinder [the cleanness of] the one who turns entirely white)- if the leprosy sprouted throughout all [of the body] except for one of these places, they don’t hinder [the cleanness] a, as it is written (Leviticus 13:12): “[If the eruption spreads out over the skin] so that it covers all the skin of the affected person [from head to foot]/"וכסתה הצרעת את כל-עור הנגע [מראשו ועד-רגליו]", excluding to those that do not defile because of the plague.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:18)


**חָזַר הָרֹאשׁ וְהַזָּקָן וְנִקְרְחוּ.** שֶׁנִּקְרַח הַשֵּׂעָר שֶׁהָיָה בָהֶן וְנַעֲשׂוּ כְעוֹר בָּשָׂר. וְכֵן הַשְּׁחִין וְהַמִּכְוָה וְהַקֶּדַח שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ צָרֶבֶת, שֶׁנִּתְרַפְּאוּ וְקָרַם עֲלֵיהֶם עוֹר כִּקְלִפַּת הַשּׁוּם:


חזר הראש והזקן ונקרחו (the head and the beard changed and became bald) – that the hair that on them became bald and became like the skin of the body. And similarly, the boil and the quick flesh and the blister/inflammation that became a scar, that healed and a scab of flesh formed on them like the husk of garlic.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:19)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ מִטַּמְּאִין בַּנְּגָעִים.** בְּאַרְבָּעָה מַרְאוֹת. וְהוּא הַדִּין דַּהֲוָה מָצֵי לְמִתְנֵי נִתְפַּשְּׁטוּ הַקְּמָטִין נַמִּי מִטַּמְּאִין בַּנְּגָעִים:


הרי אלו מיטמאין בנגעים – with the four appearances (see Tractate Negaim, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1 and following). And the same law applies that he is able to teach that the wrinkles spread, also become defiled through plagues.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:20)


**וְאֵין מִצְטָרְפִין בַּנְּגָעִים.** רֹאשׁ וְזָקָן אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין. וְכֵן עוֹר הַבָּשָׂר וְהַזָּקָן, אוֹ עוֹר הַבָּשָׂר וְהָרֹאשׁ, אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין. דְּאִם בָּא לוֹ חֲצִי גְרִיס נֶתֶק בָּרֹאשׁ, אִי נַמִּי חֲצִי גְרִיס נֶתֶק בַּזָּקָן, וַחֲצִי גְרִיס נֶגַע בְּעוֹר בָּשָׂר, אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין. וְכֵן חֲצִי גְרִיס שְׁחִין וַחֲצִי גְרִיס מִכְוָה, אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין לְכַגְּרִיס:


ואין מצטרפין בנגעים – head and beard do not combine. And similarly, the skin of the body and the beard, or the skin of the body and the head, do not combine. For if one-half of a split bean bald/blanched spots of the beard and half of a split bean in the skin of the body, do not combine. And similarly one-half of a split bean of boils and one-half of a split bean of the burns on the skin do not combine to become a full bean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:21)


**וְאֵין הַנֶּגַע פּוֹשֶׂה לְתוֹכָן.** נֶגַע שֶׁל עוֹר בָּשָׂר אֵין פּוֹשֶׂה לֹא בְנֶתֶק הָרֹאשׁ וְלֹא בְנֶתֶק הַזָּקָן וְלֹא בִשְׁחִין וְלֹא בְמִכְוָה, דְּלָא חֲשִׁיבֵי כְעוֹר בָּשָׂר:


ואין הנגע פושה לתוכן – a plague of the skin of the flesh does not spread neither in the bald/blanched spots of the head and not in the bald, blanched spots of the beard, nor in the boils nor in the burnt spots, for they are not considered like the skin of the body.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:22)


**וְאֵין מִטַּמְּאִין מִשּׁוּם מִחְיָה.** דִּנְתָקִים מִטַּמְּאִין בְּשֵׂעָר צָהֹב וּפִשְׂיוֹן, וּשְׁחִין וּמִכְוָה בְּשֵׂעָר לָבָן וּפִשְׂיוֹן:


ואין מיטמאין משום מחיה – that bald, blanched spots become defiled in yellow hair and spreading, and boils and burning in the white hair and spreading.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:23)


**אֲבָל מְעַכְּבִין אֶת הַהוֹפֵךְ כֻּלּוֹ לָבָן.** שֶׁאִם פָּרְחָה הַצָּרַעַת בְּכֻלּוֹ וְנִשְׁאַר הָרֹאשׁ אוֹ הַזָּקָן שֶׁנִּקְרְחוּ שֶׁלֹּא פָרְחָה הַצָּרַעַת בָּהֶן, אוֹ הַשְּׁחִין וְהַמִּכְוָה וְהַקֶּדַח שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ צָרֶבֶת, טָמֵא עַד שֶׁיִּפְרַח בְּכֻלּוֹ:


אבל מעכבין את ההופך כולו לבן (but they do hinder [the cleanness of] one who turns wholly white) – that if the leprosy sprouted in all of him but the head or the beard that were made bald remained that the leprosy did not sprout in them, or the boils and burning and the baldness that became scarred, is impure until it (i.e., the leprosy) blossoms throughout.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 6:8:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/6:8:24)


**הָרֹאשׁ וְהַזָּקָן שֶׁלֹּא הֶעֱלוּ שֵׂעָר.** כְּגוֹן קָטָן שֶׁלֹּא צָמַח זְקָנוֹ אוֹ לֹא הִשְׂעִיר רֹאשׁוֹ, נִדּוֹנִים כְּעוֹר בָּשָׂר לִטַּמֵּא בְּבַהֶרֶת. וְכֵן הַדִּלְדּוּלִין שֶׁבָּרֹאשׁ וּבַזָּקָן דְּלֵית בְּהוּ שֵׂעָר, נִדּוֹנִים כְּעוֹר בָּשָׂר, וּמְעַכְּבִים אֶת הַפְּרִיחָה, וְהַנֶּגַע פּוֹשֶׂה לְתוֹכָן, וּמִצְטָרְפִין לְכַגְּרִיס:


הראש והזקן שלא העלו שער – as for example a minor whose beard has not grown or the hair of his head, are judged like skin of the flesh to become defiled through a bright white spot in the skin. And similarly, the warts with a thin neck that are in the head or on the beard [of a person] that doesn’t have hair, are judged like the skin of the flesh, and they hinder the blossoming, and the plague spreads within them and combines to make a split [Cilician] bean.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:1:1)


**אֵלּוּ בֶהָרוֹת טְהוֹרוֹת.** שֶׁהָיוּ בוֹ קֹדֶם מַתַּן תּוֹרָה. מִי שֶׁהָיְתָה בּוֹ צָרַעַת קֹדֶם מַתַּן תּוֹרָה, לֹא הָיָה טָמֵא מֵחֲמַת אוֹתוֹ נֶגַע לְאַחַר מַתַּן תּוֹרָה, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) אָדָם כִּי יִהְיֶה בְעוֹר בְּשָׂרוֹ, פְּרָט לָזֶה שֶׁכְּבָר הָיָה. וְכָל הָנֵי נַמִּי דַּחֲשִׁיב, מֵהָתָם נָפְקֵי, דְּבָעִינַן שֶׁיִּוָּלֵד הַנֶּגַע בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁרָאוּי לִטַּמֵּא בוֹ וְלֹא קֹדֶם, כְּמוֹ גֵּר עַד שֶׁלֹּא נִתְגַּיֵּר וְקָטָן עַד שֶׁלֹּא נוֹלַד, וְכֵן כֻּלָּם:
