אלו בהרות טהורות שהיו בו קודם מתן תורה – whomever had on him leprosy prior to the Giving of the Torah, was not impure on account of that plague after the Giving of the Torah, as it is written (Leviticus 13:2): “When a person has on the skin of his body,” excluding what already was. And all these were also considered, from there they are derived, that we require that the plague would come to be at the time that it is appropriate to be defiled by it and not beforehand, like a convert until he had not converted and a minor until he had not been born. And similarly, all of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:1:2)


**בְּקֶמֶט וְנִתְגַּלָּה.** שֶׁהָיָה מִתְּחִלָּה הַנֶּגַע מֻסְתָּר בְּתוֹךְ הַקֶּמֶט וְהָיָה טָהוֹר מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא בְבֵית הַסְּתָרִים, וְאַחַר כָּךְ נִתְגַּלָּה:


בקמט ונתגלה – that it was from the outset concealed within the wrinkle/fold and he was ritually pure, because it was in a covered part of the body/posterior, but afterwards, it was revealed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:1:3)


**וּבַמּוֹרְדִים.** כְּלוֹמַר בִּשְׁחִין וּמִכְוָה הַמּוֹרְדִים שֶׁלֹּא נִתְרַפְּאוּ וְלֹא הֶעֱלוּ קְרוּם כִּקְלִפַּת הַשּׁוּם:


ובמורדים – meaning to say with boils and burns, the scars that did not deal and did not produce a scab like the husk of the garlic.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:1:4)


**עַד שֶׁלֹּא הֶעֱלוּ שֵׂעָר.** בָּא הַנֶּגַע, דַּחֲשִׁיבֵי כְּעוֹר בָּשָׂר, הַיְנוּ תְּחִלָּתָן טֻמְאָה. וּכְשֶׁעָלוּ שְׂעָרוֹת, הָוֵי טָהֳרָה, שֶׁדִּינָן בִּנְתָקִים. וּכְשֶׁנִּקְרְחוּ, הַיְנוּ סוֹפָן טֻמְאָה. וְכֵן שְׁחִין וּמִכְוָה וְקֶדַח דְּעַד שֶׁלֹּא בָאוּ בָּא הַנֶּגַע, הַיְנוּ טֻמְאָה. וּכְשֶׁבָּא הַשְּׁחִין וְהַמִּכְוָה וְהַקֶּדַח, הַיְנוּ טָהֳרָה. דְּבָעִינַן שֶׁיַּקְדִּים הַשְּׁחִין וְהַמִּכְוָה לַשְּׂאֵת, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) וְהָיָה בִּמְקוֹם הַשְּׁחִין שְׂאֵת, שֶׁיַּקְדִּים הַשְּׁחִין לַשְּׂאֵת וְלֹא שֶׁתַּקְדִּים הַשְּׂאֵת לַשְּׁחִין. כְּשֶׁחָזְרוּ וְנַעֲשׂוּ צָרֶבֶת, הַיְנוּ טֻמְאָה:


עד שלא העלו שער – the plague comes, that it is considered like the skin of the flesh, that is the beginning of defilement. But when the hairs arose, it was pure, for its law is like bald or blanched spots on the head or the beard, but when they became bald, that is, their end is defilement. And similarly, boils, and burnt spots on the skin and baldness, that until they did not come, the plague came, that is defilement. But when the boils, burnt spots on the skin and the baldness came, that is purity. For we require that the boils and the burnt spots on the skin come prior to the swelling/sore, as it is written (Leviticus 13:19): “And a white swelling or a white discoloration streaked with red develops where the inflammation was,”/"והיה במקום השחין שאת" , that the boils precede the swelling/sore and not that the swelling/sore precedes the boils. And when they returned and became a scar, that is defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:1:5)


**וְחָיוּ.** שֶׁנִּתְרַפְּאוּ יָפֶה:


והיו – that they healed properly/nicely.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:1:6)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בֶּן יַעֲקֹב מְטַמֵּא.** כֵּיוָן דְּבַתְּחִלָּה בָּא הַנֶּגַע בְּעוֹר הַבָּשָׂר וְחָזַר סוֹפוֹ לְקַדְמוּתוֹ, לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן לְהֶפְסֵק שֶׁבֵּינְתַיִם:


רבי אליעזר בן יעקב מטמא – since at the outset the plague came in the skin of the flesh, and it returned at its end to its initial status, we are not concerned about the interruption that is between them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:1:7)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִים.** הוֹאִיל וְהִפְסִיק הַטָּהֳרָה שֶׁבֵּינְתַיִם. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מטהרים – since the purity stopped in the meantime. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:2:1)


**נִשְׁתַּנּוּ מַרְאֵיהֶן.** אַבֶּהָרוֹת טְהוֹרוֹת קָאֵי. כְּגוֹן דַּהֲוָת כַּשֶּׁלֶג עַד שֶׁלֹּא נִתְגַּיֵּר, וּמִשֶּׁנִּתְגַּיֵּר נַעֲשֵׂית כְּסִיד הַהֵיכָל:


נשתנו מראיהן – it refers to the bright white spots on the skin that are pure, as for example, that it was like snow until he (i.e., a non-Jew) converted, but when he converted [to Judaism] it became like the lime in the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:2:2)


**כְּצֶמֶר לָבָן וְכִקְרוּם בֵּיצָה.** כְּלוֹמַר, תְּחִלָּה הָיְתָה כְצֶמֶר לָבָן וְעַכְשָׁיו נַעֲשֵׂית כִּקְרוּם בֵּיצָה:


כצמר לבן וכקרום ביצה – meaning to say, that at first it was like white wool, and now it became like the membrane/skin of an egg.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:2:3)


**נַעֲשֵׂית מִסְפַּחַת שְׂאֵת אוֹ מִסְפַּחַת עַזָּה.** כְּלוֹמַר, שֶׁהַשְּׂאֵת נַעֲשֵׂית מִסְפַּחַת, שֶׁחָזְרָה לִהְיוֹת כַּתּוֹלָדָה שֶׁלָּהּ. אוֹ שֶׁהָעַזָּה נַעֲשֵׂית כַּמִּסְפַּחַת שֶׁלָּהּ. וּפָרוֹשֵׁי קָמְפָרֵשׁ לְרֵישָׁא דְּקָתָנֵי הָיְתָה כַשֶּׁלֶג וְנַעֲשֵׂית כְּסִיד הַהֵיכָל, הַיְנוּ שֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂית עַזָּה מִסְפַּחַת. וְאוֹתָהּ שֶׁהָיְתָה כְצֶמֶר לָבָן וְנַעֲשֵׂית כִּקְרוּם בֵּיצָה, הַיְנוּ שֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂית שְׂאֵת מִסְפַּחַת:


נעשית מספחת שאת (it became the scurf/second shade of a swelling/sore) – meaning to say that the swelling/sore became scurf, that it returned to become like its derivative/subcategory. Or that the strong/bright [white] became like its scurf. But he is really explaining to the first clause (i.e., presenting a new interpretation of the Mishnah in order to refute an argument based upon that text), as it is taught [in the Mishnah]: "היתה כשלג ונעשית כסיד ההיכל"/it was as white as snow and it became as white as the lime in the Temple, that is that it became stronger/brighter than a rising on the skin. And it that was like white wool but became like the membrane/skin of an egg, that it is that the swelling/sore became a rising on the skin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:2:4)


**וּכְסִיד הַהֵיכָל וּכְשֶׁלֶג.** כְּלוֹמַר הָיְתָה כְסִיד הַהֵיכָל וְעַכְשָׁיו נַעֲשֵׂית כַּשֶּׁלֶג:


וכסיד ההיכל והשלג – meaning to say that it was like the lime of the Temple but now it became like snow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:2:5)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


רבי עקיבא אומר וכו' – And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiba.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:1)


**בַּהֶרֶת.** מֵהַשְׁתָּא אַיְרֵי בְּבֶהָרוֹת טְמֵאוֹת:


בהרת – from here we are dealing with bright white spots on the skin which are impure/unclean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:2)


**וְאֵין בָּהּ כְּלוּם.** לֹא מִחְיָה וְלֹא שֵׂעָר לָבָן:


ואין בה כלום – neither quick flesh nor white hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:3)


**בַּתְּחִלָּה.** כְּשֶׁהוּבָא אֶל הַכֹּהֵן. וְכֵן בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן אִם לֹא נוֹלַד בָּהּ כְּלוּם, יַסְגִּיר:


בתחלה – when he was brought to the Kohen. And then at the end of the first week, if nothing resulted/was forthcoming, he would be shut up/placed in isolation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:4)


**בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי.** אִם לֹא נוֹלַד בָּהּ כְּלוּם, פָּטוּר. וְכֵן לְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר, אִם לֹא נוֹלַד בָּהּ כְּלוּם, עוֹמֵד בִּפְטוּר זֶה כָּל יָמָיו וְשׁוּב אֵינוֹ מַסְגִּירוֹ:


בסוף שבוע שני – if nothing resulted/was forthcoming, he is released. And similarly, after the release, if nothing resulted/was forthcoming, he remains in this release all his days and furthermore is not shut up/placed in isolation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:5)


**עוֹדֵהוּ מַסְגִּירוֹ.** שֶׁהָיָה רוֹצֶה לְהַסְגִּירוֹ, בֵּין בַּתְּחִלָּה כְּשֶׁהוּבָא אֶל הַכֹּהֵן, בֵּין בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן, וְלֹא הִסְפִּיק לְהַסְגִּירוֹ עַד שֶׁנּוֹלְדוּ סִימָנֵי טֻמְאָה, כְּגוֹן בַּתְּחִלָּה בְּשֵׂעָר לָבָן וּמִחְיָה, וּבְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן בְּשֵׂעָר לָבָן וּמִחְיָה וּפִשְׂיוֹן, יַחְלִיט. אֲבָל מִשֶּׁהִסְגִּירוֹ, הָא תְנַן לְעֵיל בְּרֵישׁ פֶּרֶק ג', אֵין מַחְלִיטִין אֶת הַמֻּסְגָּר:


עודנו מסגירו – that he (i.e., the Kohen) wanted to shut him up/isolate him, whether at the outset when he was brought to the Kohen or whether at the end of the first week, but he did not manage of isolate him until tokens/signs of defilement were forthcoming/resulted, as for example, at the outset with the white hair and the quick flesh, , and at the end of the first week with white hair and quick flesh and spreading, he would be declared a certified leper. But, from when he was shut up/put in isolation, it is taught above in the third chapter (see Mishnah 1 of the third chapter of Tractate Negaim): "אין מחליטין את המוסגר"/They do not declare one who is shut up/isolated a certified leper.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:6)


**וּפוֹטֵר.** כְּלוֹמַר אוֹ פוֹטְרוֹ, כְּגוֹן בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי. וַאֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר אִם נוֹלְדוּ סִימָנֵי טֻמְאָה, יַחְלִיט:


ופוטר – meaning to say, or they release him. S for example, at the end of the second week. But even after the release, if tokens/signs of defilement were forthcoming/resulted, he is to be declared a certified leper.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:7)


**עוֹדֵהוּ מַחְלִיטוֹ.** שֶׁהָיָה [בָא] לְהַחְלִיט וְלֹא הִסְפִּיק עַד שֶׁהָלְכוּ סִימָנֵי טֻמְאָה:


עודהו מחליטו (he is still in the process of certifying him – as a leper) – that he had [come] to certify him as a leper, but he had not been able to do it until the signs/tokens of defilement had gone away.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:3:8)


**בַּתְּחִלָּה.** כְּשֶׁהוּבָא אֶל הַכֹּהֵן, אוֹ בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן: **יַסְגִּיר.** אֲבָל אִם מִשֶּׁהֶחְלִיטוֹ הָלְכוּ לָהֶן, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהֶחְלִיטוֹ בְשֵׂעָר לָבָן וְהָלַךְ לוֹ שֵׂעָר לָבָן וּבַהֶרֶת קַיֶּמֶת, אֵין מַסְגִּירוֹ עָלֶיהָ, אֶלָּא פוֹטְרוֹ וְטָהוֹר:


בתחלה – when he was brought to the Kohen. או בסוף שבוע ראשון, יסגיר – by t if from when he declared him to be a certified leper, they went away, as for example, that he declared him to be a certified leper with the white hair, and it went away but the bright white spot on the skin exists, they don’t shut him up/isolate him for it, but release him and he is pure.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:1)


**הַתּוֹלֵשׁ סִימָנֵי טֻמְאָה.** כְּגוֹן שֵׂעָר לָבָן בְּעוֹר בָּשָׂר, וְשֵׂעָר צָהֹב בִּנְתָקִים:


התולש סימני טומאה – as for example, the white hair in the skin of the flesh, or the yellow hair that are bald, blanched spots on the head or in the beard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:2)


**וְהַכּוֹוֶה אֶת הַמִּחְיָה.** דְּמִשֶּׁנִּכְוֵית אֵינָהּ סִימַן טֻמְאָה:


והכווה את המחיה (if one cauterized/scalded the cicatrizing spot – i.e., quick flesh) – that from when it was cauterized/burnt, it is not a sign/token of defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:3)


**עוֹבֵר בְּלֹא תַעֲשֶׂה.** דְּהִשָּׁמֶר בְּנֶגַע הַצָּרַעַת. וְכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁנֶּאֱמָר הִשָּׁמֶר, פֶּן, וְאַל, אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה:


עובר בלא תעשה – of (Deuteronomy 24:8): “In cases of skin affection be most careful [to do exactly as the Levitical priests instruct you. Take care to do as I have commanded them.]”/"השמר בנגע-הצרעת [לשמר מאד ולעשות ככל אשר-יורו אתכם הכהנים הלוים כאשר צויתם תשמרו לעשות]". And in every place where it states [in the Torah]: "השמר"/Be most careful, "פן"/lest; and "אל"/do not, it is not other than a negative commandment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:4)


**וּלְטָהֳרָה.** כְּלוֹמַר וּלְעִנְיַן טָהֳרָה מַה דִּינוֹ:


ולטהרה – meaning to say, that in regard to cleanness/purity, what is the law?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:5)


**עַד שֶׁלֹּא בָא אֵצֶל כֹּהֵן.** אִם תְּלָשָׁן, טָהוֹר. וְאִם תְּלָשָׁן לְאַחַר חִלּוּטוֹ, טָמֵא:


עד שלא בא אצל כהן – if he tears them out/plucks them, he is pure. But if he tears them out after he has been declared a certified leper, he is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:6)


**הוֹלְכִים לְגַדְוָד** גָּרְסִינַן, וְהוּא שֵׁם מָקוֹם:


הולכים לגדווד – we have this reading. And it is the name of a place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:7)


**בְּתוֹךְ הֶסְגֵּרוֹ מַהוּ.** אִם תָּלַשׁ סִימָנֵי טֻמְאָה בְּתוֹךְ שָׁבוּעַ שֶׁל הֶסְגֵּר רִאשׁוֹן אוֹ שֶׁל הֶסְגֵּר שֵׁנִי, מַהוּ:


בתוך הסגירו מהו (if he does it during his quarantine – what is the law) – if he tore out/plucks the tokens/signs of defilement within the week of the first isolation/quarantine or of the second isolation, what is the law?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:8)


**הִתְחַלְתִּי מֵבִיא לָהֶם רְאָיוֹת.** בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא מַיְתֵי דְּכָךְ הָיְתָה רְאָיָתוֹ, מִפְּנֵי מָה עַד שֶׁלֹּא בָא אֵצֶל כֹּהֵן, טָהוֹר, לֹא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁלֹּא רָאָה הַכֹּהֵן סִימָנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה שֶׁבּוֹ, הַתּוֹלֵשׁ סִימָנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה תוֹךְ הֶסְגֵּר נַמִּי דִין הוּא שֶׁיְּהֵא טָהוֹר, שֶׁלֹּא רָאָה הַכֹּהֵן סִימָנֵי טֻמְאָה שֶׁבּוֹ. הִלְכָּךְ, אֶחָד עוֹמֵד בִּפְנֵי הַכֹּהֵן, כְּלוֹמַר אֶחָד אוֹתוֹ הָעוֹמֵד בִּפְנֵי הַכֹּהֵן וּמִהֵר לִתְלֹשׁ סִימָנֵי טֻמְאָה קֹדֶם שֶׁיֹּאמַר לוֹ הַכֹּהֵן טָמֵא אַתָּה, וְאֶחָד אוֹתוֹ שֶׁתְּלָשָׁן תּוֹךְ יְמֵי הֶסְגֵּרוֹ, בֵּין זֶה וּבֵין זֶה טְהוֹרִין. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


התחלתי מביא להם ראיות – in the Tosefta (Tractate Negaim, Chapter 3, Halakha 4) . Here we do not have the reading of the words of the Sages or until the bright white spot on the skin is reduced from being like a split [Cilician] bean, that especially concerning one who tears out/plucks out the tokens/signs of defilement during his being shut up/in isolation/quarantine is also the law that he would be pure/clean, for the Kohen that did not see the signs/tokens of defilement that are in him. Therefore, one who stands before a Kohen, meaning to say, one, that person who stands before a Kohen and hurries to pluck out/tear out the signs/tokens of defilement before the Kohen would say to him: “You are impure,” and one who tears them out within the days of his isolation, whether this or that, they are pure. And such is the Halakha.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:9)


**מֵאֵימָתַי הִיא טָהֳרָתוֹ.** שֶׁל אוֹתוֹ שֶׁתָּלַשׁ סִימָנֵי טֻמְאָה לְאַחַר חִלּוּטוֹ:


מאימתי הוא טהרתו – of the person who tore out/plucked the signs/tokens of defilement after he was declared to be a certified leper.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:10)


**כְּשֶׁיִּוָּלֶד לוֹ נֶגַע אַחֵר וְיִטְהַר מִמֶּנּוּ.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּנִתְרַפָּא מִן הַשְּׁנִיָּה, אִגַּלַּאי מִלְּתָא דְּחָס רַחֲמָנָא עֲלֵיהּ, וְאִי נַמִּי הֲוָת קַיְמָא קַמַּיְתָא הֲוָה מִתַּסֵּי מִנַּהּ כְּמוֹ מִזּוֹ:


כשיוולד לו נגע אחר ויטהר ממנו – for since he was cured from the second, the matter became revealed that the All-Merciful has forbearance upon him, or alternatively, that the first [plague] endures and he will be healed from that one as well.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:11)


**עַד שֶׁתִּפְרַח בְּכֻלּוֹ.** נֶגַע הָאַחֲרוֹן, דְּלֵיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ לְמִידִי. דְּהָא אִי הֲוָה בֵיהּ נַמִּי קַמָּא, הַשְׁתָּא מִיהָא הָוֵי טָהוֹר:


עד שתפרח בכולו (until it spreads over his entire body) – the last plague, that one doesn’t have to be concerned for anything, because if he had in him also the first, now, however/at least, he would be pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:4:12)


**אוֹ עַד שֶׁתִּתְמַעֵט בַּהַרְתּוֹ מִכַּגְּרִיס.** דַּאֲפִלּוּ לֹא נִתְלַשׁ הַשֵּׂעָר וְלֹא נִכְוֵית הַמִּחְיָה, הָיָה נִטְהָר, מִכֵּיוָן דְּנִתְמַעֲטָה הַבַּהֶרֶת מִכַּגְּרִיס. וּמִיהוּ עַד שֶׁתִּפְרַח בְּכֻלּוֹ לֹא קָאֵי אֶלָּא אַתּוֹלֵשׁ סִימָנֵי טֻמְאָה, אֲבָל כּוֹוֶה אֶת הַמִּחְיָה אֵינוֹ נִטְהָר בִּפְרִיחָה, שֶׁאִלּוּ הָיְתָה הַמִּחְיָה קַיֶּמֶת לֹא הָיָה טָהוֹר בִּפְרִיחָה. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


או עד שתתמעט בהרתו מכגריס – for even if the hair had not been torn out nor the quick flesh cauterized, he would be made pure, for since that the bright white spot on the skin had been reduced from [the size] of a split [Cilician] bean . But however, until it spread through is entire body, is not referring other than to someone who tears out the signs/tokens of impurity, but cauterizing the quick flesh is not made pure through an eruption (i.e., the white eruption which causes the leper to be declared clean – see Leviticus 13:12), for if the quick flesh exists, it was not pure through an eruption. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:5:1)


**וְנִקְצְצָה טְהוֹרָה.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) כָּל יְמֵי אֲשֶׁר הַנֶּגַע בּוֹ יִטְמָא, וְלֹא יָמִים שֶׁהָיְתָה בוֹ בַהֶרֶת וְנִקְצְצָה:


ונקצצה טהורה – as it is written (Leviticus 13:46): “He shall be impure as long as the disease is on him,” but not the days that the bright white spot on his skin was on him and it was cut off.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:5:2)


**קְצָצָהּ מִתְכַּוֵּן.** בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא מוּכָח דְּהֵיכָא דְקָצַץ כָּל הַבַּהֶרֶת וְקָצַץ מִן הַבָּשָׂר אֲשֶׁר סְבִיבוֹתֶיהָ עִמָּהּ, כֻּלֵּי עָלְמָא לֹא פְלִיגֵי דְּאֵין לוֹ טָהֳרָה עוֹלָמִית, דִּקְנָסוּהוּ רַבָּנָן מֵאַחַר שֶׁנִּתְכַּוֵּן לְשָׁרֵשׁ אַחֲרֶיהָ. וְהֵיכָא דִקְצָצָהּ וְשִׁיֵּר מִמֶּנָּה כָל שֶׁהוּא, כֻּלֵּי עָלְמָא לֹא פְלִיגֵי דְּיֵשׁ לוֹ טָהֳרָה בִּפְרִיחָה, כֵּיוָן דְּשִׁיֵּר מִמֶּנָּה לֹא קְנָסוּהוּ כֻלֵּי הַאי. כִּי פְלִיגֵי, כְּשֶׁקְּצָצָהּ מְצֻמְצֶמֶת הַבַּהֶרֶת כְּמוֹ שֶׁהִיא לֹא פָחוֹת וְלֹא יוֹתֵר:


קצצה מתכוין – in the Tosefta (Tractate Negaim, Chapter 3, Halakha 5 ) it is proven that where he cut off all of the bright white spot on the skin and cut off from the flesh that was around it with it, no one disagrees that he doesn’t have eternal purity. For the Rabbis fined him after he had intended to uproot it/tear it out. But where he cut it and left a remainder from it, whatever the amount, everyone does not argue that he has purity through an eruption (i.e., the white eruption which causes the leper to be declared clean), since he left a remnant from it, they did not fine him all that much. What they do disagree about is when he cut off the bright white spot in the skin in an exactly defined manner, like it is, no less and no more.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:5:3)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר לִכְשֶׁיִּוָּלֶד לוֹ נֶגַע אַחֵר וְיִטְהַר מִמֶּנּוּ וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים עַד שֶׁתִּפְרַח בְּכֻלּוֹ.** וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים. הָכָא לֹא גָרְסִינַן בְּמִלְּתַיְהוּ דַחֲכָמִים אוֹ עַד שֶׁתִּתְמַעֵט בַּהֶרֶת מִכַּגְּרִיס, דְּדַוְקָא גַבֵּי תוֹלֵשׁ סִימָנֵי טֻמְאָה דְרֵישָׁא וְנִשְׁאֲרָה הַבַּהֶרֶת, הָתָם שַׁיָּךְ לְמִתְנֵי עַד שֶׁתִּתְמַעֵט מִכַּגְּרִיס, אֲבָל הָכָא הֲרֵי נִקְצְצָה כָל הַבַּהֶרֶת:


ר' אליעזר אומר לכשיולד לו נגע אחר ויטהר ממנו וחכמים אומרים עד שתפרח בכולו – And the Halakha is according to the Sages (i.e., until it spreads all over him). Here we do not have the reading of the words of the Sages (as we had in the previous Mishnah): "או עד שתתמעט בהרת מכגריס"/or until his bright white spot on the skin will diminish to less than the size of a split [Cilician] bean, that specifically concerning tearing/plucking out, the signs/tokens of defilement of the first clause [of the Mishnah]: "ונשארה הבהרת"/but the bright white spot in the skin remained. There it belongs to teach [in the Mishnah]: “until it diminishes to less than the size of a split [Cilician] bean,” but here, the entire bright white spot in the skin was cut off.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 7:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/7:5:4)


**וְאִם הָיְתָה בְרֹאשׁ הָעָרְלָה יִמֹּל.** לָאו בְּמִילָה בִזְמַנָּהּ אַיְרֵי, דְּהָא מִלְּתָא דִפְשִׁיטָא הִיא, הַשְׁתָּא שַׁבָּת דְּאִסּוּר סְקִילָה דָּחְיָא, צָרַעַת דְּאִסּוּר לָאו גְּרֵידָא לֹא כָל שֶׁכֵּן, אֶלָּא אֲפִלּוּ מִילָה שֶׁלֹּא בִזְמַנָּהּ שֶׁאֵינָהּ דּוֹחָה אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת, דּוֹחָה אֶת הַצָּרַעַת, דְּאָתֵי עֲשֵׂה דְמִילָה וְדָחֵי אֶת לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה דְצָרַעַת, דְּהָכִי קַיְמָא לָן בְּכָל הַתּוֹרָה כֻלָּהּ, כָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁאַתָּה מוֹצֵא עֲשֵׂה וְלֹא תַעֲשֶׂה, אִם אַתָּה יָכוֹל לְקַיֵּם אֶת שְׁנֵיהֶם, מוּטָב. וְאִם לָאו, יָבֹא עֲשֵׂה וְיִדְחֶה אֶת לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה:


ואם היתה בראש הערלה ימול – we are not dealing with circumcision at its appropriate time (i.e., eight-days old for a baby boy), for this is a simple matter, now that on Shabbat the prohibition [which if one violates it leads to the punishment] of stoning is suspended; leprosy, which is a mere prohibition, not all the more so, but even circumcision that is not [performed] at its appropriate time, does not supersede the Sabbath, but supersedes the leprosy, for the positive commandment of circumcision comes and supersedes the negative commandment of leprosy, and in this we hold throughout the entire Torah. Every place that you find a positive commandment and a negative commandment, if you are able to fulfill both of them, it is good. But if not, the positive commandment should come and supersede the negative commandment.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:1:1)


**הַפּוֹרֵחַ מִן הַטָּמֵא טָהוֹר.** טָמֵא שֶׁנֶּחְלַט בְּאֶחָד מִן הַנְּגָעִים, וְאַחַר כָּךְ פָּרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ, טָהוֹר:


הפורח מן הטמא טהור (a leprosy that blossoms after a person has been declared unclean, is clean) -an unclean person that was declared a certified leper with one of the plagues, and afterwards it blossomed throughout his body (i.e., that it all turned white), is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:1:2)


**חָזְרוּ בוֹ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים.** אִם לְאַחַר הַפְּרִיחָה חָזְרוּ וְנִתְגַּלּוּ אֲפִלּוּ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים בִּלְבַד:


חזרו בו ראשי אברים (the tips of the limbs reappeared with it) – if after the “blossoming”/eruption (i.e., the white eruption which causes the leper to be declared clean) even the tips of the limbs reappeared alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:1:3)


**טָמֵא.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) וּבְיוֹם הֵרָאוֹת בּוֹ בָּשָׂר חַי יִטְמָא. וְהוּא שֶׁיֵּרָאוּ כָעֲדָשָׁה:


טמא – as it is written (Leviticus 13:14): “But as soon as un-discolored flesh appears in it, he shall be impure.”/"וביום הראות בו בשר חי יטמא" , and it should appear like a lentil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:1:4)


**עַד שֶׁתִּתְמַעֵט בַּהַרְתּוֹ מִכַּגְּרִיס.** שֶׁיֵּלֵךְ כָּל הַנֶּגַע וְלֹא יִשְׁתַּיֵּר בּוֹ כַגְּרִיס:


עד שתתמעט בהרתו מכגריס – that he gave rise/brought forth the entire plague and nothing remains of it like the size of a split bean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:1:5)


**מִטָּהוֹר טָמֵא.** טָהוֹר שֶׁפָּרְחָה צָרַעַת בְּכֻלּוֹ, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהֶחְלִיטוֹ בְשֵׂעָר לָבָן אוֹ בְמִחְיָה וְנָשַׁר שֵׂעָר לָבָן אוֹ נִתְכַּסֵּית הַמִּחְיָה, וּפְטָרוֹ, אִי נַמִּי הִסְגִּירוֹ בְּבַהֶרֶת כַּגְּרִיס וְעָמַד בְּעֵינָיו שְׁנֵי שָׁבוּעוֹת וּפְטָרוֹ, בְּכָל הָנֵי אִם אַחַר פְּטוּרוֹ פָּרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ, טָמֵא:


מטהור טמא – something pure that the leprosy blossomed throughout his body. As for example that he had declared him to be a certified leper with white hair or quick flesh and the white hair fell out or the quick flesh was covered, and he was released; alternatively, he (i.e., the Kohen) locked him up/isolated him with a bright white spot on the skin the size of a split [Cilician] bean but it remained unchanged for two weeks and he was released, in all of these, if after his release it blossomed throughout his body, he is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:1:6)


**עַד שֶׁתַּחֲזֹר לִכְמוֹת שֶׁהָיְתָה.** דְּאִם נִשְׁתַּיֵּר מִן הַפְּרִיחָה כָּל שֶׁהוּא סָמוּךְ לָאוֹם, טָמֵא מִשּׁוּם פִּשְׂיוֹן. וְהָא דִתְנַן בְּסָמוּךְ שֶׁאִם הָלְכָה מִחְיָה וְאַחַר כָּךְ פָּרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ, טָהוֹר, וּתְנַן נַמִּי בַּהֶרֶת וּבָהּ שֵׂעָר לָבָן פָּרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַשֵּׂעָר לָבָן בִּמְקוֹמוֹ, טָהוֹר, וּמַשְׁמַע וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן אִם אֵינוֹ בִמְקוֹמוֹ שֶׁהוּא טָהוֹר, הָתָם מַיְרֵי שֶׁלֹּא פְטָרוֹ הַכֹּהֵן עֲדַיִן כְּשֶׁפָּרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ, וְהָכָא מַיְרֵי כְּשֶׁפְּטָרוֹ וְאַחַר כָּךְ פָּרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ:


עד שתחזור לכמות שהיתה – that if there remained from the blossoming whatever the amount near/adjacent to the starting point of leprosy, he is unclean because of spreading, for as it is taught in the Mishnah adjacent (see Mishnah 2 of this eighth chapter of Tractate Negaim) that if the quick flesh went away and afterwards it (i.e., the bright white spot) blossomed over all of his body, he is pure, and it is also taught in the Mishnah (i.e., see Mishnah 3 of this eighth chapter of Tractate Negaim) concerning the bright white spot on the skin/בהרת and in it is white hair which blossomed throughout his body, even though the white hair is in its place, he is pure, and it implies, that all the more so, if it (i.e., the white hair) is not in its place, he is pure, for there (i.e., Mishnah 3 of this chapter) we are speaking that the Kohen did not yet release him when it blossomed throughout his body, but here (in our Mishnah) we are speaking when he (i.e., the Kohen) released him and afterwards it blossomed throughout his body.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:2:1)


**וְאַחַר כָּךְ הָלְכָה לָהּ הַמִּחְיָה.** שֶׁפָּשָׂה הַנֶּגַע עָלֶיהָ. אֲבָל בִּשְׁעַת הַפְּרִיחָה לֹא הָלְכָה לָהּ מִחְיָה עַד אַחַר כָּךְ. [אֲבָל לֹא נִתְכַּסֵּית הַמִּחְיָה], אֵין הַפְּרִיחָה מְטַהַרְתּוֹ:


ואח"כ הלכה לה מחיה – that the plague spread upon it. But at the hour of the blossoming, the quick flesh did not go away until afterwards [but the quick flesh was not covered] the blossoming does not purify it.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:3:1)


**בַּהֶרֶת.** כַּגְּרִיס:


בהרת – like the size of a split [Cilician] bean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:3:2)


**וּבָהּ שֵׂעָר לָבָן.** הָא קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דִּפְרִיחָה מְטַהֶרֶת אַחַר כָּל סִימָנֵי טֻמְאָה:


ובה שער לבן – surely it comes to tell us that blossoming [of the bright white spot] purifies after all of the tokens/signs of defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:3:3)


**פָּרְחָה בְמִקְצָתוֹ, טָמֵא.** אִצְטְרִיךְ לְאַשְׁמְעִינַן אֲפִלּוּ פָרְחָה בְרֻבּוֹ, דְּלֹא אָמְרִינַן רֻבּוֹ כְּכֻלּוֹ. אִי נַמִּי אַשְׁמְעִינַן דִּבְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן אוֹ בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי טָמֵא בִּפְרִיחַת מִקְצָתוֹ אֲפִלּוּ בְכָל שֶׁהוּא מִשּׁוּם פִּשְׂיוֹן:


פרחה במקצתו טמא – it is necessary to inform us that even if it blossomed through most of his body, that we don’t say, the majority is like the whole. Alternatively it informs us that at the end of the first week or at the end of the second week he is impure with blossoming over part of his body even whatever the amount because of spreading.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:4:1)


**שֶׁבִּפְרִיחָתָן טִהֲרוּ טָמֵא.** שֶׁעַל יְדֵי פְרִיחָתָן טִהֲרוּ אֶת הַטָּמֵא. כְּשֶׁחָזְרוּ וְנִתְגַּלּוּ אַחֲרֵי כֵן, חוֹזֵר לְטֻמְאָתוֹ:


שבפריחתן טיהרו טמא – that through their blossoming, they have purified the defiled person. When they have reappeared and have been uncovered/revealed after that, he returns to his defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:4:2)


**שֶׁבַּחֲזִירָתָם טִמְּאוּ טָהוֹר.** שֶׁעַל יְדֵי חֲזָרָתָן שֶׁחָזְרוּ וְנִתְגַּלּוּ טִמְּאוּ אֶת הַטָּהוֹר, אִם חָזְרוּ וְנִתְכַּסּוּ, שֶׁפָּשָׂה עֲלֵיהֶם הַנֶּגַע, טָהוֹר. וְאִם חָזְרוּ וְנִתְגַּלּוּ אַחַר כָּךְ, טָמֵא. וְאִצְטְרִיךְ לְאַשְׁמְעִינַן דַּאֲפִלּוּ זִמְנֵי טוּבָא, כִּדְקָתָנֵי וַאֲפִלּוּ מֵאָה פְעָמִים:


שבחזירתם טמאו טהור – that through their return that they returned and were uncovered/revealed. They defiled the pure person, if they returned and were covered that the plague spread upon them, he is pure, bur if they returned and were uncovered afterwards, he is impure. And it is necessary to inform us that even many times, as it is taught [in this Mishnah], even one hundred times.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:5:1)


**כֹּל הָרָאוּי לִטַּמֵּא בְנֶגַע הַבַּהֶרֶת.** כְּגוֹן עוֹר בָּשָׂר:


כל הראוי לטמא בנגע הבהרת (all who are susceptible to be defiled through the plague of the bright white spot) – such as the skin of the flesh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:5:2)


**מְעַכֵּב אֶת הַפְּרִיחָה.** אִם נִשְׁתַּיֵּר מִמֶּנּוּ כָל שֶׁהוּא שֶׁלֹּא פָרְחָה בוֹ, מְעַכֵּב אֶת טָהֳרָתוֹ:


מעכב את הפריחה (hinders the blossoming [over the entire body which marks the person as clean])- if there remained from it whatever the amount that did not blossom in it, it hinders/delays his purification.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:5:3)


**וְכֹל שֶׁאֵינוֹ רָאוּי לִטַּמֵּא בְנֶגַע הַבַּהֶרֶת.** כְּגוֹן רֹאשׁ וְזָקָן וְכָל הָנֵי דַחֲשִׁיב בַּהֲדַיְהוּ:


וכל שאינו ראוי ליטמא בנגע הבהרת – as for example, the head and the beard, and all of these that are considered together with them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:5:4)


**אֵין מְעַכְּבִין אֶת הַפְּרִיחָה.** דְּאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא פָרַח הַנֶּגַע עֲלֵיהֶם, כֵּיוָן דְּפָרַח בִּשְׁאָר הַגּוּף, טָהוֹר. וְרָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים לֹא קָא חֲשִׁיב, דְּהַנְהוּ מְעַכְּבִין אֶת הַפְּרִיחָה, דִּרְאוּיִין לִטַּמֵּא בְנֶגַע הַבַּהֶרֶת נִינְהוּ, שֶׁהֲרֵי הַנֶּגַע פּוֹשֶׂה בָּהֶן:


אין מעכבין את הפריחה – for even though the plague did not blossom upon them, since it blossomed in the rest of the body, he is ritually pure. And the tips of the limbs are not considered for they hinder the blossoming, for they are susceptible to become defiled through the plague of the bright white spot in the skin, for the plague spreads in them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:5:5)


**וְנַעֲשׂוּ צָרֶבֶת טְהוֹרִים.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּעַכְשָׁיו שֶׁנִּקְרְחוּ הָרֹאשׁ וְהַזָּקָן רְאוּיִים לִטַּמֵּא בְּנֶגַע הַבַּהֶרֶת, וְכֵן שְׁחִין וּמִכְוָה וְקֶדַח הַמּוֹרְדִים שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ צָרֶבֶת, הֲרֵי הֵן כְּעוֹר בָּשָׂר, כֵּיוָן דְּבִשְׁעַת הַפְּרִיחָה לֹא הָיוּ רְאוּיִין, לֹא מְעַכְּבִים וְטָהוֹר:


ונעשו צרבת טהורים (and they formed a scab, they are pure) – even though that now when the head and/or the beard became bald they became susceptible to be defiled through the plague of the bright white spot on the skin, and similarly, boils and burns and the festering blister formed a scab, they are like the skin of the flesh, since at the time of the blossoming, they were not susceptible, they are not hindered and are pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:5:6)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה מְקוֹם הַמִּחְיָה בַּהֶרֶת טָמֵא.** דִּפְרִיחָה קַמַּיְתָא לָאו פְּרִיחָה הִיא, כֵּיוָן דְּאִשְׁתַּיֵּר מֵעוֹר הַבָּשָׂר חֲצִי עֲדָשָׁה סָמוּךְ לָרֹאשׁ, וּפְרִיחָה דְהַשְׁתָּא שֶׁנִּתְכַּסֵּית הַמִּחְיָה שֶׁל חֲצִי עֲדָשָׁה נַמִּי לָא חֲשִׁיבָא פְרִיחָה, כֵּיוָן דִּכְבָר נִקְרְחוּ וְנַעֲשׂוּ צָרֶבֶת וַהֲרֵי הֵן כְּעוֹר בָּשָׂר, וְלֹא מַהֲנֵי עַד דְּפָרַח נַמִּי עֲלַיְהוּ:


אע"פ שנעשה מקום המחיה בהרת טמא – for the first blossoming is not blossoming, since there remains from the skin of the flesh one-half of a lentil adjacent to the head, whereas the current blossoming where the quick flesh is covered of half a lentil’s size is also not considered blossoming, since they have been made bald already and they made a scab, they are like the skin of the flesh, and it is not effective until it blossoms also upon them.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:1)


**אַחַת טְמֵאָה.** מֻחְלֶטֶת:


אחת טמאה – certified as a leper.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:2)


**וְאַחַת טְהוֹרָה.** לְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר. שֶׁעָמַד בְּעֵינָיו בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי:


ואחת טהורה – after the release, that it remained unchanged at he end of the second week.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:3)


**וּפָרְחָה מִזּוֹ לָזוֹ.** בֵּין מִטְּהוֹרָה לַטְּמֵאָה בֵּין מִטְּמֵאָה לַטְּהוֹרָה:


ופרחה מזו לזו – whether from pure to impure or whether from impure to pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:4)


**וְאַחַר כָּךְ פָּרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ טָהוֹר.** מִשּׁוּם דִּטְהוֹרָה נַעֲשֵׂית טְמֵאָה כְּשֶׁפָּרַח מִמֶּנָּה לַטְּמֵאָה, דַּהֲרֵי פָשְׂתָה, וְכִי פָרַח אַחַר כָּךְ בְּכֻלּוֹ, הָוֵי פוֹרֵחַ מִן (הטמאה לטהור) [הַטָּמֵא, וְטָהוֹר]:


ואח"כ פרחה בכולו טהור – because something ritually pure becomes impure when it blossomed from it to defile it, for it had spread, and when it blossomed afterwards through the entire body, it is blossoming from (the impure to the pure) [the impure, and the pure].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:5)


**בִּשְׂפָתוֹ הָעֶלְיוֹנָה.** חֲצִי גְרִיס בַּהֶרֶת. וּבִשְׂפָתוֹ הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה חֲצִי גְרִיס, וּכְשֶׁסּוֹגֵר פִּיו נִרְאֵית כַּגְּרִיס בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד. וְכֵן בִּשְׁתֵּי אֶצְבָּעוֹת וּבִשְׁנֵי רִיסֵי עֵינָיו, כְּשֶׁמְּדַבְּקָן נִרְאֵית כַּגְּרִיס שָׁלֵם:


בשפתו העליונה ([if there were two bright spots, each the size of half of split [Cilician] bean]] one on his upper lip) – half of a split [Cilician] bean of a bright white spot on the skin, and on his upper lip, half of a split [Cilician] bean, and when he closes his mouth, it appears like a split [Cilician] bean in one place. And similarly on two fingers or on the two eye-lids with eye-lashes, and he makes them cleave together it appears like a complete [Cilician] bean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:6)


**אֲבָל לֹא בַבֹּהַק.** מַרְאֵה לַבְנוּנִית לְמַטָּה מֵאַרְבָּעָה מַרְאוֹת, וּמְעַכֵּב אֶת הַפְּרִיחָה:


אבל לא בבוהק (but not on the white scurf/tetter) – it appears white below from the four appearances and hinders the blossoming.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:7)


**חָזְרוּ בוֹ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים.** שֶׁנִּתְגַּלּוּ מִן הַנֶּגַע וְנַעֲשָׂה בֹהַק:


חזרו בו ראשי אברים – that were revealed from the plague and became a white scurf/tetter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:6:8)


**טָהוֹר.** וְהוּא הַדִּין בִּשְׁאָר הַגּוּף. דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּבֹהַק מְעַכֵּב אֶת הַפְּרִיחָה בַתְּחִלָּה, אֵינוֹ מְעַכֵּב בַּסּוֹף. וְאַגַּב סֵיפָא דְּבָעֵי לְמִתְנֵי רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים, תָּנָא הָכָא רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים. וּבַסֵּיפָא נָקַט רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים, לְהוֹדִיעֲךָ כֹּחוֹ דְרַבִּי מֵאִיר דַּאֲפִלּוּ בְרָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים שֶׁנִּתְגַּלּוּ פָחוֹת מִכָּעֲדָשָׁה וְנַעֲשׂוּ בֹהַק מְטַמֵּא רַבִּי מֵאִיר, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן בִּשְׁאָר הַגּוּף. וְהַךְ רֵישָׁא דְּחָזְרוּ בוֹ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים כְּמִין בֹּהַק טָהוֹר, כְּרַבָּנָן אָתְיָא וְלֹא כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר. וְרַבָּנָן דִּמְטַהֲרֵי, לָאו דַּוְקָא פָחוֹת מִכָּעֲדָשָׁה, דַּאֲפִלּוּ טוּבָא נַמִּי טָהוֹר, דְּבֹהַק בַּסּוֹף אֵינוֹ מְעַכֵּב. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


טהור – and the same law applies in the rest of the body, for even though the white scurf/tetter hinders the blossoming at the outset, it doesn’t hinder it at the end, and subsidiary to the concluding clause [of the Mishnah] that wishes to teach "ראשי אברים"/the tips of the limbs, it teaches [in the Mishnah] here, “the tips of the limbs.” But in the concluding clause, it (i.e., the Mishnah) takes the phrase, “the tips of the limbs” to inform you about the power of [the opinion of] Rabbi Meir that even at the tips of the limbs that were revealed/uncovered less than the size of a lentil, and became a white scurf/tetter, Rabbi Meir declares unclean, and all the more so in the rest of the body, but that of the first-clause [of the Mishnah] that “the tips of the limbs reappeared,” like a species of pure/clean white scurf, it is brought according to the Rabbis, but not according to Rabbi Meir. But the Rabbis who declare it pure, not exactly/specifically less than a lentil, for even more also it is ritually pure, for a white scurf/tetter at the end does not hinder. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:1)


**הַבָּא כֻלּוֹ לָבָן.** מִתְּחִלָּה בָּא כֻלּוֹ מְכֻסֶּה בַנֶּגַע:


הבא כולו לבן – from the outset he comes completely covered with a plague.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:2)


**יַסְגִּיר.** כְּדִין בַּהֶרֶת כַּגְּרִיס. שֶׁמָּא יִוָּלֵד לוֹ שֵׂעָר לָבָן אוֹ מִחְיָה. דְּאֵין פְּרִיחָה מְטַהֶרֶת בַּתְּחִלָּה:


יסגיר – like the law of a bright white spot on the skin like a split [Cilician] bean, lest it subsequently appear on him white hair or quick flesh. For the white eruption which causes the leper to be declared clean does not purify at the outset.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:3)


**נוֹלַד לוֹ שֵׂעָר לָבָן.** בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן אוֹ בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי, יַחְלִיט. וְאִם לֹא בָאוּ סִימָנֵי טֻמְאָה, יִפְטֹר:


נולד לו שער לבן – at the end of the first week or at the end of the second week, let him be declared a certified leper. But if these tokens/signs of impurity did not appear, he should be released.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:4)


**הִשְׁחִירוּ שְׁתֵּיהֶן.** כָּל הָנֵי דַחֲשִׁיב, סִימָנֵי טָהֳרָה נִינְהוּ, וּמְפֹרָשִׁים הֵם לְעֵיל בְּסוֹף פֶּרֶק קַמָּא:


השחירו שתיהן – all of these that are considered, they are signs/tokens of purity, and they are explained above at the end of the first chapter (Mishnah 6).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:5)


**נוֹלַד לוֹ מִחְיָה אוֹ שֵׂעָר לָבָן.** אִם לְאַחַר שֶׁבָּאוּ סִימָנֵי טָהֳרָה הַלָּלוּ נוֹלַד לוֹ מִחְיָה אוֹ שֵׂעָר לָבָן, טָמֵא. וְאִם לָאו, טָהוֹר:


נולד לו מחיה או שער לבן – if after these signs/tokens of ritual purity came, there appeared upon him quick flesh or white hair, he is impure, but if not, he is ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:6)


**וְכֻלָּן שֶׁחָזְרוּ בָהֶם רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים.** הַבָּא כֻלּוֹ לָבָן בַּתְּחִלָּה, בֵּין שֶׁהֻזְקַק לְהַסְגִּיר בֵּין לְהַחְלִיט בֵּין לִפְטֹר, אִם חָזְרוּ וְנִתְגַּלּוּ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים שֶׁלָּהֶן, הֲרֵי הֵן כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיוּ וּטְהוֹרִים, דְּלָא חֲשִׁיבֵי מִחְיָה. כֵּיצַד, הַבָּא כֻלּוֹ לָבָן בַּתְּחִלָּה וְהֻסְגַּר שְׁנֵי שָׁבוּעוֹת וְלֹא נוֹלְדוּ בוֹ סִימָנֵי טֻמְאָה, וּפְטָרוֹ, וְאַחַר כָּךְ נִתְגַּלּוּ בוֹ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים, הַיְנוּ חָזְרוּ בוֹ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים מִתּוֹךְ הַפְּטוּר, וְטָהוֹר. וְאִם הֶחְלִיטוֹ בְשֵׂעָר לָבָן וְהִשְׁחִירוּ הַשְּׂעָרוֹת וְאַחַר כָּךְ נִתְגַּלּוּ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים, הַיְנוּ חָזְרוּ בוֹ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים מִתּוֹךְ הֶחְלֵט, וְטָהוֹר. וְאִם בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן אוֹ בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי נִתְגַּלּוּ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים, הַיְנוּ חָזְרוּ בּוֹ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים מִתּוֹךְ הֶסְגֵּר, וְטָהוֹר. כָּךְ נִרְאֵית פֵּרוּשׁ מִשְׁנָה זוֹ מִתּוֹךְ תּוֹרַת כֹּהֲנִים. אֲבָל רַמְבַּ"ם לֹא פֵרְשָׁהּ כֵּן:


וכולן שחזרו בהם ראשי אברים – He that comes entirely white at the outset, whether he needed to be shut up/placed in isolation/quarantine whether to be declared as a certified leper or whether to be released, if their tips of the limbs reappeared/were uncovered again, they are like they were and ritually pure, as we don’t consider the quick flesh. How so? A person who comes entirely white from the outset and was isolated/quarantined for two weeks and there didn’t appear on him signs/tokens of impurity, and he was released, but afterwards, the tips of the limbs were uncovered/revealed on him, that is, that the tips of the limbs returned from the release, and he is pure. But if he was declared to be a certified leper with white hair and the hair turned black, and afterwards, the tips of the limbs were uncovered/revealed, that is, the tips of the limbs were restored while being a certified leper and he is pure. But if at the end of the first week or the end of the second week, the tips of the limbs were revealed/uncovered, that is, the tips of the limbs were restored on him while in isolation/quarantine, and he is ritually pure. This is how the explanation of this Mishnah appeared from Sifra/Torat Kohanim. But Maimonides did not explain it this way.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:7)


**פָּרְחָה בְמִקְצָתוֹ.** בְּמִקְצָת רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים שֶׁנִּתְגַּלּוּ:


פרחה במקצתו – in part of the tips of the limbs that were uncovered/revealed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:8)


**טָמֵא.** שֶׁהֲרֵי פָשָׂה הַנֶּגַע:


טמא – that behold the plague spread.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:9)


**פָּרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ.** אַחַר שֶׁפָּרְחָה בְמִקְצָתוֹ:


פרחה בכולו – after it had blossomed in part of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:7:10)


**טָהוֹר.** דַּהֲוֵי לֵיהּ פּוֹרֵחַ מִן הַטָּמֵא. אֲבָל אִם מִתְּחִלָּה לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּתְגַּלּוּ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים פָּרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ בְּבַת אַחַת, טָמֵא מִשּׁוּם פִּשְׂיוֹן. וְהָא דְתָנָא רֵישָׁא פָּרְחָה בְמִקְצָתוֹ טָמֵא, הוּא הַדִּין פָּרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ נַמִּי טָמֵא, וְלֹא נָקַט פָּרְחָה בְמִקְצָתוֹ אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם דְּבָעֵי לְמִתְנֵי בָתְרַהּ פָּרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ טָהוֹר, דְּהַיְנוּ לְאַחַר שֶׁפָּרְחָה בְמִקְצָתוֹ:


טהור – that he has blossoming from the impure, but if from the outset after the tips of the limbs were uncovered it blossomed throughout the entire body at one time, he is impure because of spreading. But that which the first clause [of the Mishnah] teaches that if it blossomed in part of his body, he is impure, the same law applies when it blossomed/erupted throughout the body, he is also impure, but it (i.e., the Mishnah) did not use [the phrase] "פרחה במקצתו"/that it blossomed in part of the body other than because it wanted to teach the latter "פרחה בכולו טהור"/it blossomed/erupted in all of his body that he is pure, that is, after it blossomed/erupted in part of his body.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:8:1)


**מִתּוֹךְ הַטָּהֳרָה טָמֵא.** כְּגוֹן הַבָּא כֻלּוֹ לָבָן בַּתְּחִלָּה, וּלְאַחַר הֶסְגֵּר אוֹ הֶחְלֵט נִטְהַר, דְּאִם חָזְרוּ בוֹ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים וְנִתְגַּלּוּ, טָהוֹר, כִּדְפָרֵישְׁנָא לְעֵיל. וְאִם מִתּוֹךְ טָהֳרָה זוֹ חָזְרוּ וְנִתְכַּסּוּ בְּבַת אַחַת, טָמֵא מִשּׁוּם פִּשְׂיוֹן, וְהַיְנוּ דְקָאָמַר אִם פָּרְחָה כֻלּוֹ כְּאַחַת מִתּוֹךְ טָהֳרָה, טָמֵא, כִּדְפָרֵישְׁנָא בְמַתְנִיתִין דִּלְעֵיל דְּאִם פָּרְחָה כֻלּוֹ בְּבַת אַחַת נַמִּי טָמֵא כְּמוֹ פָּרְחָה בְמִקְצָתוֹ:


מתוך הטהרה טמא – as for example, a person who comes completely white at the outset, and after the isolation/quarantine/being shut up or the declaration of being a certified leper he became pure, if the tips of the limbs returned on him and were uncovered, he is pure, as we explained above (i.e., in the previous Mishnah). But if during a state of cleanness/purity, they returned and became uncovered at one instant, he is impure because of spreading, and that is what it states, that if it blossomed/erupted (the white eruption which causes the leper to be declared clean) at once while in a state of purity, he becomes impure, as we explained in the Mishnah above, that if it his entire body blossomed/erupted at a single instant, he is also impure like [the case of] part of his body blossoming/erupting.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:8:2)


**פָּטוּר מִן הַפְּרִיעָה וּמִן הַפְּרִימָה.** כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהוּא מֻסְגָּר. וְאִלּוּ טָהוֹר מִתּוֹךְ הֶחְלֵט, חַיָּב בִּפְרִיעָה וּפְרִימָה, כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהוּא מֻחְלָט. פְּרִיעָה וּפְרִימָה, בְּגָדָיו יִהְיוּ פְרֻמִים וְרֹאשׁוֹ יִהְיֶה פָרוּעַ:


פטור מן הפריעה ומן הפרימה (he is exempt from the letting the hair grow in neglect and rending his garments – see Leviticus 13:45) – all the while that he is in quarantine/isolation. But whereas he is pure/clean while he is in a state of being a certified leper, he is obligated for letting his hair grow in neglect and tearing his clothing, all the while that he is a certified leper. פריעה ופרימה: His clothing will be torn and his hair in disarray.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:8:3)


**מְטַמְּאִין בְּבִיאָה.** אִם בָּאוּ אֶל הָאֹהֶל נִטְמָא כָּל אֲשֶׁר בָּאֹהֶל:


מטמאין בביאה – if they came into the tent, everything that is in the tent is defiled.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:9:1)


**פָּרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ וְאַחַר כָּךְ נִתְגַּלּוּ בוֹ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים.** הָכָא מַיְרֵי שֶׁלֹּא הִסְפִּיק לְהַחְלִיטוֹ עַד שֶׁפָּרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ וְנִתְגַּלּוּ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים, דְּרַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל מְדַמֵּי לֵיהּ לְבָא כֻלּוֹ לָבָן בַּתְּחִלָּה חוּץ מֵרָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים, דְּטָעוּן הֶסְגֵּר. וְרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בֶּן עֲזַרְיָה סָבַר, דְּכֵיוָן דְּאִלּוּ לֹא פָרְחָה הָיָה מַחְלִיטוֹ, דִּינוֹ כְבַהֶרֶת קְטַנָּה וּבָהּ מִחְיָה, דְּאִי פָרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ וְאַחַר כָּךְ נִתְגַּלּוּ רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים, טָמֵא. וְעוֹד יֵשׁ לְפָרֵשׁ, דַּאֲפִלּוּ הֶחְלִיטוֹ בְּמִחְיָה וּפָרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ וְטִהֲרוֹ, קָסָבַר רַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל דְּאֵין מְטַמֵּא בַחֲזָרַת רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים, דְּכִי מְטַמֵּא בַחֲזָרַת רָאשֵׁי אֵבָרִים שֶׁאַחַר פְּרִיחָה, הֵיכָא דְּטָהַר בְּתוֹרַת פְּרִיחָה, אֲבָל הָכָא לָאו בְּתוֹרַת פְּרִיחָה טָהַר, דְּהָא אִלּוּ נִתְמַעֲטָה מִחְיָה מִכָּעֲדָשָׁה [נַמִּי] טָהוֹר:


פרחה בכולו ואח"כ נתגלו בו ראשי אברים – here we are speaking about [the case] that he (i.e., the Kohen) did not manage to declare him a certified leper until it had erupted/blossomed throughout all of his body and uncovered the tips of the limbs, that Rabbi Yishmael compared him to someone who comes in totally white at the outset except for the tips of the limbs, which requires isolation/quarantine/being shut up. But Rabbi Eleazar ben Azariah holds that since whereas it did not erupt/blossom, he (i.e., the Kohen) would declare him to be a certified leper, his law is like a small bright white spot on the skin which has on it quick flesh, for if it had erupted on his entire body and afterwards, the tips of the limbs were uncovered/revealed, he would be impure. And there is more to explain, that even if he had declared him a certified leper with quick flesh and it had erupted throughout all of the body and he had purified him, as Rabbi Yishmael holds, that one does not defile with the reappearance of the tops of the limbs, that one defiles with the reappearance of the tops of the limbs that is after the eruption/blossoming [which causes the leper to be declared clean], where he is pure in the practice of eruption/blossoming, but here, it is not the practice of eruption/blossoming where he is pure, for had there been a diminution of quick flesh from like the size of a lentil, he would be pure.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:10:1)


**מִי שֶׁהָיָה מֻחְלָט.** בְּשֵׂעָר לָבָן אוֹ בְמִחְיָה אוֹ בְּפִשְׂיוֹן:


מי שהיה מוחלט – with white hair or quick flesh or spreading.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:10:2)


**לֹא הִסְפִּיק לְהַרְאוֹת לַכֹּהֵן.** שֶׁסִּימָנֵי טֻמְאָה הָלְכוּ:


לא הספיק להראות לכהן – that the signs/tokens of uncleanness/impurity went away.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:10:3)


**שֶׁאִלּוּ הֶרְאָה לַכֹּהֵן.** כְּשֶׁהָלְכוּ סִימָנֵי טֻמְאָה הָיָה מְטַהֵר, וְאִם אַחַר כֵּן הָיָה פוֹרֵחַ, הָיָה טָמֵא, דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ פּוֹרֵחַ מִן הַטָּהוֹר, אֲבָל הַשְׁתָּא הָוֵי לֵיהּ פּוֹרֵחַ מִן הַטָּמֵא, וְטָהוֹר:


שאילו הראה לכהן – that the signs/tokens of impurity had gone away, he (i.e., the Kohen) would have purified hi, but if after that it flowered/erupted, he was impure, that he would be flowering from a state of being clean/pure. But now, it is for him a flowering from a state of being impure, and he is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:10:4)


**בַּהֶרֶת וְאֵין בָּהּ כְּלוּם.** אֵין בּוֹ סִימָן מִסִּימָנֵי טֻמְאָה:


בהרת ואין בה כלום – that there is no sign/token from the signs/tokens of impurity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 8:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/8:10:5)


**עַד שֶׁפָּרְחָה בְכֻלּוֹ טָמֵא.** כְּלוֹמַר, טָעוּן הֶסְגֵּר כְּבָא כֻלּוֹ לָבָן בַּתְּחִלָּה. וְאִם מִתְּחִלָּה קֹדֶם שֶׁפָּרְחָה הָרְאָה אֶל הַכֹּהֵן הָיָה מַסְגִּיר, וּבְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ כְּשֶׁפָּרְחָה, הָוֵי לֵיהּ פּוֹרֵחַ מִתּוֹךְ הֶסְגֵּר וְטָהוֹר:


עד שפרחה בכולו טמא – meaning to say, he is required to be in isolation/shut-up/quarantine like one who comes [to the Kohen] totally white at the outset. But if from the outset, prior to it erupting/flowering, he showed it to the Kohen, he would be shut up/put in isolation/quarantine, and at the end of the week when it flowered/erupted, he would be like someone who had a white eruption which causes the leper to be declared clean from within isolation and would be pure.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:1:1)


**הַשְּׁחִין וְהַמִּכְוָה מִטַּמְּאִין בְּשָׁבוּעַ אֶחָד.** אִם נוֹלַד בִּמְקוֹמָן בַּהֶרֶת, מַסְגִּירָן שָׁבוּעַ אֶחָד, וּלְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ אִם יֵשׁ בּוֹ שֵׂעָר לָבָן אוֹ פִשְׂיוֹן, מַחְלִיטוֹ. וְאִם לָאו, פּוֹטְרוֹ. אֲבָל בְּמִחְיָה אֵין מִטַּמְּאִין, דְּמִחְיַת בָּשָׂר חַי בָּעִינַן, וְאֵין זֶה בָּשָׂר חַי, כֵּיוָן דִּמְקוֹמוֹ שְׁחִין וּמִכְוָה:


השחין והמכוה מיטמאין בשבוע אחד – if in their place (i.e., the boil or the burning) was born/originated the bright white spot on the skin, we shut him up/quarantine/isolate him and at the end of one week if there is white hair or spreading, he (i.e., the Kohen) declares him to be a certified leper, and if not, he releases him. But with quick flesh he is not defiled, for quick flesh requires live flesh, and this is not live flesh since in its place is boils and burning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:1:2)


**בְּגֶפֶת.** פְּסֹלֶת שֶׁל זֵיתִים, וְהוּא חַם מֵחֲמַת עַצְמוֹ. וְכֵן חַמֵּי טְבֶרְיָא מֵחֲמַת מְקוֹרָן הֵן חַמִּים וְלֹא מֵחֲמַת הָאוּר:


גפת (a pressed hard mass, peat, turf)- refuse of olives, and it is hot on account of itself. And similarly, the hot springs of Tiberias through the heat of their source they are hot and not in consequence of the fire.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:1:3)


**כֹּל שֶׁאֵינוֹ מֵחֲמַת הָאֵשׁ זֶהוּ שְׁחִין.** לַאֲתוֹיֵי אֲבָר מֵעִקָּרוֹ:


כל שאינו מחמת האש זהו שחין – to include the lead directly from the mine (hot – see Tractate Hullin 8a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:1:4)


**רֶמֶץ.** אֵפֶר כִּירָה, וְחַם מֵחֲמַת הָאוּר:


רמץ (hot ashes, embers) – the ashes of a portable stove on feet (with caves for two pots). And it is hot on account of the fire.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:1:5)


**כֹּל שֶׁהוּא מֵחֲמַת הָאֵשׁ.** לַאֲתוֹיֵי חַמֵּי הָאוּר:


כל שהוא מחמת האש – and it is hot from the effect of/in consequence of the fire. כל שהוא מחמת האש – to include the heat of the fire.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:2:1)


**אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין.** חֲצִי גְרִיס שְׁחִין וַחֲצִי גְרִיס מִכְוָה, אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין לְכַגְּרִיס:


אין מצטרפין – one-half a split [Cilician] bean of boils with one-half a split [Cilician] bean of burning do not combine [to make] a complete split [Cilician] bean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:2:2)


**וְאֵין פּוֹשִׂין מִזֶּה לָזֶה.** דְּאִם שְׁחִין וּמִכְוָה זֶה אֵצֶל זֶה, וּבְאֶחָד מֵהֶן בַּהֶרֶת כַּגְּרִיס, וְהִסְגִּירָהּ, וּבְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ פָּשְׂתָה לַחֲבֵרוֹ אוֹ לְעוֹר הַבָּשָׂר, אֵין מַחְלִיטוֹ. וְכֵן אִם הַבַּהֶרֶת בְּעוֹר הַבָּשָׂר וּפָשְׂתָה לַשְּׁחִין אוֹ לַמִּכְוָה, לֹא חָשׁוּב פִּשְׂיוֹן:


ואין פושין מזה וזה – that if a boil and a burning are one next to the other, and in one of them is a bright white spot on the skin the size of a split [Cilician] bean, and he was shut up/isolated/quarantined, and at the end of the week, it spread to its neighbor or on the skin of the flesh, we don’t declare him a certified leper. And similarly, if the bright white spot was on the skin of the flesh and spread to the boil or to the burn, it is not considered spreading.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:2:3)


**הָיוּ מוֹרְדִין.** שֶׁלֹּא נִתְרַפְּאוּ יָפֶה וְלֹא נִקְרַם הָעוֹר וַעֲדַיִן מוֹצִיאִין לֵחָה, אֵין מְטַמְּאִין בַּנְּגָעִים, דִּכְתִיב [וַיִּקְרָא יג] שְׁחִין וְנִרְפָּא, וּבְמִכְוָה כְתִיב [שָׁם] וְהָיְתָה מִחְיַת הַמִּכְוָה:


היו מורדין – that they didn’t heal properly and the skin did not have a scab on it, and it still produces secretion, we don’t defile it through plagues, as it is written (Leviticus 13:18): “When an inflammation (i.e., boil) appears on the skin of one’s body and it heals.,” but with a burning, it is written (Leviticus 13:24): "והיתה מחית המכוה"/ “and the patch from the burn is a dis-coloration, [either white streaked with rea, or white.]”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:2:4)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה מְקוֹמוֹ צַלֶּקֶת.** שֶׁנִּכָּר שֶׁהָיָה שָׁם שְׁחִין אוֹ מִכְוָה וְאֵין שָׁוֶה לִשְׁאָר עוֹר בָּשָׂר, אַף עַל פִּי כֵן נִדּוֹן כְּעוֹר הַבָּשָׂר, מֵאַחַר שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה קְרוּם חָזָק:


אע"פ שנעשה מקומו צלקת (even though spot became the rough, scabby surface/scar) – that it is recognized that there was a boil there or a burn and it is not equivalent to the rest of the skin of the flesh, nevertheless it is judged like the skin of the flesh, for after it became a strong scab.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:1)


**אָמְרוּ לוֹ לָמָּה.** יַסְגִּיר הָא אִי אֶפְשָׁר לְהִוָּלֵד לוֹ סִימַן טֻמְאָה:


אמרו לו למה – [why] should he be shut up/put in quarantine/isolated, but it is impossible for him to give rise to a sign/token of impurity?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:2)


**לְגַדֵּל שֵׂעָר אֵין רְאוּיָה.** דְּכַף הַיָּד אֵינוֹ מְגַדֵּל שֵׂעָר לְעוֹלָם:


לגדל שער אין ראויה – for the palm of the hand one never grows hair ever.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:3)


**לְפִשְׂיוֹן אֵינָהּ פּוֹשָׂה.** דִּשְׁחִין וּמִכְוָה אֵין פּוֹשִׂין לְעוֹר הַבָּשָׂר:


לפשיון אינה פושה – for boils and burning do not spread on the skin of the flesh (as we saw in the previous Mishnah).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:4)


**לְמִחְיָה אֵינָהּ מִטַּמָּא.** דְּהָא תְנַן בִּשְׁנֵי סִימָנִים בְּשֵׂעָר לָבָן וּבְפִשְׂיוֹן, וְלֹא בְמִחְיָה:


ולמחיה אינה מטמא – for it is taught in the Mishnah (see Mishnah 1 of this chapter) that with two signs/tokens – with white hair and spreading but not with quick flesh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:5)


**שֶׁמָּא תִכְנֹס.** כַּגְּרִיס, וּכְשֶׁיִּרְאֶנָּה הַכֹּהֵן בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ יִפְטֹר אוֹתוֹ, וּלְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר תַּחֲזֹר וְתִפְשֶׂה בְתוֹךְ הַשְּׁחִין שֶׁמְּקוֹמוֹ כַּסֶּלַע, וְיַחְלִיט:


שמא תכנוס (perhaps it will contract) – like a split [Cilician] bean, and when the Kohen will see it at the end of the week he will release him, but after the release, it will return and spread within the boil whose place is like a Sela and he will declare him to be a certified leper.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:6)


**אָמְרוּ לוֹ הֲרֵי מְקוֹמָהּ כַּגְּרִיס.** כְּלוֹמַר, וְאִם הָיָה מְקוֹם הַצָּרֶבֶת כַּגְּרִיס בִּלְבַד וְהַבַּהֶרֶת בְּתוֹכָהּ, לָמָּה יַסְגִּיר, וַהֲלֹא אֵין שַׁיָּךְ לוֹמַר כָּאן שֶׁמָּא תִכְנֹס וְתִפְשֶׂה:


אמרו לו הרי מקומה כגריס – meaning to say, that if it the place of the scar/shriveled surface was like a split [Cilician] bean alone and the bright white spot on the skin was in it, why would he (i.e., the Kohen) isolate/quarantine him, but does it not belong to state here that perhaps it will contract and then spread?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:7)


**אָמַר לָהֶן לֹא שָׁמַעְתִּי.** כְּלוֹמַר גַּם זֶה יַסְגִּיר, אֲבָל לֹא שָׁמַעְתִּי בוֹ טַעַם:


אמר להן לא שמעתי – meaning to say that he (i.e., the Kohen) should also shut him up/isolate him/quarantine him, but I did not hear the reason regarding it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:8)


**אֲלַמֵּד בּוֹ.** תַּרְשֵׁנִי שֶׁאֲלַמֵּד בְּדִין זֶה דָּבָר:


אלמד בו – permit me that I will teach a thing regarding this law.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:9)


**אִם לְקַיֵּם דִּבְרֵי חֲכָמִים הֵן.** אִם תִּמְצָא טַעַם לְקַיֵּם דִּבְרֵי חֲכָמִים שֶׁאָמְרוּ שֶׁטָּעוּן הֶסְגֵּר, אֱמֹר, אֲבָל לְלַמֵּד שֶׁאֵינוֹ טָעוּן הֶסְגֵּר לֹא תֹאמַר, שֶׁאֵינִי מַנִּיחַ מַה שֶּׁקִּבַּלְתִּי מֵרַבּוֹתַי וְשׁוֹמֵעַ מִמְּךָ:


אם לקיים דברי חכמים הן – if you find a reason to uphold the words of the Sages that stated that it requires isolation/quarantine, say it, but to teach that it doesn’t require isolation/quarantine, do not say it, for I do not set aside what I received from my Rabbis and listen from you.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 9:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/9:3:10)


**שֶׁמָּא יִוָּלֵד לוֹ שְׁחִין אַחֵר חוּצָה לוֹ וְיִפְשֶׂה לְתוֹכוֹ.** שֶׁשְּׁחִין פּוֹשֶׂה לִשְׁחִין. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


שמא יוולד לו שחין אחר חוצה לו ויפשה לתוכו – that boils spread into boils. And this is the Halakha.



## Chapter 10


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:1:1)


**הַנְּתָקִים.** נְגָעִים שֶׁבָּרֹאשׁ אוֹ בַזָּקָן, קְרוּיִין נְתָקִים, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהַשֵּׂעָר נִתָּק וְנוֹפֵל וְנוֹשֵׁר מֵחֲמַת הַנֶּגַע. וְהַנְּתָקִים אֵין מִטַּמְּאִין בְּאַרְבָּעָה מַרְאוֹת כְּנֶגַע שֶׁבִּמְקוֹם בָּשָׂר, אֶלָּא מִטַּמְּאִים בְּכָל מַרְאוֹת:


הנתקים (bald, blanched spot) – plagues that are in the head or in the beard are called נתקים, as a result that the hair is torn loose and falls and drops off on account of the plague. But the bald, blanched spots do not become defiled in the four appearances like a plague in the place of flesh, bur become defiled through all of the appearances.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:1:2)


**בְּשֵׂעָר צָהֹב.** דּוֹמֶה לְתַבְנִית זָהָב:


בשער צהוב – similar to the golden shape.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:1:3)


**דַּק לָקוּי קָצָר.** מַה שֶּׁאָמְרָה תוֹרָה וּבוֹ שֵׂעָר צָהֹב דַּק, מַשְׁמָעוּתוֹ לָקוּי קָצָר:


דק לקוי קצר (thin - so diseased -sparse that it is short hair) – what the Torah stated (Leviticus 13:30): “[The priest shall examine the affection. If it appears to go deeper than the skin] and here is thin yellow hair in it, [the priest shall pronounce him impure; it is a scall, a scaly eruption in the hair or beard],” the implication of לקוי/diseased – sparse – is that it is short.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:1:4)


**אֲפִלּוּ אָרֹךְ.** שֶׁאֵין מַשְׁמָעוּת דַּק אֶלָּא רָקִיק:


אפילו ארוך – that its implication is not thin, but rather beaten/flattened.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:1:5)


**מָה הַלָּשׁוֹן אוֹמְרִים.** מִלְּשׁוֹן בְּנֵי אָדָם אַתָּה לָמֵד. כְּשֶׁאוֹמְרִים קָנֶה זֶה דַּק, מַה מַּשְׁמָעוֹ, בֵּין לָקוּי קָצָר בֵּין לָקוּי אָרֹךְ. וְאִית סְפָרִים דְּגָרְסֵי, וְלֹא לָקוּי אָרֹךְ, בִּתְמִיהָה, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁמָּא לָקוּי קָצָר וְלֹא לָקוּי אָרֹךְ:


מה הלשון אומרים – you learn from the language usage of people. When they say, “this reed is thin,” what is its implication? Whether it is diseased that it is short or whether it is diseased that it is long. But there are books that have the reading: "ולא לקוי ארוך"/but it is not diseased that it is long, in astonishment, meaning to say, that perhaps it is so diseased that it is short, but not so diseased that it is long.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:1:6)


**נִלְמַד מִן הַשֵּׂעָר.** דֶּרֶךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם כְּשֶׁאוֹמְרִים עַל הַשֵּׂעָר שֶׁהוּא דַק, עַל הַלָּקוּי קָצָר אוֹמְרִים, וְלֹא עַל הַלָּקוּי הָאָרֹךְ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


נלמוד מן השער – it is the manner of people when they speak about the hair that it is thin, they say that when it is diseased, it is short, but not on the diseased [hair] that it is long. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiba.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:2:1)


**מְכֻנָּס.** שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד:


מכונס (close together) – two hairs in one place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:2:2)


**מְפֻזָּר.** אַחַת בְּמִזְרַח הַנֶּגַע וְאַחַת בְּמַעֲרָבוֹ:


מפוזר – one in the eastern part of the plague and one its western part [of the plague].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:2:3)


**מְבֻצָּר.** שֶׁהַשֵּׂעָר בְּאֶמְצַע הַנֶּתֶק, כְּמִבְצָר שֶׁהוּא בְאֶמְצַע הָעִיר:


מבוצר (surrounded -of the hair in the flesh affected by the eruption) – that the hair in the middle of the bald, blanched spot on the head, like a fortress that is in the middle of the city.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:2:4)


**הָפוּךְ.** שֶׁנֶּהְפַּךְ הַשֵּׂעָר לְצָהֹב מֵחֲמַת הַנֶּתֶק, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהַנֶּתֶק קָדַם לַשֵּׂעָר [צָהֹב]:


הפוך – that the hair was turned to yellow because of the bald, blanched spot in the head, as for example, that the bald, blanched spot in the head preceded the hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:2:5)


**שֶׁאֵינוֹ הָפוּךְ.** שֶׁקָּדַם שֵׂעָר צָהֹב לַנֶּתֶק:


ושאינו הפוך – that the yellow/golden hair preceded the bald, blanched spot in the head.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:2:6)


**שֶׁאֵין שֵׂעָר אַחֵר מַצִּיל מִיָּדוֹ.** שֶׁאִם יֵשׁ בַּנֶּגַע שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת לְבָנוֹת, אֲפִלּוּ מָלֵא שְׂעָרוֹת שְׁחֹרוֹת אֵינָן מַצִּילוֹת (על השתי) שְׂעָרוֹת לְבָנוֹת שֶׁלֹּא יְטַמְּאוּ. אֲבָל שֵׂעָר צָהֹב, אִם יֵשׁ עִמּוֹ [שְׁתֵּי] שְׂעָרוֹת שְׁחֹרוֹת אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


שאין שער אחד מציל מידו – that if there is in the plague two white hairs, even filled with black hairs, they don’t protect (on the two) white hairs that they should not be defiled. But the yellow/golden hair, if there is with it [two] black hairs, it does not defile. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:1)


**הַצּוֹמֵחַ מַצִּיל.** שֵׂעָר שָׁחֹר שֶׁצָּמַח בַּנֶּתֶק:


הצומח – black hair that grew in the bald, blanched spot on the head.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:2)


**מִיַּד שֵׂעָר צָהֹב וּמִיַּד פִּשְׂיוֹן.** שֶׁאִם הֻחְלַט בְּשֵׂעָר צָהֹב אוֹ בְּפִשְׂיוֹן, וְצָמְחוּ בוֹ שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת שְׁחֹרוֹת, טָהוֹר:


מציל מיד שער צהוב ומיד פשיון – that if he had been declared a certified leper through golden hair or with spreading, and two black hairs grew, he is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:3)


**בֵּין מְכֻנָּס.** דִּשְׁתֵּיהֶן בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד:


בין מכונס – that both of them are in one place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:4)


**בֵּין מְפֻזָּר.** אַחַת לְמִזְרַח הַנֶּגַע וְאַחַת לְמַעֲרָבוֹ:


בין מפוזר – one to the east of the plague and one to the west [of the plague].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:5)


**וְהַמְשֹׁאָר.** שֶׁקָּדְמוּ שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת שְׁחֹרוֹת הַלָּלוּ לַנֶּתֶק, כְּגוֹן שֶׁכְּשֶׁנִּתַּק שְׂעַר רֹאשׁוֹ אוֹ זְקָנוֹ כַגְּרִיס נִשְׁאֲרוּ בְתוֹךְ הַנֶּתֶק שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת שְׁחֹרוֹת:


והמשואר – that the two black hairs preceded those for the bald, blanched spot on the head, as for example, if the hair of his head or his beard had been torn loose like a split [Cilician] bean, there remained within the bald, blanched spot two black hairs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:6)


**וּמְבֻצָּר.** אִם הֵן מְבֻצָּרוֹת בְּאֶמְצַע הַנֶּתֶק, מַצִּילוֹת. אֲבָל עוֹמְדוֹת מִן הַצַּד, אֵין מַצִּילוֹת:


מבוצר (of the hair in the flesh affected by the eruption – are cut off) – if they are cut off in the middle of the bald, blanched spot on the head, they protect. But if they stand from the side, they do not protect.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:7)


**עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא רָחוֹק מִן הַקָּמָה.** עַד שֶׁיְּהוּ שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת הַלָּלוּ רְחוֹקוֹת מִן הַשְּׂעָרוֹת הַמַּקִּיפוֹת אֶת הַנֶּתֶק כְּשִׁעוּר שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת. וְהַשְּׂעָרוֹת שֶׁסְּבִיב הַנֶּתֶק הֵן קְרוּיִין קָמָה. וּכְשֶׁשְּׂעָרוֹת הַלָּלוּ רְחוֹקוֹת מִן הַקָּמָה מְקוֹם שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת, נֶחְשָׁבוֹת כִּמְבֻצָּרוֹת בְּתוֹךְ הַנֶּגַע:


עד שיהא רחוק מן הקמה – until there would be these two hairs [of the same color] far from the hairs that surround the bald, blanched spot on the head (or beard) like the measure of two hairs. But the hairs that are around the bald, blanched spot [on the head] are called קמה/standing, and when these hairs are two hairs distant from the standing hairs, they are considered like they are an eruption surrounded with sound flesh within the plague.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:3:8)
