**אַחַת צְהֻבָּה וְאַחַת שְׁחֹרָה.** בִּצְהֻבָּה שֶׁקָּדְמָה לַנֶּתֶק דְּלֹא הַוְיָא סִימַן טֻמְאָה, אִצְטְרִיךְ לֵיהּ לְמֵימַר דְּאֵין מִצְטָרֶפֶת עִם הַשְּׁחֹרָה לְהַצִּיל מִיַּד שֵׂעָר צָהֹב וּמִיַּד הַפִּשְׂיוֹן. וּסְתָם מַתְנִיתִין כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בֶּן יַעֲקֹב דְּאָמַר בְּסָמוּךְ לְקַמָּן דְּשֵׂעָר צָהֹב שֶׁקָּדַם לַנֶּתֶק לֹא מְטַמֵּא וְלֹא מַצִּיל:


אחת צהובה ואחת שחורה – with the golden [hair] that preceded the bald, blanched spot, there wasn’t a token/sign of defilement, it is necessary for him (i.e., the Tanna/teacher of the Mishnah) to state that they don’t combine with the black hair to protect from the golden hair and from the spreading. But our anonymous Mishnah is according to Rabbi Eliezer ben Yaakov who stated nearby ahead (see the next Mishnah), that the golden hair that preceded the bald, blanched spot on the head does not defile and it does not protect.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:4:1)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה מְטַמֵּא.** כְּדִתְנַן לְעֵיל דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה מְטַמֵּא הָפוּךְ וְלֹא הָפוּךְ:


רבי יהודה מטמא – as it is taught in the Mishnah above (i.e., Mishnah 2 of this chapter) that Rabbi Yehuda defiles already turned (i.e., its color) or not turned.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:4:2)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בֶּן יַעֲקֹב אוֹמֵר לֹא מְטַמֵּא וְלֹא מַצִּיל.** רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בֶּן יַעֲקֹב וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן לְפָרוֹשֵׁי מִלְּתָא דְּתַנָּא קַמָּא דְּאָמַר טָהוֹר קָאָתוּ, רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בֶּן יַעֲקֹב סָבַר, טָהוֹר דְּאָמַר תַּנָּא קַמָּא לוֹמַר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא, אֲבָל אֵינוֹ מַצִּיל. וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן סָבַר, טָהוֹר וּמַצִּיל קָאָמַר. וּלְעֵיל פָּסְקִינַן דַּהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ר' אליעזר בן יעקב אומר לא מטמא ולא מציל – Rabbi Eliezer ben Yaakov and Rabbi Shimon explain the matter of the first Tanna/teacher (in the second Mishnah) that they brought it pure, Rabbi Eliezer ben Yaakov holds that it is pure, for the first Tanna/teacher to state that it doesn’t defile, but it doesn’t protect. But Rabbi Shimon holds that it is pure and protects as he states, but above, we made the legal decision that the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:5:1)


**כֵּיצַד מְגַלְּחִין אֶת הַנָּתֶק.** בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן (להסגרו) שֶׁלֹּא פָשָׂה וְלֹא נוֹלַד בּוֹ שֵׂעָר צָהֹב (כתיב והתגלח):


כיצד מגלחין את הנתק – at the end of the first week (to his isolation/being shut up/quarantined) that it did not spread and did not give rise to golden hair (as it is written – Leviticus 13:33 – “the person with the scall shall shave himself [but without shaving the scall; the priest shall isolate him for another seven days]).”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:5:2)


**הָיָה מְגַלֵּחַ חוּצָה לוֹ.** מְגַלֵּחַ סָבִיב לַנֶּתֶק,


היה מגלח חוצה לו – he shaves around the bald, blanched spot [on the head or in the beard].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:5:3)


**וּמַנִּיחַ שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) וְאֶת הַנֶּתֶק לֹא יְגַלֵּחַ, וְכִי מַה יֵּשׁ בּוֹ, וַהֲלֹא כְבָר נִתַּק הַשֵּׂעָר וְנָפַל, אֶלָּא סָמוּךְ לַנֶּתֶק לֹא יְגַלֵּחַ, שֶׁמְּשַׁיֵּר סָבִיב לַנֶּתֶק שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת מִכָּל צַד, נִמְצָא הַנֶּתֶק מֻקָּף שְׂעָרוֹת כַּעֲטָרָה:


ומניח שתי שערות – as it is written (Leviticus 13:33): "והתגלח ואת-הנתק לא יגלח"/ “the person with he scall shall shave himself, but without shaving the scall,” for what does he have in it? For hasn’t the hair already been torn loose and fallen out? But rather, near the bald, blanched spot (i.e., the “scall”) he doesn’t shave, that he leaves around the bald, blanched spot two hairs from every side, it is found that the bald, blanched spot (i.e., the scall) is surround by hair like a crown.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:5:4)


**הֶחְלִיטוֹ בְשֵׂעָר צָהֹב.** וְאַחַר שֶׁהֻחְלַט בּוֹ הָלַךְ שֵׂעָר צָהֹב וְאַחֲרֵי כֵן חָזַר:


החליטו בשער צהוב – and after he was declared a certified leper through it, the golden hair went away and after that came back.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:5:5)


**וְכֵן בְּפִשְׂיוֹן.** שֶׁלֹּא חָזַר שֵׂעָר צָהֹב אֶלָּא שֶׁפָּשָׂה. בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ הֲרֵי הוּא מֻחְלָט כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה:


וכן בפשיון – that the golden hair did not return but rather that it spread, whether this (i.e., golden hair) or that (i.e., spreading), he is a certified leper like he was.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:5:6)


**בַּתְּחִלָּה בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן.** כְּלוֹמַר, דִּין זֶה שֶׁאָמַרְנוּ נוֹהֵג בֵּין שֶׁהֶחְלִיטוֹ בְשֵׂעָר צָהֹב בַּתְּחִלָּה כְּשֶׁהוּבָא אֶל הַכֹּהֵן, בֵּין שֶׁהֶחְלִיטוֹ בְשֵׂעָר צָהֹב בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן, אוֹ בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי, אוֹ לְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר, כְּגוֹן שֶׁעָמַד בְּעֵינָיו שְׁנֵי שָׁבוּעוֹת וּפְטָרוֹ וּלְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר נוֹלַד שֵׂעָר צָהֹב וְהֶחְלִיטוֹ. וּבְכֻלְּהוּ הָלַךְ שֵׂעָר צָהֹב וְחָזַר, אוֹ שֶׁלֹּא חָזַר אֶלָּא שֶׁפָּשָׂה הַנֶּתֶק, הֲרֵי הוּא כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה:


בתחלה בסוף שבוע ראשון – meaning to say, this law that we stated is in effect whether he was declared a certified leper with the golden hair when he was brought to the Kohen, whether he was declared to be a certified leper with golden hair at the end of the first week, or at the end of he second week, or after the release, as for example, it remained unchanged for two weeks and he was released, but after the release, two yellow hairs appeared and he was declared a certified leper. But in all of them, [if] the golden hair went away and then returned, or that it didn’t return but the scall/bald, blanched spot, he is as he was.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:5:7)


**הֶחְלִיטוֹ בְּפִשְׂיוֹן.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפָרֵשׁ בְּהֶחְלִיטוֹ בְּשֵׂעָר צָהֹב כָּךְ יֵשׁ לְפָרֵשׁ הֶחְלִיטוֹ בְּפִשְׂיוֹן. וְהָכָא בְּפִשְׂיוֹן לֹא גָרְסִינַן בַּתְּחִלָּה, שֶׁאֵין הַפִּשְׂיוֹן מְטַמֵּא אֶלָּא אַחַר הֶסְגֵּר:


החליטו בפשיון – as it was explained that he was declared a certified leper with the golden hair, such one explains his being declared a certified leper through spreading. But here, with spreading, we don’t have this reading at the beginning, for the spreading does not defile other than after isolation/being shut up.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:1)


**שְׁנֵי נְתָקִים זֶה בְצַד זֶה.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּכָל אֶחָד כַּגְּרִיס הַקִּלְקִי, דְּבִנְתָקִים נַמִּי בָּעִינַן כַּגְּרִיס, דִּכְתִיב נֶגַע הַנֶּתֶק, הִקִּישׁ נֶגַע לְנֶתֶק, מָה הַנֶּגַע בְּכַגְּרִיס, אַף נֶתֶק בְּכַגְּרִיס. וְאִם שְׁנֵי הַנְּתָקִים מֻנָּחִים זֶה אֵצֶל זֶה וְיֵשׁ שִׁיטַת שֵׂעָר מַפְסֶקֶת בֵּין שְׁנֵי הַנְּתָקִים וְחוֹלֶקֶת אוֹתָם לִשְׁנַיִם כִּמְחִצָּה הַמַּפְסֶקֶת:


שני נתקים זה בצד זה – and there is in each of them a like a Cilician split bean. For with bald, blanched spots (i.e., scalls), we require like the size of a split [Cilician] bean, as it is written (Leviticus 13:31): “But if the priest finds the scall affection”/נגע הנתק – and an analogy is made between נגע/plague and נתק/bald, blanched spot on the head (or on the beard), just as the plague is like the size of a split [Cilician] bean, so even the bald, blanched spot on the head (or on the beard) is like the size of a split [Cilician] bean. But if two bald, blanched spots/scalls are placed one next to the other and there is a line of hair that separating the two bald, blanched spots/scalls, and divides it into two like a partition that separates.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:2)


**נִפְרַץ.** שֵׂעָר שֶׁל אוֹתָהּ הַשִּׁיטָה:


נפרץ – the hair of that line/row [that separates them].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:3)


**בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד.** בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁל הֶסְגֵּר אוֹ בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי אוֹ לְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר:


במקום אחד – at the end of the first week of isolation/being shut up/quarantine or at the end of the second week or after the release.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:4)


**טָמֵא.** שֶׁהֲרֵי פָשָׂה הַנֶּתֶק:


טמא – for the bald, blanched spot/scall had spread.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:5)


**בִּשְׁנֵי מְקוֹמוֹת.** שֶׁאוֹתָהּ שִׁיטָה נִפְרְצָה לְמַטָּה וּלְמַעְלָה וְנִשְׁאֲרוּ מֵאוֹתָהּ שִׁיטָה שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת בָּאֶמְצַע:


בשני מקומות – that line/row that separated below and above and remained from that line/row two hairs in the middle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:6)


**טָהוֹר.** דִּמְבֻצָּרוֹת נִינְהוּ וּמַצִּילוֹת:


טהור – for they (i.e., of the hair affected by the eruption) are surrounded and protected.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:7)


**כַּמָּה תְהֵא הַפִּרְצָה.** אִם בִּשְׁנֵי מְקוֹמוֹת נִפְרַץ, הָוֵי שִׁעוּר הַפִּרְצָה מְקוֹם שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת, דִּבְהָכִי חֲשִׁיבֵי הַשְּׂעָרוֹת שֶׁבָּאֶמְצַע מְבֻצָּרוֹת:


כמה תהא הפרצה – if in the two places they were separated, the measurement of the gap in the place of the two hairs, and thusly the hairs are considered be surrounded.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:6:8)


**וְאִם בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד נִפְרַץ טָמֵא.** אֲפִלּוּ נִפְרַץ כַּגְּרִיס, טָמֵא, שֶׁאֵין שֵׂעָר שָׁחֹר מְבֻצָּר וְכָנוּס בְּתוֹךְ הַנֶּגַע, כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא נִפְרַץ בִּשְׁנֵי מְקוֹמוֹת:


ואם במקום אחד נפרצו טמא – even if it was severed/broken off like the size of a split [Cilician] bean, it is impure, for the black hair is not surrounded and gathered in within the plague, since it is not severed in -two places.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:7:1)


**זֶה לִפְנִים מִזֶּה.** כְּגוֹן נֶתֶק כַּגְּרִיס הַקִּלְקִי מְרֻבָּע, וְשִׁיטָה שֶׁל שֵׂעָר מַקִּיפוֹ סָבִיב, וְחוּץ לְאוֹתָהּ שִׁיטָה מַקִּיף נֶתֶק כַּגְּרִיס כָּל סְבִיבוֹתֶיהָ, דְּהַיְנוּ רְצוּעָה שֶׁל נֶתֶק בְּרֹחַב כָּעֲדָשָׁה מַקֶּפֶת אֶת הַשִּׁיטָה שֶׁל שֵׂעָר, שֶׁזֶהוּ שִׁעוּר הַנֶּתֶק הַמַּקִּיף הַשְּׂעָרוֹת הַמְבֻצָּרוֹת:


זה לפנים מזה – as for example, if a bald, blanched spot/scall in the head [or in the beard] is like a split Cilician bean squared, and a row/line of hair completely surrounds it, except for that row/line that surrounds the bald, blanched spot/scall like [the size of] a split [Cilician] bean all around it, that, a strip of scall the width of a lentil surrounds the row of hair, and this is the measurement of the scall that surrounds the hair in the flesh affected by the eruption.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:7:2)


**נִפְרַץ מִמָּקוֹם אֶחָד טָמֵא.** הַפְּנִימִי. שֶׁהֲרֵי שֵׂעָר שָׁחֹר בְּצִדּוֹ וְאֵינוֹ מְבֻצָּר לְתוֹכוֹ. אֲבָל הַחִיצוֹן טָהוֹר, דְּהָא שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת שְׁחֹרוֹת מְבֻצָּרוֹת לְתוֹכוֹ:


נפרץ ממקום אחד טמא – the inner part, for the black hair at its side and which is not surrounded inside it. But the outer part is pure, for there are black hairs that are surrounded inside it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:7:3)


**מִשְּׁנֵי מְקוֹמוֹת טָהוֹר.** הַפְּנִימִי, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן הַחִיצוֹן. שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם נֶתֶק אֶחָד וּבְתוֹכָן שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת שְׁחֹרוֹת מְבֻצָּרוֹת:


משתי מקומות טהור – the inside and all the more so, the outside, that both of them were made into one scall, and within them are two black hairs which are surrounded.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:7:4)


**כַּמָּה תְהֵא הַפִּרְצָה.** מִשְּׁנֵי מְקוֹמוֹת וִיהֵא טָהוֹר, מְקוֹם שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת:


כמה תהא הפרצה – from the two places and it will be pure, the place of the two hairs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:7:5)


**נִפְרַץ מִמָּקוֹם אֶחָד כַּגְּרִיס טָהוֹר.** וְהָא דְַאֲמַרַן נִפְרַץ בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד טָמֵא, הַיְנוּ בְּפָחוֹת מִכַּגְּרִיס. אֲבָל מֵאַחַר דְּנִפְרְצָה כַּגְּרִיס, אֲפִלּוּ מִמָּקוֹם אֶחָד נַעֲשׂוּ שְׁנֵי הַנְּתָקִים אֶחָד, וּשְׂעָרוֹת הַנִּשְׁאָרוֹת הֲרֵי הֵן מְבֻצָּרוֹת:


נפרץ ממקום אחד כגריס טהור – for we have said (in the previous Mishnah), that if it was separated in one place, it is impure, that is, with less than a split [Cilician] bean. But after it was severed/broken off like a split [Cilician] bean, even in one place, the two scalls became one, and the hair that remains are surrounded.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:8:1)


**וּבוֹ שֵׂעָר צָהֹב טָמֵא.** שֵׂעָר צָהֹב מְטַמֵּא בֵּין בַּתְּחִלָּה, בֵּין בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן, בֵּין בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי, בֵּין לְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר:


ובו שער צהוב טמא – golden/yellow hair defiles, whether at the beginning [or] whether at the end of the first week, whether at the end of the second week,[or] whether after the release.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:8:2)


**נוֹלַד בּוֹ שֵׂעָר שָׁחֹר טָהוֹר.** אִי בְצָמַח לְאַחַר הַנֶּתֶק, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָן מְבֻצָּרוֹת. וְאִי בִמְשֹׁאָר, דַּוְקָא בִמְבֻצָּר, דְּרָחוֹק מִקָּמַת הָרֹאשׁ מְקוֹם שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת כְּדַאֲמַרַן:


נולד בו שער שחור טהור – if it (i.e., the two black hairs) grew after the bald, blanched spot in the head [or the beard] even though they are not an eruption surrounded with sound flesh. But if it is a remainder, especially an eruption surrounded with sound flesh, that is distant by two hairs from the standing flesh of the head as we have stated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:8:3)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהָלַךְ לוֹ שֵׂעָר שָׁחֹר טָהוֹר.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּנִטְהַר הַצָּהֹב, תּוּ לֹא מְטַמֵּא אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנָּשַׁר הַשָּׁחֹר. אֲבָל אִם הֶחְלִיטוֹ בְּפִשְׂיוֹן, וּבָא הַשָּׁחֹר וְטָהַר הַפִּשְׂיוֹן וְאַחֲרֵי כֵן הָלַךְ הַשָּׁחֹר, חוֹזֵר הַפִּשְׂיוֹן וּמְטַמֵּא. אוֹ שֶׁפָּשָׂה וְכָנַס וְחָזַר וּפָשָׂה, לֹא אָמְרִינַן כֵּיוָן דְּנִטְהַר הַנֶּתֶק לֹא מְטַמֵּא:


אע"פ שהלך לו שער שחור טהור – for since the golden/yellow [hair] has been purified, it further does not defile, even though the black [hair] fell out, but, if he was declared a certified leper through spreading and the black [hair] came and purified the spreading and afterwards, the black [hair] went away, the spreading returns and defiles, or that it spread and contracted and then once again spread, we don’t say since the bald, blanched spot [in the head] became pure it does not defile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:8:4)


**כָּל נֶתֶק שֶׁטָּהַר שָׁעָה אַחַת.** בֵּין שֶׁהֶחְלִיטוֹ בְּפִסְיוֹן וְכָנַס, אוֹ שֶׁבָּא שֵׂעָר שָׁחֹר וְטִהֲרוֹ, בֵּין הֶחְלִיטוֹ בְשֵׂעָר צָהֹב וּבָא שֵׂעָר שָׁחֹר וְטִהֲרוֹ וְאַחֲרֵי כֵן הָלַךְ הַשָּׁחֹר, אוֹ מַה שֶּׁכָּנַס חָזַר וְנִתַּק, שׁוּב אֵין לְנֶתֶק זֶה טֻמְאָה לְעוֹלָם, אַף עַל גַּב דְּקַיְמוּ סִימָנֵי טֻמְאָה שֶׁלּוֹ כְּגוֹן שֵׂעָר צָהֹב וּפִשְׂיוֹן. וְלֹא מִבַּעְיָא בְהָנֵי דִּכְבָר טָהֲרוּ בְּשֵׂעָר שָׁחֹר, אֶלָּא אֲפִלּוּ פָשָׂה אַחַר שֶׁהָלַךְ הַשָּׁחֹר אוֹ נוֹלַד בּוֹ שֵׂעָר צָהֹב אַחֵר, כֵּיוָן דְּטָהַר הַנֶּתֶק כְּבָר שׁוּב אֵין לוֹ טֻמְאָה:


כל נתק שטהר שעה אחת – whether that he was declared a certified leper through spreading and it contracted, or that black hair came and purified him, whether he was declared a certified leper with golden/yellow hair and black hair came and purified him and afterwards the black [hair] went away, or what that had contracted returned and became a bald, blanched spot, again this bald, blanched spot is not defilement forever, even though that we hold that the tokens/signs of his uncleanness, such as golden/yellow hair and spreading, and we don’t have need with these that already is purified with black hair, but even if it spread after the black went away or that other yellow/golden hair grew in it, since the bald, blanched spot was purified already, it further has no uncleanness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:8:5)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר כָּל שֵׂעָר צָהֹב שֶׁטָּהַר שָׁעָה אַחַת.** כְּגוֹן הַאי דַּהֲוָה קָאֵי בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁבָּא הַשָּׁחֹר, אֵין לוֹ טֻמְאָה לְעוֹלָם. אֲבָל אִם אַחַר שֶׁהָלַךְ הַשָּׁחֹר נוֹלַד בַּנֶּתֶק שֵׂעָר צָהֹב אַחֵר אוֹ פָשָׂה אַחֲרֵי כֵן, טָמֵא, דְּכָל נֶתֶק שֶׁטָּהַר לֹא אָמְרִינַן. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא:


ר' שמעון אומר כל שער צהוב שטהר שעה אחת – as for example, this that refers to at the time when the black [hair] comes, it has no uncleanness forever. But if after the black [hair] came, yellow/golden hair appeared in the bald, blanched spot or it spread afterwards, it is impure, for we don’t say that every bald, blanched spot that is purified. And the Halakha is according to the first Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah].



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:1)


**וְנִתַּק כָּל רֹאשׁוֹ.** שֶׁפָּרַח הַנֶּתֶק בְּכָל הָרֹאשׁ. בֵּין שֶׁהֻחְלַט בְּשֵׂעָר צָהֹב בֵּין שֶׁהֻחְלַט בְּפִשְׂיוֹן:


וניתק כל ראשו – that the bald, blanched spot spread throughout the head, whether that he was certified as a leper with golden hair, whether he was certified as a leper through spreading.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:2)


**טָהוֹר.** דִּבְכֻלְּהוּ פְּרִיחָה מְטַהֶרֶת. וּמִנַּיִן שֶׁהַפְּרִיחָה מְטַהֶרֶת בָּרֹאשׁ, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) וְאִישׁ כִּי יִמָּרֵט רֹאשׁוֹ קֵרֵחַ הוּא טָהוֹר הוּא. בַּזָּקָן מִנַּיִן, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר וְאִישׁ, לְרַבּוֹת אֶת הַזָּקָן. וְהַבָּא כֻלּוֹ נֶתֶק בַּתְּחִלָּה אֵינוֹ טָהוֹר, כְּשֵׁם שֶׁהַבָּא כֻלּוֹ לָבָן בַּתְּחִלָּה אֵינוֹ טָהוֹר בְּבַהֶרֶת:


טהור – that in all of it, spreading all over purifies (see Tractate Negaim, Chapter 7, Mishnah 5 and Chapter 8, Mishnayot 1 and 2). And from where do we learn that spreading throughout purifies in the head, as it is written (Leviticus 13:40): "ואיש כי ימרט ראשו קרח הוא טהור הוא"/ “If a man loses the hair of his head and becomes bald, he is pure.” Regarding the beard, from where do we learn [that the same is the case]? The inference teaches us: "ואיש"/if a man – to include the beard. But if the bald, blanched spot comes throughout at the outset, he is not pure, that just as if it turns all white at the beginning, he is not pure with regard to the bright white spot on the skin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:3)


**אֵין מְעַכְּבִין זֶה אֶת זֶה.** אִם הָיָה בָרֹאשׁ נֶתֶק כַּגְּרִיס וְאַחַר כָּךְ נִתַּק כָּל הָרֹאשׁ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִתַּק הַזָּקָן, טָהוֹר. וְכֵן כְּשֶׁפָּרַח בַּזָּקָן וְלֹא פָרַח בָּרֹאשׁ, טָהוֹר, דְּאֵין מְעַכְּבִין זֶה עַל זֶה:


אין מעכבין זה את זה – if there was a bald, blanched spot on his head like the size of a split [Cilician] bean and afterwards all of his head became torn loose, even though the beard was not torn loose, he is pure. And similarly, when there is white spreading all over in the beard, but it doesn’t spread all white in the head, he is pure, for they do not hinder one another.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:4)


**וְדִין הוּא.** שֶׁיְּהוּ מְעַכְּבִין:


ודין הוא – that they do hinder.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:5)


**שֶׁיֵּשׁ דָּבָר אַחֵר מַפְסִיק בֵּינֵיהֶן.** שֶׁשְּׂעַר הָרֹאשׁ וְהַזָּקָן דְּאֵין מִטַּמְּאִין בְּבַהֶרֶת, מַפְסִיקִין בֵּין עוֹר הַפָּנִים לִשְׁאָר הַגּוּף. וְעוֹר הַפָּנִים וּשְׁאָר הַגּוּף מְעַכְּבִין זֶה עַל זֶה, דְּכָל שֶׁרָאוּי לִטַּמֵּא בְנֶגַע הַבַּהֶרֶת מְעַכֵּב פְּרִיחַת הַבַּהֶרֶת:


שיש דבר אחד מפסיק ביניהן – that the hair of the head and of the beard that they do not become defiled with the bright white spot on the skin that intervenes between the skin of the face to the rest of the body. But the skin of the face and the rest of the body do hinder each other, for all that is appropriate to be defiled through the plague of the bright white spot on the skin hinders the eruption of the bright white spot on the skin which causes the leper to be declared clean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:6)


**אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין זֶה עִם זֶה.** בַּמָּקוֹם שֶׁהָרֹאשׁ וְהַזָּקָן מִתְחַבְּרִים זֶה עִם זֶה, אִם נוֹלַד חֲצִי גְרִיס בָּזֶה וַחֲצִי גְרִיס בָּזֶה, אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין:


אין מצטרפין זה עם זה – in the place that the head and the beard join together. If a half of a split [Cilician] bean was to originate in this one (i.e., the head) and a half of a split [Cilician] bean was to originate in that one, they do not combine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:7)


**וְאֵין פּוֹשִׂין מִזֶּה לָזֶה.** דְּאִם יֵשׁ נֶתֶק כַּגְּרִיס בָּרֹאשׁ סָמוּךְ לַזָּקָן, וּלְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן אוֹ לְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי אוֹ לְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר פָּשָׂה בַזָּקָן, לָא חֲשִׁיב פִּשְׂיוֹן:


ואין פושין מזה לזה – if there is a bald, blanched spot the size of a split [Cilician] bean on the head near/adjacent to the beard, and at the end of the first week or at the end of the second week or after the release, it spread on the beard, it is not considered spreading.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:8)


**מִן הַפֶּרֶק שֶׁל לֶחִי.** הָעֶלְיוֹן. מוֹתֵחַ חוּט מֵאֹזֶן לְאֹזֶן, כָּל שֶׁמִּן הַחוּט וּלְמַעְלָה זֶהוּ הָרֹאשׁ, וְכָל שֶׁמִּן הַחוּט וּלְמַטָּה זֶה הַזָּקָן:


מן הפרק של לחי – [from the joint of the] upper [jawbone]. He pulls/stretches a thread from ear to ear, all that is from the thread and above is the head, and all that is from the thread and below is the beard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:9:9)


**פִּקָּה שֶׁל גַּרְגָּרֶת.** רֹאשׁ הַשִּׁפּוּי כּוֹבַע, וְהַבָּשָׂר הַמַּקִּיף בְּשִׁפּוּי כּוֹבַע מִחוּץ, קָרוּי פִּקָּה:


פיקה של גרגרת (thyroid cartilage/Adam’s apple) – the top of the thyroid cartilage begins to protrude and go downward, and the flesh that surrounds the thyroid cartridge from outside is called פיקה /protruding cartilage.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:1)


**הַקָּרַחַת וְהַגַּבַּחַת.** שְׁתֵּיהֶן בָּרֹאשׁ, אֶלָּא שֶׁמִּשִּׁפּוּעַ קָדְקֹד וְלַאֲחוֹרָיו קָרוּי קָרַחַת, מִשִּׁפּוּעַ קָדְקֹד כְּלַפֵּי פָנָיו קָרוּי גַּבַּחַת, כְּדִתְנַן בְּסָמוּךְ. וְאֵינָם כַּנְּתָקִים, שֶׁהַנֶּתֶק חוֹזֵר וּמְגַדֵּל שֵׂעָר, וְהַקָּרַחַת וְהַגַּבַּחַת אֵינָן חוֹזְרִין וּמְגַדְּלִין שֵׂעָר:


הקרחת והגבחת ( a leprous affection on the back of the head – making it bald -from the crown sliding backwards; leprous affection on the front of the head making it bald- from the crown sliding down forward to where the hair begins) – both of them are in the head, but from where the slanting of the skull and backward is called קדקוד/the skull, opposite his face is called גבחת/the front of the head, as it is taught in the Mishnah nearby (i.e., in this Mishnah. And they are not like bald, blanched spots in the head, for the bald, blanched spots return and grow hair, but the leprous affection on the back of the head and the leprous affection on the front of the head do not return/come back and grow hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:2)


**מִטַּמְּאוֹת בִּשְׁנֵי שָׁבוּעוֹת.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) כְּמַרְאֵה צָרַעַת עוֹר בָּשָׂר, מָה עוֹר בָּשָׂר בִּשְׁנֵי שָׁבוּעוֹת, אַף כָּאן בִּשְׁנֵי שָׁבוּעוֹת:


מיטמאות בשני שבועות – as it is written (Leviticus 13:43): "כמראה צרעת עור בשר"/ “like the leprosy of body skin in appearance, just as the body skin is in two weeks, so too here (i.e., with baldness of the back or front of the head) in two weeks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:3)


**וּבְמִחְיָה וּבְפִשְׂיוֹן.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) צָרַעַת פֹּרַחַת הִיא בְּקָרַחְתּוֹ. צָרַעַת, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁמִּטַּמְּאָה בְמִחְיָה. פּוֹרַחַת, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁמִּטַּמְּאָה בְּפִשְׂיוֹן. הִיא, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁאֵינָהּ מִטַּמְּאָה בְּשֵׂעָר לָבָן:


ובמחיה ובסיון – as it is written (Leviticus 13:42): “[But if a white affection streaked with red appears on the bald part in the front or at the back of the head,] it is a scaly eruption that is spreading over the bald part in the front [or at the back of the head].”/"צרעת פורחת היא בקרחתו" . [The word] צרעת/affection – teaches that it defiles through quick flesh; [the word]פורחת /spreading, teaches that it defiles through spreading; [the word] "היא"/that it, teaches that it does not defile with white hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:4)


**נֶשֶׁם.** סַם שֶׁהָאוֹכְלוֹ אֵינוֹ מְגַדֵּל שֵׂעָר, וְהַסָּךְ בּוֹ אֵינוֹ מְגַדֵּל שֵׂעָר:


נשם (a medicine which produces depilation) – it is a drug that the person who consumes it does not grow hair, and the person who smeared it does not grow hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:5)


**מַכָּה שֶׁאֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה לְגַדֵּל שֵׂעָר.** כְּגוֹן מַכָּה גְדוֹלָה שֶׁהַבָּשָׂר נַעֲשֶׂה צַלֶּקֶת:


מכה שאינה ראויה לגדל שער – as for example a large would where the flesh becomes a rough, scabby surface/a scar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:6)


**פִּקָּה שֶׁל צַוָּאר.** הִיא חֻלְיָא הָרִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁל צַוָּאר:


פיקה של צואר (protruding cartilage of the neck) – this is the first vertebrae of the neck.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:7)


**וְאֵין פּוֹשׂוֹת מִזּוֹ לָזוֹ.** מִקָּרַחַת לְגַבַּחַת וּמִגַּבַּחַת לְקָרַחַת:


ואין פושות מזו לזו – from the leprous affection on the back of the head to the leprous affection on the front of the head or from the leprous affection on the front of the head to the leprous affection on the back of the head.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 10:10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/10:10:8)


**אִם יֵשׁ שֵׂעָר.** מַפְסִיק בֵּינֵיהֶן בֵּין קָרַחַת לְגַבַּחַת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אם יש שער – that interrupts between them between the leprous affection on the back of the head and the leprous affection on the front of the head. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



## Chapter 11


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:1:1)


**כָּל הַבְּגָדִים.** לַאֲתוֹיֵי בְּגָדִים שֶׁל עֲבָדִים וְנָשִׁים וּקְטַנִּים, וַאֲפִלּוּ שֶׁל גֵּר תּוֹשָׁב. וּבִלְבַד שֶׁהֵן מִצֶּמֶר וּפִשְׁתִּים, כְּדִכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) בְּבֶגֶד צֶמֶר אוֹ בְּבֶגֶד פִּשְׁתִּים, בְּגָדִים שֶׁל צֶמֶר וּפִשְׁתִּים אִין, בְּגָדִים שֶׁל מִידִי אַחֲרִינָא לֹא:


כל הבגדים – to include the clothing of slaves and women and minors, and even of a resident alien/גר תושב. But as long as they (i.e., the clothing) are from wool or flax, as it is written (Leviticus 13:47): “[When an eruptive affection occurs] in a cloth of wool or linen fabric/"בבגד צמר או בבגד פשתים", clothes of wool but there is no flax in them, but not clothing of something else.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:1:2)


**יֵרָאוּ בַתְּחִלָּה.** אֲפִלּוּ בְנֶגַע שֶׁהָיָה בָהֶם קֹדֶם שֶׁבָּאוּ לִידֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל. דְּהַאי בֶּגֶד הֲוָה חֲזִי לִנְגָעִים, אֶלָּא שֶׁרְשׁוּתוֹ שֶׁל נָכְרִי הָיְתָה מְעַכֶּבֶת, הִלְכָּךְ יָמִים שֶׁהָיָה בָהֶן הַנֶּגַע בְּעוֹדוֹ בִרְשׁוּת הַנָּכְרִי אֵינָן עוֹלִים לוֹ מִן הַמִּנְיָן, וּמִשֶּׁבָּא לִרְשׁוּת יִשְׂרָאֵל יֵרָאֶה בַתְּחִלָּה:


יראו בתחילה – even with a plague that was in them prior to their coming into the hand of an Israelite. For this cloth is fit for plagues, but that the domain of a heathen hinders it, therefore the days that there was a plague in them while it was still in the domain of a heathen do not count from the number, but when it came into the domain of a Israelite, it should be examined at the outset.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:1:3)


**וְעוֹרוֹת הַיָּם אֵינָן מִטַּמְּאִין.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) לַפִּשְׁתִּים וְלַצָּמֶר אוֹ בְעוֹר, מַה צֶּמֶר וּפִשְׁתִּים מִין הַגָּדֵל בָּאָרֶץ, אַף עוֹר מִמִּין הַגָּדֵל בָּאָרֶץ:


עורות הים אינן מיטמאין – as it is written (Leviticus 13:48): "לפשתים ולצמר או בעור"/ “[in the warp or in the woof] of the linen or the wool, or in a skin [or in anything made of skin],” just as wool and flax are a species that grows on the land, even skin from a species that grows on the land.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:1:4)


**חִבֵּר לָהֶן.** לְעוֹרוֹת שֶׁל דָּגִים הַגְּדֵלִים מִן הַיָּם:


חיבר להן – to the skins of fish that grow from the sea.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:1:5)


**מִן הַגָּדֵל בָּאָרֶץ אֲפִלּוּ חוּט אֲפִלּוּ מְשִׁיחָה.** וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיְּחַבְּרֶנּוּ לוֹ כְּדֶרֶךְ חִבּוּרוֹ, בִּשְׁתֵּי תְּכִיפוֹת אוֹ יוֹתֵר:


מן הגדל בארץ אפילו חוט אפילו משיחה – and as long as they attach it in the manner of its attachment, with two stitches or more.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:2:1)


**שֶׁטְּרָפָן.** שֶׁעֵרְבָן וְעָשָׂה מֵהֶן בֶּגֶד:


שטרפן (that were hackled/combed)- that were mixed and he made cloth from them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:2:2)


**אִם רֹב מִן הַגְּמַלִּים וְכוּ'.** שֶׁהַצֶּמֶר שֶׁל רְחֵלִים מִתְבַּטֵּל בְּרֹב:


אם רוב מן הגמלים וכו' - that the wool of the sheep is abolished/suspended in the majority [of the camel’s wool].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:2:3)


**מֶחֱצָה לְמֶחֱצָה.** שֶׁצֶּמֶר גְּמַלִּים כְּצֶמֶר רְחֵלִים בְּשָׁוֶה:


מחצה למחצה – that the wool of camels is like the wool of sheep in equal shares.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:2:4)


**וְכֵן הַפִּשְׁתָּן וְהַקַּנְבּוֹס שֶׁטְּרָפָן זֶה בָזֶה.** וְעָשָׂה מֵהֶן בֶּגֶד, דִּינָן כְּדִין צֶמֶר גְּמַלִּים וְצֶמֶר רְחֵלִים שֶׁטְּרָפָן:


וכן הפשתן והקנבוס שטרפן זה בזה – and he made of them cloth, their law is like the law of the wool of camels and the wool of sheep that were hackled.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:3:1)


**הַצְּבוּעִים.** אֲפִלּוּ בִידֵי שָׁמַיִם:


הצבועים – even at the hands of Heaven.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:3:2)


**הַבָּתִּים בֵּין צְבוּעִים.** אֲפִלּוּ בִידֵי אָדָם:


הבתים בין צבועים – even at the hands of man.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:3:3)


**הָעוֹרוֹת כַּבָּתִּים.** וַאֲפִלּוּ צְבוּעִים בִּידֵי אָדָם מִטַּמְּאִין בַּנְּגָעִים. וַהֲלָכָה שֶׁהַבְּגָדִים אֵין מִטַּמְּאִין צְבוּעִים, וַאֲפִלּוּ בִידֵי שָׁמַיִם, דִּכְתִיב [שָׁם] בְּבֶגֶד צֶמֶר אוֹ בְּבֶגֶד פִּשְׁתִּים, מַה פִּשְׁתִּים כִּבְרִיָּתוֹ, אַף צֶמֶר כִּבְרִיָּתוֹ. וּכְתִיב לַפִּשְׁתִּים וְלַצָּמֶר, מַה פִּשְׁתִּים לָבָן, אַף צֶמֶר לָבָן. וְעוֹרוֹת הַצְּבוּעִים בִּידֵי אָדָם אֵין מִטַּמְּאִין בַּנְּגָעִים. אֲבָל בִּידֵי שָׁמַיִם מִטַּמְּאִין בַּנְּגָעִים, כְּדִבְרֵי רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


העורות כבתים – and even if they are colored at the hands of man become susceptible to uncleanness through plagues. But the Halakha that colored clothing is not susceptible to uncleanness and even at the hands of Heaven, as it is written (Leviticus 13:47): [“When an eruptive affection occurs in] a cloth of wool or linen fabric,” / "בגד צמר או בבגד פשתים", just as linen/flax is like its creation, even wool is like its creation. And it is written (Leviticus 13:48): “[In the warp or in the woof] of the linen or the wool, [or in a skin or anything made of skin],” just as flax/linen is white, even wool is white, and the colored skins made by man do not become susceptible to uncleanness through plagues, but at the hands of Heave, they do become susceptible to uncleanness through plagues, according to the words of Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:1)


**שֶׁשִּׁתְיוֹ צָבוּעַ.** הַשְּׁתִי שֶׁלּוֹ צָבוּעַ. וְאֵין צָבוּעַ מִטַּמֵּא:


ששתיו צבוע – its warp is colored, but that which is colored is not susceptible to uncleanness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:2)


**וְעֶרְבּוֹ לָבָן.** וְאִי אָזְלִינַן בָּתַר הָעֵרֶב הוּא לָבָן וּמִטַּמֵּא:


וערבו לבן – but if we follow after the woof which is white, it is susceptible to uncleanness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:3)


**הַכֹּל הוֹלֵךְ אַחַר הַנִּרְאֶה.** בִּסְתָם בְּגָדִים הָעֵרֶב נִרְאֶה, בְּכָרִים וּכְסָתוֹת הַשְּׁתִי נִרְאֶה. וְהַכֹּל לְפִי הָאֻמָּנוּת:


הכל הולך אחר הנראה – with undefined clothing the woof is seen, [whereas] with mattresses and bolsters, the warp is seen. But everything is according to the artisanry.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:4)


**יְרַקְרַק.** יָרֹק שֶׁבַּיְרֻקִּים:


ירקרק – the palest of the pale (green) colors (see Leviticus 13:49 and 14:37).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:5)


**אֲדַמְדַּם.** אָדֹם שֶׁבָּאֲדֻמִּים. כְּלוֹמַר, שֶׁמַּרְאֵה הַיַּרְקוּת וְהָאַדְמִימוּת בָּהֶם חָזָק מְאֹד. יְרַקְרַק, כִּכְנַף הַטַּוָּס וּכְהוּצִין שֶׁל דֶּקֶל. אֲדַמְדַּם, כִּזְהוֹרִית יָפָה שֶׁבַּדָּם:


אדמדם – the red that is among the reddish, meaning to say, that the appearance of the greens and the redness/reddish color in them is very strong. ירקרק/palest of pale green is like the wing of a peacock and like the long and thin foliage of a palm branch (that is prickly). אדמדם/red that is among the reddish is like the nice crimson of the blood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:6)


**הָיָה יְרַקְרַק.** כַּגְּרִיס, וְלִבְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ פָּשָׂה אֲדַמְדַּם אֵצֶל יְרַקְרַק:


היה ירקרק כגיסת ולסוף שבוע פשה אדמדם אצל ירקרק:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:7)


**נִשְׁתַּנָּה וּפָשָׂה.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָיָה בַתְּחִלָּה יְרַקְרַק כַּגְּרִיס, וְלִבְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ פָּשָׂה וְנַעֲשָׂה גָּדוֹל כַּסֶּלַע, וּבֵין הָאוֹם וּבֵין הַפִּשְׂיוֹן שֶׁלּוֹ אֲדַמְדַּם:


נשתנה ופשה – as for example, that it was at the outset palest of pale green like a split [Cilician] bean, and at the end of the week, it spread and became large like a rock, but whether it was the starting point of leprosy or whether its spread was red that is among the reddish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:8)


**נִשְׁתַּנָּה וְלֹא פָשָׂה.** בַּתְּחִלָּה כַגְּרִיס שֶׁל יְרַקְרַק, וְלִבְסוֹף כַּגְּרִיס שֶׁל אֲדַמְדַּם:


נשתנה ולא פשה – at the beginning it was like a split [Cilician] bean of pale green color and at the end like a split [Cilician] bean of reddish color.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:9)


**כְּאִלּוּ לֹא נִשְׁתַּנָּה.** וְאוֹתוֹ שֶׁפָּשָׂה טָעוּן שְׂרֵפָה, וְאוֹתוֹ שֶׁלֹּא פָשָׂה מַסְגִּירוֹ שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי:


כאילו לא נשתנה – and that which spread requires burning, and that which did not spread, we shut up/isolate/quarantine for a second week.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:4:10)


**יֵרָאֶה [בַתְּחִלָּה].** דַּחֲשִׁיב לְנִשְׁתַּנָּה כְּנֶגַע אַחֵר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


יראה (בתחלה) – that it is considered as if it hanged to another plague. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:1)


**הָעוֹמֵד.** בְּעֵינוֹ בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן, שֶׁלֹּא פָשָׂה וְלֹא כָהָה מַרְאִיתוֹ:


העומד – unchanged at the end of the first week, that it didn’t spread nor did its appearance become dim/faint.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:2)


**יְכַבֵּס.** מְקוֹם הַנֶּגַע וְיַסְגִּיר:


יכבס – the place of the plague and isolate/quarantine him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:3)


**הָעוֹמֵד בַּשֵּׁנִי.** בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי לֹא כָהָה וְלֹא פָשָׂה:


העומד בשני – at the end of the second week it didn’t become dim/faint nor did it spread, [it should be burned].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:4)


**הַפּוֹשֶׂה בָזֶה וּבָזֶה.** בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן אוֹ בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי:


הפושה בזה ובזה – at the end of he first week or at the end of the second week.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:5)


**הִכְהָה בַתְּחִלָּה.** כְּשֶׁהוּבָא אֶל הַכֹּהֵן וְרָאָהוּ וְקֹדֶם שֶׁהִסְגִּיר כָּהָה:


הכהה בתחילה – when it was brought to the Kohen and he saw it but prior to his isolating/quarantining him, it became faint.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:6)


**יְכַבֵּס וְיַסְגִּיר.** כְּאִלּוּ כָהָה בְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן:


יכבס ויסגיר – as if it became faint at the end of the first week.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:7)


**הִכְהָה.** שֶׁהָיָה מִתְּחִלָּה חָזָק הַיַּרְקוּת אוֹ הָאַדְמִימוּת, וְנַעֲשָׂה יָרֹק בֵּינוֹנִי אוֹ אָדֹם בֵּינוֹנִי:


הכהה – that it was at the beginning a strong greenish color or reddish color, and it became medium green or medium red.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:8)


**אֵינוֹ זָקוּק לוֹ.** אֵין זָקוּק לְהַסְגִּירוֹ, וְטָהוֹר. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


אינו זקוק לו (it is not subject to it) – it is not necessary to isolate/quarantine him, and he is pure. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:9)


**הִכְהָה בָרִאשׁוֹן יְכַבֵּס וְיַסְגִּיר.** כְּאִלּוּ לֹא כָהָה:


הכהה בראשון יכבס ויסגיר – it is as if it did not grow faint.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:10)


**הִכְהָה בַשֵּׁנִי.** דִּבְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי מְצָאוֹ שֶׁכָּהָה:


הכהה בשני – that at the end of the second week, he found it that it had become faint.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:11)


**קוֹרְעוֹ וְשׂוֹרֵף מַה שֶּׁקָּרַע, וְצָרִיךְ מַטְלִית.** לְשׁוֹן טְלַאי, חֲתִיכַת בֶּגֶד בִּמְקוֹם הַקֶּרַע, דִּכְתִיב וְאִם תֵּרָאֶה עוֹד, וְאֵין עוֹד אֶלָּא מְקוֹמוֹ, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁהוּא טוֹלֶה עָלָיו מַטְלִית:


קורעו ושורף מה שקרע, וצריך מטלית – the language of a patch/טלאי (see Joshua 9:5: “they had worn-out, patched sandals on their feet, and threadbare clothes on their bodies”). A piece of cloth in place of the ear, as it is written (Leviticus 13:57): “And if it occurs again in the cloth – [whether in warp or in woof – or in any article of skin],”/"ואם תראה עוד [בבגד או-בשתי או-בערב או כבל-כלי-עור]" , but [the word] “again” is none other than in its place, which teaches that one covers it with a patch.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:5:12)


**רַבִּי נְחֶמְיָה אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי נְחֶמְיָה:


רבי נחמיה אומר כו' – but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Nehemiah.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:1)


**חָזַר נֶגַע לַבֶּגֶד.** בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר מִן הַבֶּגֶד, וְלֹא בַמַּטְלִית שֶׁנָּתַן עַל מְקוֹם הַקְּרִיעָה:


חזר הנגע לבגד – in another place from the cloth, but not upon the patch that he placed on the place of the tear.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:2)


**מַצִּיל אֶת הַמַּטְלִית.** כְּשֶׁשּׂוֹרֵף אֶת הַבֶּגֶד כֻּלּוֹ. כְּדִכְתִיב וְאִם תֵּרָאֶה עוֹד בַּבֶּגֶד וְגוֹ'‏ וְשָׂרַף אֶת הַבֶּגֶד. מַצִּיל אֶת הַמַּטְלִית, אוֹתוֹ טְלַאי שֶׁנָּתַן עַל מְקוֹם הַקְּרִיעָה מַצִּילוֹ, וְאֵינוֹ שׂוֹרְפוֹ עִם הַבֶּגֶד:


מציל את המטלית – when he burns the entire cloth, as it is written (Leviticus 13:57): "ואם תראה עוד"/”And if it occurs again [in the cloth – whether in warp or in woof – or in any article of skin],” and he burns the cloth. [But] he saves the patch, that patch that he put on the place of the tear he saves, and he doesn’t burn it with the cloth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:3)


**חָזַר לַמַּטְלִית.** לְאוֹתוֹ טְלַאי שֶׁחִבֵּר בּוֹ:


חזר למטלית – o that place that he attached to it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:4)


**שׂוֹרֵף אֶת הַבָּגֶד.** כְּאִלּוּ חָזַר בַּבֶּגֶד עַצְמוֹ. אֲבָל הַמַּטְלִית אֵינוֹ טָעוּן שְׂרֵפָה, אֶלָּא אִם הוּא שָׁלֹשׁ עַל שָׁלֹשׁ, מַסְגִּירוֹ. וְאִם עָמַד בְּעֵינָיו שָׁבוּעוֹת שְׁנַיִם אוֹ פָשָׂה בְּשָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן, שׂוֹרֵף. וְאִם פָּחוֹת מִשָּׁלֹשׁ עַל שָׁלֹשׁ, אֵינוֹ מִטַּמֵּא בַנְּגָעִים:


שורף את הבגד – as if it returned to the cloth itself. But the patch does not require burning, but rather if it is three [handbreadths] by three [handbreadths], he isolates/quarantines it, and if it remained unchanged two weeks or it spread in the first week, he burns it. But if it is less than three [fingerbreadths] by three [fingerbreadths] it is not susceptible to uncleanness by plagues.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:5)


**הַטּוֹלֶה מִן הַמֻּסְגָּר.** שֶׁעָשָׂה טְלַאי מֵחֲתִיכַת בֶּגֶד מֻסְגָּר עַל בֶּגֶד טָהוֹר. וּמֻסְגָּר קָרֵי לְבֶגֶד שֶׁעָמַד בְּעֵינָיו בְּשָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן, וּבְשָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי כָּהָה, דְּקוֹרֵעַ מִמֶּנּוּ הַנֶּגַע וְשׂוֹרֵף מַה שֶּׁקָּרַע, וּשְׁאָר הַבֶּגֶד קָרוּי מֻסְגָּר:


הטולה מן המוסגר – that he made a patch from the piece of cloth that had been in isolation on the pure/clean cloth. And the isolated one is called to a cloth that remained unchanged in the first week but in the second became pale/dim, that he tears out from it the plague and burns what he tore, but the rest of the cloth is called מוסגר/isolated/quarantined.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:6)


**חָזַר הַנֶּגַע לַבֶּגֶד.** הַמֻּסְגָּר. שֶׁנִּטְּלָה מִמֶּנּוּ חֲתִיכָה זוֹ שֶׁהִיא עַתָּה טְלַאי לְבֶגֶד טָהוֹר, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַבֶּגֶד הַמֻּסְגָּר טָעוּן שְׂרֵפָה כְּדִכְתִיב וְאִם תֵּרָאֶה עוֹד בַּבֶּגֶד וְגוֹ':


חזר הנגע לבגד – that is isolated/quarantined. That this piece that was taken from it which is now a patch for a pure cloth, even though the cloth that is isolated/quarantined requires burning, as it is written (Leviticus 13:57): "ואם תראה עוד בבגד"/”And if it occurs again in the cloth.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:7)


**מַצִּיל אֶת הַמַּטְלִית.** הַיְנוּ הַטְּלַאי הַזֶּה שֶׁנִּטַּל מִמֶּנּוּ וּמְחֻבָּר בְּבֶגֶד טָהוֹר, מַצִּילוֹ וְאֵינוֹ שׂוֹרְפוֹ עִם הַבֶּגֶד הַמֻּסְגָּר:


מציל את המטלית – that is this patch that was taken from it and attached on to a pure/clean cloth, we save/protect it and we don’t burn it with the quarantined/isolated cloth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:8)


**חָזַר.** הַנֶּגַע:


חזר – the plague.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:9)


**לַמַּטְלִית.** לַחֲתִיכָה זוֹ שֶׁנִּטְּלָה מִן הַמֻּסְגָּר:


למטלית – to this piece that was taken from the isolated/quarantined [cloth].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:6:10)


**יִשָּׂרֵף.** הַמֻּסְגָּר בְּכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁהוּא, כְּאִלּוּ הָיְתָה חֲתִיכָה זוֹ מְחֻבֶּרֶת בּוֹ. וַחֲתִיכָה תְּשַׁמֵּשׁ הַבֶּגֶד הַשֵּׁנִי שֶׁהִיא מְחֻבֶּרֶת בּוֹ: **בְּסִימָנִים.** שֶׁמַּסְגִּיר עִם הַבֶּגֶד הַשֵּׁנִי. דְּדִינוֹ שֶׁל שֵׁנִי כְּאִלּוּ הַנֶּגַע בּוֹ, בֵּין לְעִנְיַן הֶסְגֵּר בֵּין לְעִנְיַן שְׂרֵפָה וְכִבּוּס וְכָל דָּבָר. וּלְאַחַר שֶׁשִּׁמְּשָׁה הַבֶּגֶד הַשֵּׁנִי, אֲפִלּוּ הָלַךְ הַנֶּגַע לְגַמְרֵי מִן הַחֲתִיכָה, טָעוּן שְׂרֵפָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי מִשֶּׁחָזַר בּוֹ הַנֶּגַע מִתְּחִלָּתוֹ כְּבָר הֻזְקַק לִשְׂרֵפָה:


ישרף – the quarantined [cloth] in every place that it is , as if it was the piece that was attached to it. And that piece may serve he second cloth that is attached to it. בסימנים – that it is shut up/isolated/quarantined with the second cloth. For the law of the second is as if the plague is in it, whether in regard to isolation, or whether in regard to burning and washing and every matter. But after it served the second cloth, even if the plague went away completely from the piece, it (requires burning, for when the plague returned from its outset, it was already required burning.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:1)


**קַיְטָא.** מַלְבּוּשׁ שֶׁרְגִילִים לִלְבֹּשׁ בִּימוֹת הַחַמָּה. תַּרְגּוּם קַיִץ, קַיְטָא. וְלִי נִרְאֶה שֶׁהוּא לָשׁוֹן רוֹמִ"י שֶׁקּוֹרִים לְמַלְבּוּשׁ שֶׁל אִשָּׁה קוטיי"א:


קייטא – clothing that it is customary to wear in the days of the heat. The Aramaic translation of קיץ/summer is summer/קייטא. But it appears to me that it is the Roman language that calls the dress of a woman KUTYA.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:2)


**שֶׁיֶּשׁ לָהּ פִּסְפָּסִין.** מְנֻמֶּרֶת בִּצְבָעִים הַרְבֵּה, לָבָן וְיָרֹק וְשָׁחֹר וְדוֹמֵיהֶן. פִּסְפָּסִין לְשׁוֹן פַּס יָד, שֶׁפִּסַּת יָד מִבֶּגֶד לָבָן וּפִסַּת יָד מִבֶּגֶד שֶׁל צֶבַע:


שיש לו פספסין (that it has stripes)- striped/speckled in many colors, white and green and black and similar to those. [The word] פספסין – is the language of פס יד/the palm/the hand from the wrist to the tip of the finger, that the wrist is from the white cloth and the wrist is from the cloth of color.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:3)


**וּלְבָנִים פּוֹשִׂים מִזֶּה לָזֶה.** מִלָּבָן לְלָבָן. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַצָּבוּעַ מַפְסִיק, חָשׁוּב פִּשְׂיוֹן. דְּפִשְׂיוֹן רָחוֹק מְטַמֵּא בְכַגְּרִיס:


ולבנים פושים מזה לזה – from [one] white [garment] to [another] white [garment]. But even though the colored part interrupts, it is considered spreading. For spreading far defiles with like the size of a split [Cilician] bean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:4)


**וַהֲרֵי הוּא פִסְפָּס יְחִידִי.** כְּלוֹמַר, אִם לֹא הָיָה בוֹ אֶלָּא פִסְפָּס אֶחָד לָבָן, וְכָל שְׁאָר הַפִּסְפָּסִין צְבוּעִין, מִי בָעֵי הֶסְגֵּר, אוֹ לֹא, מִשּׁוּם דְּאֵין לוֹ לְהֵיכָן יִפְשֶׂה:


והרי הוא פספס יחידי (And lo, it is a distinctive check?) – meaning to say, if he didn’t have in it other than one speckled white area and all the rest are speckled colored. Does it require isolation or not because it lacks to where it would spread?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:5)


**אָמַר לָהֶן לֹא שָׁמַעְתִּי.** כְּלוֹמַר, קִבַּלְתִּי מֵרַבּוֹתַי דְּטָעוּן הֶסְגֵּר, וְלֹא פֵרְשׁוּ לִי הַטַּעַם לָמָּה:


אמר להן לא שמעתי – meaning to say, I received from my Teachers/Rabbis that it requires isolation, but they did not explain the reason why.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:6)


**אֲלַמֵּד בּוֹ.** כְּלוֹמַר, תַּרְשֵׁנִי לוֹמַר לְפָנֶיךָ טַעַם לַדָּבָר:


אלמד בו – meaning to say, permit me to state before you a reason for the matter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:7)


**אִם לְפָרֵשׁ דִּבְרֵי חֲכָמִים הֵן.** שֶׁלֹּא תִסְתֹּר מַה שֶּׁקִּבַּלְתִּי:


אם לפרש דברי חכמים הן – that you will not contradict what I have received [from my Rabbis].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:8)


**שֶׁמָּא יַעֲמֹד שְׁנֵי שָׁבוּעוֹת.** שֶׁאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין לוֹ בַמֶּה יִפְשֶׂה יָכוֹל לָבֹא לִידֵי טֻמְאָה, אִם יַעֲמֹד בְּעֵינָיו שְׁנֵי שָׁבוּעוֹת:


שמא יעמוד שני שבועות – that even though he lacks in what manner it will spread, it is able to come to uncleanness, for if it remains unchanged for two weeks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:9)


**הַפִּשְׂיוֹן הַסָּמוּךְ.** כְּשֶׁפּוֹשֶׂה סָמוּךְ לָאוֹם:


הפסיון הסמוך – that it spreads adjacent to/near the starting point of the leprosy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:10)


**הָרָחוֹק.** שֶׁהָאוֹם בְּרֹאשׁ אֶחָד שֶׁל בֶּגֶד וְהַפִּשְׂיוֹן בְּרֹאשׁוֹ הַשֵּׁנִי:


הרחוק – that the starting point of the leprosy is at one end of the cloth and the spreading is at the other end.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:7:11)


**הַחוֹזֵר.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁכָּהָה בַשֵּׁנִי דְּקוֹרְעוֹ וְשׂוֹרֵף מַה שֶּׁקָּרַע, וּכְתִיב וְאִם תֵּרָאֶה עוֹד בַּבֶּגֶד וְשָׂרַף וְגוֹ'. וְזֶהוּ הַחוֹזֵר, וְשִׁעוּרוֹ בְכַגְּרִיס:


והחוזר – as for example, that it became dim/pale in the second [week] that he tears it and burns what he tore, as it is written (Leviticus 13:57): "ואם תיראה עוד בבגד...ושרף [באש תשרפנו את אשר-בו הנגע]"/And if it occurs again in the cloth [whether in warp or in woof – or in any article of skin, it is a wild growth; the affected article] shall be consumed in fire.” (Bartenura does NOT accurately quote the verse as he has the word "ושרף"/and he shall burn – which does not appear in the text of the Torah.). And this is that which returns, and its measurement is like that of a split [Cilician] bean.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:1)


**הַשְּׁתִי וְהָעֵרֶב.** שֶׁל צֶמֶר, שֶׁנִּכָּר בֵּין שְׁתִי לְעֵרֶב, שֶׁהַשְּׁתִי דַּק וְהָעֵרֶב עָב מְאֹד:


השתי והערב – [the warp and woof] of wool, that is recognized whether warp to woof,that the warp is thin and the woof is very thick.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:2)


**הַשְּׁתִי מִשֶּׁיִּשָּׁלֵק.** דִּכְתִיב בְּבֶגֶד צֶמֶר אוֹ בְּבֶגֶד פִּשְׁתִּים אוֹ בִשְׁתִי אוֹ בְעֵרֶב, מַה בֶּגֶד שֶׁנִּגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתּוֹ, אַף שְׁתִי וָעֵרֶב מִשֶּׁתִּגָּמֵר מְלַאכְתָּן. וְתַנָּא קַמָּא סָבַר, אוֹ בִשְׁתִי, הִפְסִיק הָעִנְיָן:


השתי מישלק – as it is written (Leviticus 13:47-48): “in a cloth made of wool or linen fabric. In the warp or in the woof of the linen or the wool,” just as the cloth, when its work was completed, even the warp and the woof, when their work is completed. But the first Tanna/teacher holds, or with the warp, the matter was interrupted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:3)


**מִשֶּׁיִּשָּׁלֵק.** בְּמַיִם רוֹתְחִים. וְיֵשׁ סְפָרִים שֶׁגּוֹרְסִים מִשֶּׁיִּשָּׁלֶה, כְּלוֹמַר מִשֶּׁיִּהְיוּ שׁוֹלִין וּמְסִירִים אוֹתוֹ מִן הַיּוֹרָה שֶׁשּׁוֹלְקִים אוֹתָן בָּהּ לְלַבְּנָן:


משישלק – in boiling water. But there are books that have the reading "משישלה", meaning to say that when they would be pulled/drawn out and transfer it from the dyer’s kettle when they boil them in it to whiten them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:4)


**הָאוּנִין שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן.** אֲגֻדּוֹת מַטְוֶה שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן. וּלְפִי שֶׁבַּפִּשְׁתָּן אֵין נִכָּר בֵּין שְׁתִי לָעֵרֶב כְּמוֹ בַצֶּמֶר, מִשּׁוּם הָכִי לֹא מְפַלֵּיג בְּהוּ בֵּין שְׁתִי לְעֵרֶב:


והאונין של פשתן (bundles of wet flax) – bundles of yard of flax. But because the flax is not recognized between warp and woof like wool, because of this, there is no division in them between warp to woof.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:5)


**מִשֶּׁיִּתְלַבֵּנוּ.** שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים אוֹתָן בַּתַּנּוּר שֶׁיִּתְחַמְּמוּ בְהֶבֶל הַתַּנּוּר וְיִתְלַבְּנוּ שָׁם:


משיתלבנו – that they place them in the oven so that they will heat up with the vapor of the oven and they be whitened/bleached there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:6)


**פְּקַעַת.** עֵץ שֶׁהַנָּשִׁים מְסַבְּבוֹת הַמַּטְוֶה מִן הַכּוּשׁ לְתוֹכוֹ:


פקעת (coil of thread) – wood that the women surround the yarn from the reed used as a spindle into it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:7)


**כְּדֵי לֶאֱרֹג מִמֶּנָּה שָׁלֹשׁ עַל שָׁלֹשׁ.** שֶׁיְּהֵא בָהּ שִׁעוּר לַעֲשׂוֹת כָּל הַבֶּגֶד שֶׁל שָׁלֹשׁ אֶצְבָּעוֹת עַל שָׁלֹשׁ אֶצְבָּעוֹת מִמֶּנָּה, גַּם הַשְּׁתִי גַּם הָעֵרֶב, כִּדְקָתָנֵי וַאֲפִלּוּ כֻלָּהּ שְׁתִי וַאֲפִלּוּ כֻלָּהּ עֵרֶב:


כדי לארוג ממנה שלש על שלש – in order that there be there the measurement to make all of the cloth of three fingerbreadths by three fingerbreadths from it, also the warp [and] also the woof, as it is taught [in the Mishnah], “and even it is all warp, even if all of it is woof.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:8)


**הָיְתָה פְסִיקוֹת.** שֶׁהָיוּ הַחוּטִים שֶׁבַּפְּקַעַת מֻפְסָקִין, לֹא הָוֵי חִבּוּר. אֲבָל אִם הָיוּ קְשׁוּרִים הָוֵי חִבּוּר:


היתה פסיקות (broken threads) – that the threads in the coil of thread were broken, there is no attachment. But if they are knotted, there is attachment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:8:9)


**אֲפִלּוּ פְסִיקָה אַחַת.** שֶׁאֵין בְּכָל הַפְּקַעַת אֶלָּא פְסִיקָה אַחַת וּקְשָׁרָהּ, לֹא הָוֵי חִבּוּר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אפילו פסיקה אחת – that there isn’t throughout all of the coil of thread other than one tear and he knotted it, it is not an attachment. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda..



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:1)


**הַמַּעֲלֶה מִן הַפְּקַעַת לַחֲבֶרְתָּהּ.** פְּעָמִים שֶׁהַנָּשִׁים עוֹשׂוֹת פְּקַעַת בַּת אַמָּה אוֹ בַּת שְׁתֵּי אַמּוֹת וּמְגַלְגְּלוֹת וּמְסַבְּבוֹת מִזּוֹ לָזוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹת פְּקַעַת גְּדוֹלָה, וְאִם עַד שֶׁלֹּא תַעֲלֶה אֶת כֻּלָּהּ נִרְאָה נֶגַע בְּאַחַת מֵהֶן וְחוּט מְחַבְּרָן, הָוֵי הַשֵּׁנִי טָהוֹר:


המעלה מן הפקעת (one who winds thread from one coil to another) – sometimes when women make a coil of thread a cubit [in length] or two cubits [in length], and rolls and surrounds from one to the other to make a large coil, if before she winds all of it, a plague appears on one of them, and the thread fastens them, the second is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:2)


**סְלִיל.** עֵץ חָלוּל כְּמִין קָנֶה שֶׁמְּחַבְּרִים עָלָיו הַמַּטְוֶה:


סליל (shuttle containing the spool) – a hallow piece of wood like a species of a reed that they fasten upon it the yarn.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:3)


**כֹּבֶד הָעֶלְיוֹנָה.** שְׁנֵי עֵצִים עֲגֻלִּים שֶׁהָאוֹרְגִין כּוֹרְכִים בָּהֶן הַשְּׁתִי שֶׁלָּהֶן וְגוֹלְלִין, וְנִרְאָה נֶגַע בַּכֹּבֶד הָאֶחָד,


כובד העליונה (the upper beam from which the warp depends) – two round pieces of wood that the weaves attach on them their warp and rolls them, and the plague appears on the one beam.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:4)


**הַשֵּׁנִי טָהוֹר.** דְּלֹא הָוֵי חִבּוּר:


השני טהור – that it was not an attachment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:5)


**וְכֵן שְׁנֵי דַפֵּי חָלוּק.** נַמִּי אֵינָן חִבּוּר. וְלֹא כְעֵין חֲלוּקִים שֶׁלָּנוּ שֶׁמְּחַתְּכִים וּמְחַבְּרִין בּוֹ בָתֵּי הַזְּרוֹעַ וּשְׁאָר חֲתִיכוֹת בִּתְפִירָה שֶׁל חִבּוּר גָּמוּר, דְּהַהוּא וַדַּאי חֲשִׁיב חִבּוּר כְּאִלּוּ הוּא בֶגֶד אֶחָד, אֶלָּא מַיְרֵי דְּלֹא מִחַבַּר כֻּלֵּי הַאי:


וכן שני דפי לחוק (and similarly, the two wings of a shirt) – also are not an attachment. And they are not similar to tour shirts that we cut and attach to it the sleeves and the other pieces with a stitch of a complete attachment, for that is definitely considered attaching as if it is one cloth, but we are speaking that they are not attached all that much.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:6)


**בְּנֶפֶשׁ הַמַּסֶּכֶת.** חוּט הָעֵרֶב שֶׁנִּכְנָס לְתוֹךְ הַשְּׁתִי כַּנֶּפֶשׁ לְתוֹךְ הַגּוּף, וְעַד שֶׁיִּתְיַשְּׁבוּ עַל מְכוֹנָם אֵצֶל הָאָרוּג הָוֵי כִשְׁתִי לְהִצְטָרֵף עִמּוֹ לְנֶגַע כַּגְּרִיס. אִי נַמִּי, בַּתְּחִלָּה כְּשֶׁאוֹרֵג בּוֹ שְׁנֵי בָתֵּי נִירִים קֹדֶם שֶׁיֶּאֱרֹג בּוֹ שָׁלֹשׁ עַל שָׁלֹשׁ:


בנפש המסכת (the woof of the standing loom) – the thread of the woof that enters into the warp like the soul into the body, and until that settles on their defined place near that which is woven it is like the warp to combine with it to a plague like a split [Cilician] bean. Alternatively, at the outset, when he weaves in it the two portions of he web produced by passing the spool with the woof across the warp prior to weaving in it three [fingerbreadths] by three [fingerbreadths].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:7)


**וּבַשְּׁתִי הָעוֹמֵד.** הֵם חוּטֵי הַשְּׁתִי שֶׁבֵּין כֹּבֶד הָעֶלְיוֹן לַתַּחְתּוֹן, שֶׁעוֹמְדִים שָׁם לֵאָרֵג וּלְהָסֵךְ בָּהֶן חוּטֵי הָעֵרֶב. וְקָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּלֹא תֵימָא כָּל חוּט וְחוּט עוֹמֵד בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ וְאֵין בּוֹ רֹחַב כַּגְּרִיס, אֶלָּא כֻלָּם חִבּוּר זֶה לָזֶה, וְאִם נִרְאָה (נמי) נֶגַע בְּצַד זֶה, טָמֵא בְּצַד אַחֵר:


בשתי העומד (the standing warp) – they are the threats of the warp that are between the upper beam and the lower beam, the roller on which the web is wound as it advances, that stands there to be woven and to anoint them the threads of the woof. And this comes to tell us that it does not say that each and every thread stands on its own and that it doesn’t have the width of a split [Cilician] bean, but rather all of it is attached one to the other. But if a plague (also) appears on this side, it is impure on the other side.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:9:8)


**אִם הָיָה רָצוּף.** שֶׁהַחוּטִים רְצוּפִים זֶה אֵצֶל זֶה כְּעֵין רִצְפָּה, שֶׁאוֹרְגִין זֶה אֵצֶל זֶה, שֶׁכֵּן דֶּרֶךְ הָאוֹרְגִים לְיַשְּׁבָן לִהְיוֹת אֲרִיגָתָם מְכֻוֶּנֶת, אָז חֲשִׁיבָא חִבּוּר. וְאִי לָאו הָכִי, חֲזִינַן כָּל חוּט כְּאִלּוּ עוֹמֵד בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ וְלֹא הָוֵי חִבּוּר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


אם היה רצוף (if he warp – on the loom – is joined closely – the threads being properly arranged) – that the threads are joined closely one next to the other like a cobweb/block pavement, for such is the manner of the weavers to arrange them so that their weaving is corresponding, then it is considered attachment. But if not like this, we view each thread as if it stands on its own and it is not an attachment. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:10:1)


**נִרְאָה הַנֶּגַע בַּשְּׁתִי הָעוֹמֵד הָאָרִיג טָהוֹר.** כְּשֶׁאֵין עָתִיד לֶאֱרֹג יוֹתֵר:


נראה – the plague. בשתי העומד האריג טהור (the standing warp, the web is clean/pure) – for he will not weave any further in the future.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:10:2)


**הַשְּׁתִי הָעוֹמֵד טָהוֹר.** הַיְנוּ מַה שֶּׁאֵין עָתִיד לְקַצֵּץ מִן הַשְּׁתִי עִם הָאָרוּג:


השתי העומד טהור – that is what he doesn’t plan to cut from the warp with that which is woven (i.e., the web).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:10:3)


**הַנִּימִין.** חוּטִין שֶׁבְּרֹאשׁ הַסָּדִין וּבְסוֹפוֹ:


הנימין (fringes) – threads that are the top of the sheet and at its bottom.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:10:4)


**מַצִּיל אֶת הָאֻמְרִיּוֹת.** שְׂפַת הַבֶּגֶד, שֶׁאֵינוֹ שׂוֹרֵף אוֹתוֹ, דִּכְתִיב וְשָׂרַף אֶת הַבֶּגֶד בַּצֶּמֶר אוֹ בַפִּשְׁתִּים, שֶׁאֵין תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר בַּצֶּמֶר אוֹ בַפִּשְׁתִּים, אֶלָּא לְלַמֶּדְךָ שֶׁאֵין שׂוֹרֵף הָאֻמְרִיּוֹת עִמָּהֶן:


מציל את האומריות (it protects the bordered garment/hems) – the rim of the cloth. That he doesn’t burn it, as it is written (Leviticus 13:52): "ושרף את-הבגד [או את-השתי או את-הערב] בצמר או בפשתים"/ “the cloth – [whether warp or woof] in wool or linen, [or any article of skin-in which the affection is found,] shall be burned, [for it is a malignant eruption; it shall be consumed in fire],” to teach you that they don’t burn the hems with them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:10:5)


**וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵן אַרְגָּמָן.** דְּלֹא תֵימָא דְּאֵין מַצִּיל אֶלָּא כְשֶׁהָאֻמְרִיּוֹת שֶׁל מִין אַחֵר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִטַּמֵא בַנְּגָעִים כְּגוֹן שֶׁל מֶשִׁי וְשֶׁל זָהָב, קָמַשְׁמַע לָן וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵן אַרְגָּמָן שֶׁהוּא צֶמֶר צָבוּעַ מַצִּיל:


ואפילו הן ארגמן – that you should not say that it doesn’t protect other than when the hims are of a different species that doesn’t defile with plagues as for example, of silk or of gold, which comes to teach us, that even if they are of purple [wool] which is colored wool it protects.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:1)


**כֹּל הָרָאוּי לִטַּמֵּא טְמֵא מֵת.** בְּצֶמֶר וּפִשְׁתִּים אוֹ בְעוֹר:


כל הראוי ליטמא טמא מת – with wool or flax or with skins.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:2)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִטַּמֵּא מִדְרָס.** כְּגוֹן בֶּגֶד שָׁלֹשׁ עַל שָׁלֹשׁ, דְּאֵינוֹ מִטַּמֵּא מִדְרָס עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא בוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים עַל שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים, וּמִטַּמֵּא בְמֵת. וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁכָּל הַמִּטַּמְּאִין מִדְרָס מִטַּמְּאִין בַּנְּגָעִים, וּבִלְבַד בִּלְבָנִים שֶׁאֵינָם צְבוּעִים:


אע"פ שאינו מיטמא מדרס – such as a cloth of three [fingerbreadths] by three [fingerbreadths], that is not defiled by threading until it has three handbreadths by three handbreadths, and it becomes susceptible to be defiled through contact with a corpse, and all the more so, that everything that becomes susceptible to become defiled through treading becomes susceptible to become defiled through plagues, as long as it is white things that are not colored.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:3)


**קֶלַע שֶׁל סְפִינָה וּוִילוֹן וְכוּ'.** כָּל הָנֵי דַחֲשִׁיב, מִטַּמְּאִין בְּמֵת וְאֵין מִטַּמְּאִים מִדְרָס:


קלע של ספינה ווילון וכו' (sail of a ship and a curtain/veil) – all of these are considered that they are susceptible to be defiled through corpse uncleanness but they are not susceptible to be defiled through treading.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:4)


**שָׁבִיס.** לְשׁוֹן הַשְּׁבִיסִים וְהַשַּׂהֲרֹנִים הַכָּתוּב בִּישַׁעְיָה (פרק ג):


שביס (woolen band with which the hairnet is fastened) – it is the language of “the fillets and the crescents” that is written in Isaiah (3:18).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:5)


**וְגַלְגִּלּוֹן.** אַבְנֵט שֶׁחוֹגְרִים בּוֹ:


וגלגלון (girdle of net-work, bandage, wrap) – a belt that they gird him with.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:6)


**סָגוֹס.** בֶּגֶד עָב מְאֹד וְהוּא כְּאַדֶּרֶת שֵׂעָר:


סגוס (a coarse woolen blanket – mentioned usually as a mates to sleep on) – a very thick cloth and it is alike a cloak/cover of hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:7)


**בָּאָרִיג וּבַמּוֹכִין.** בְּגוּף הָאַדֶּרֶת וּבַשְּׂעָרוֹת הַבּוֹלְטִים מִמֶּנָּה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בֶּן יַעֲקֹב:


באריג ובמוכין (on the web and on a soft spongy substance/hackled wool, rag) – on the body of the cloak/cover of hair and on the hairs that protrude from it. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Eliezer ben Yaakov.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:8)


**הַחֵמֶת וְהַתּוּרְמָל.** כֵּלִים שֶׁל עוֹר, וְקוֹשְׁרִים אוֹתָן וְנִכְפָּל הָעוֹר בִּמְקוֹם הַקֶּשֶׁר, וְאֵין הַנֶּגַע שֶׁבִּכְפָלָיו נִרְאֶה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן מִתְפַּשֵּׁט הָעוֹר. וּלְהָכִי קָתָנֵי נִרְאִין כְּדַרְכָּן, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁאֵין פּוֹשְׁטוֹ, אֶלָּא רוֹאֵהוּ כָךְ כְּמוֹת שֶׁהוּא. חֵמֶת, נוֹד שֶׁל עוֹר. תּוּרְמָל, כְּמִין כִּיס גָּדוֹל שֶׁל עוֹר שֶׁהָרוֹעֶה מַנִּיחַ חֲפָצָיו בְּתוֹכוֹ:


החמת והתורמל (the waterskin and the shepherd’s leather wallet) – vessels of leather, and they tie them and the leather is folded in the place of the knot, and the plague is in its folds is not seen unless the leather is spread out. And for this it is taught [in the Mishnah]: "נראין כדרכן"/and they are examined in their usual way, meaning to say, that they don’t spread them out, but rather he (i.e., the Kohen) sees it as such as it is . the waterskin, and leather bottle/skin. The תורמל – is like a kind of large leather pocket that the shepherd places his belongings into it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:11:9)


**מִתּוֹכוֹ לַאֲחוֹרָיו.** אִם מִתְּחִלָּה נִרְאָה נֶגַע בְּתוֹכוֹ וְהִסְגִּירוֹ, וּבְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ נִרְאָה נֶגַע בַּאֲחוֹרָיו מִבַּחוּץ, חֲשִׁיב פִּשְׂיוֹן. וּבַתּוֹסֶפְתָּא מַשְׁמַע דְּדַוְקָא בְעוֹרוֹת אָמְרִינַן הָכִי, וְלֹא בִבְגָדִים:
