מתוכו לאחוריו ([and it spreads] from its inside to its outer surface) – if from the outset the plague appears from in its insides, and he was shut up/quarantined, and at the end of the week, the plague appears on its outer surface, it is considered spreading. But n the Tosefta (Tractate Negaim, Chapter 5, Halakha 5:14) it implies, that we are speaking here especially with hides but not with clothing.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:12:1)


**בֶּגֶד הַמֻּסְגָּר.** שֶׁהָיָה בוֹ נֶגַע וּצְבָעוֹ וְאֵין הַנֶּגַע נִכָּר בּוֹ:


בגד מוסגר – that had in it a plague and it became dyed, and the plague is not recognized in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:12:2)


**כֻּלָּן טְהוֹרִים.** דְּאֶת הַוַּדַּאי הוּא מְטַמֵּא, וְאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא אֶת הַסָּפֵק:


כולם טהורים – that which is definite it defiles, but it doesn’t defile that which is doubtful.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:12:3)


**וַעֲשָׂאוֹ מוֹכִין.** קְרָעִים קְטַנִּים פְּחוּתִים מִשָּׁלֹשׁ עַל שָׁלֹשׁ, אֶלָּא שֶׁלֹּא הֻבְדְּלוּ לְגַמְרֵי:


ועשאו מוכין – small tears that are less than three [fingerbreadths] by three [fingerbreadths], but they were not separated completely.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:12:4)


**כֻּלָּן טְמֵאִין.** דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁנִּזְקַק לַטֻּמְאָה אֵין הַסָּפֵק מְטַהֲרוֹ:


כולן טמאין – that since it is joined to defilement/uncleanness, the doubt does not purify it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 11:12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/11:12:5)


**טָמֵא וְאָסוּר בַּהֲנָאָתוֹ.** דְּהָא שְׂרֵפָה בָעֵי:


טמא ואסור הנאתו – for it requires burning.



## Chapter 12


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:1)


**כָּל הַבָּתִּים.** וְדַוְקָא בְאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, דִּבְבֵית אֶרֶץ אֲחֻזַּתְכֶם כְּתִיב (ויקרא יד):


כל הבתים and especially in the Land of Israel, as it is written (Leviticus 14:34): “[and I inflict an eruptive plague] upon a house in the land you possess.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:2)


**חוּץ מִשֶּׁל נָכְרִים.** אִם קָנוּ בַיִת בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל. דְּהָכִי מַשְׁמַע, אֲחֻזַּתְכֶם מִטַּמְּאָה בַנְּגָעִים, וְאֵין אֲחֻזַּת הַנָּכְרִים מִטַּמְּאָה בַנְּגָעִים. וּכְשֵׁם שֶׁאֵין אֲחֻזָּתָם מִטַּמְּאָה בַנְּגָעִים, כָּךְ בִּגְדֵיהֶם וְגוּפָם אֵין מִטַּמְּאִים בַּנְּגָעִים:


חוץ משל נכרים – if they purchased a chouse in the Land of Israel. For such is implied, "אחוזתכם"/[in the land] you possess (Leviticus 14:34) is susceptible to be defiled by plagues, but the possession of heathens is not susceptible to be defiled by plagues. And just as their possession is not susceptible to be defiled by plagues, so their clothes and their bodies are not susceptible to be defiled by plagues.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:3)


**יֵרָאוּ בַתְּחִלָּה.** וְהַיָּמִים שֶׁהָיָה בָהֶם הַנֶּגַע כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהָיוּ בְיַד נָכְרִים, אֵינָם עוֹלִים לָהֶן מִן הַחֶשְׁבּוֹן:


יראו בתחילה – and the days that there was a plague in them all the while that they were in the hands of the heathens, do not count from the total amount.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:4)


**בַּיִת עָגֹל.** שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ זָוִיּוֹת:


בית עגול – that have no corners.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:5)


**טְרִיגוֹן.** שֶׁיֶּשׁ לוֹ שָׁלֹשׁ זָוִיּוֹת. שָׁלֹשׁ בִּלְשׁוֹן רוֹמִי טר"י. וְהוּא הַדִּין בַּיִת שֶׁיֶּשׁ לוֹ שְׁתֵּי זָוִיּוֹת אוֹ חָמֵשׁ:


טריגון (triangular) – that they have three corners. [The number] “three” in the Roman language is TREI. And the same law applies concerning a house that has six corners or five.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:6)


**אֵינוֹ מִטַּמֵּא בַנְּגָעִים.** דְּגַבֵּי נְגָעִים כְּתִיב קִירֹת קִירֹת שְׁתֵּי פְעָמִים, וּמִעוּט קִירוֹת שְׁנַיִם, הֲרֵי אַרְבָּעָה:


אינו מיטמא בנגעים – for concerning plagues, it is written (Leviticus 14:37 and 14:39): "קירות", "קירות" (Leviticus 14:37: "והנה הנגע בקירת הבית"/the plague in the walls of the house; Leviticus 14:39: "והנה פשה בקירת הבית"/ “[if he sees that] the plague has spread on the walls of the house) twice – and the minority/the least of [this plural] is two, hence four.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:7)


**וּבְאַסְקַרְיָא.** הוּא תֹּרֶן הַסְּפִינָה. וְיֵשׁ סְפָרִים שֶׁגּוֹרְסִים אַכְסַדְיָא, וְהֵן קוֹרוֹת שֶׁקּוֹשְׁרִים בְּיַחַד וּמוֹלִיכִין אוֹתָן בַּנְּהָרוֹת וּבַיָּם. וּבִלְשׁוֹן מִקְרָא קְרוּיִין רַפְסוֹדוֹת:


ובאסקריא (mast, yard – where the captain sits for looking out, raft) – it is the mast/yard of a ship. But there are books that the reading אכסדיא/raft and hey are the beams that they tie together and place them in the rivers and in the sea. And in the language of the Bible, they are called רפסודות (2 Chronicles 2:15): “[We undertake to cut down as many trees of Lebanon as you need, and deliver them to you] as rafts by sea to Jaffa; you will transport them to Jerusalem.”/"רפסדות על-ים יפו ואתה תעלה אתם ירושלם".


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:8)


**אֵינָן מִטַּמְּאִין בַּנְּגָעִים.** דִּכְתִיב בְּבֵית אֶרֶץ, עַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַבַּיִת מְחֻבָּר בָּאָרֶץ:


אינן מיטמאין בנגעים – as it is written (Leviticus 14:34): "בבית ארץ [אחזתכם]"/upon a house in the land that you possess”, until the house will be attached to the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:9)


**עַל אַרְבַּע קוֹרוֹת.** כְּמוֹ אוֹ עַל אַרְבַּע קוֹרוֹת. שֶׁהָיָה הַבַּיִת בָּנוּי עַל גַּבֵּי הַקּוֹרוֹת:


על ארבע קורות – like or on four beams. For the house was built on top of beams.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:1:10)


**אֲפִלּוּ עַל אַרְבָּעָה עַמּוּדִים.** שֶׁהָעַמּוּדִים מְחֻבָּרִים בָּאָרֶץ וְהֵן נֶחְשָׁבִים אַרְבַּע זָוִיּוֹת לַבַּיִת וְהַגַּג עֲלֵיהֶן, מִטַּמֵּא בַנְּגָעִים אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא פָתוּחַ מִכָּל צְדָדָיו:


אפילו על ארבעה עמודים – for the pillars are attached to the ground and they are considered four corners to the house and the roof that is upon them is susceptible to being defiled through plagues and even though it is open from all of its sides.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:2:1)


**בַּיִת שֶׁאַחַד מִצְּדָדָיו.** אֶחָד מֵאַרְבָּעָה כְתָלָיו מְחֻפֶּה בְשַׁיִשׁ מרמר"א:


בית שאחד מצדדיו – one of the four walls is covered with marble MARMARA.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:2:2)


**בְּסֶלַע.** צֹר שֶׁהוּא שָׁם מִשֵּׁשֶׁת יְמֵי בְרֵאשִׁית:


בסלע – a rock which is there from the Six Days of Creation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:2:3)


**בִּלְבֵנִים.** עֲשׂוּיִים בְּכִבְשָׁן:


בלבנים – that are made in a kiln/furnace.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:2:4)


**בְּעָפָר.** שֶׁהַכֹּתֶל עָשׂוּי מִגּוּשֵׁי עָפָר:


בעפר – that the wall is made from clods/handfuls of earth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:2:5)


**טָהוֹר.** דְּבָעִינַן שֶׁיִּהְיוּ אַרְבָּעָה קִירוֹת הַבַּיִת רְאוּיִין לִטַּמֵּא בַנְּגָעִים, וְאִם אֶחָד מִן הַקִּירוֹת עָשׂוּי בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵין רָאוּי לִטַּמֵּא, כְּגוֹן כָּל הָנֵי דְאָמְרִינַן, הַבַּיִת טָהוֹר:


טהור – that we require that the four walls of the house will be susceptible to uncleanness through plagues, but if one of the walls is made of something that is not appropriate to be made unclean, as for example, all of these that we have mentioned (i.e., stone, bricks, dirt), the house is clean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:2:6)


**בַּיִת שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ בוֹ אֲבָנִים וְעֵצִים וְעָפָר.** כָּל שְׁלָשְׁתָּן, אֶלָּא אֶחָד מֵהֶן הָיָה בוֹ, וְנִרְאָה הַנֶּגַע בְּאוֹתוֹ הָאֶחָד, טָהוֹר. דְּבָעִינַן שֶׁיְּהֵא רָאוּי לִטַּמֵּא קֹדֶם רְאִיַּת הַנֶּגַע:


בית שלא היו בו אבנים ועצים ועפר – all three of them, but rather, one of them was in it, and the plague appeared in that one, it is pure. For we require that it be appropriate to be made susceptible to uncleanness prior to seeing the plague.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:2:7)


**עַד שֶׁיִּהְיוּ בוֹ אֲבָנִים וְעֵצִים וְעָפָר.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יד) אֶת אֲבָנָיו וְאֶת עֵצָיו וְאֵת כָּל עֲפַר הַבָּיִת:


עד שיהיו בו אבנים ועצים ועפר – as it is written (Leviticus 14:45): “[The house shall be torn down - ] its stones, and timber and all the coating on the house –[and taken to an impure place outside the city].”



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:3:1)


**עַד שֶׁיֵּרָאֶה כִשְׁנֵי גְרִיסִין.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְרָאָה אֶת הַנֶּגַע וְהִנֵּה הַנֶּגַע, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁאֵין נִגְעֵי בָתִּים מִטַּמְּאִין, עַד שֶׁיֵּרָאוּ כִּשְׁנֵי גְרִיסִין:


עד שיראה כשני גריסים – as it is written (Leviticus 14:37): "וראה את-הנגע והנה הנגע בקירת הבית"/ “If, when he examines the plague, the plague in the walls of the house,” it teaches that the it is not the plagues of plagues of the house do not make it susceptible to uncleanness, until there will appear like [the size of] two split [Cilician] beans.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:3:2)


**אוֹ עַל אֶבֶן אֶחָת.** וְהַיְנוּ טַעֲמָא דְאָמַר רַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל אַרְבַּע אֲבָנִים לְאַרְבָּעָה כָתְלֵי הַבַּיִת, לְכָל כֹּתֶל אֶבֶן אֶחָת. וּלְרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא דְּאָמַר עַד שֶׁיֵּרָאוּ כִשְׁנֵי גְרִיסִים עַל שְׁתֵּי אֲבָנִים לֹא עַל אֶבֶן אַחַת, אֵין פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁמֹנֶה אֲבָנִים, לְכָל כֹּתֶל שְׁתֵּי אֲבָנִים:


או על אבל אחת – and that is the reason that Rabbi Yishmael stated four stones, for the four walls of the house, for each wall has one stone. But for Rabbi Akiba who stated, until that there should appear like [the size of] two split [Cilician] beans on two stones, not on one stone, for not less than eight stones, as each wall has two stones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:3:3)


**רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** טַעֲמָא דְרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן [בְּסַנְהֶדְרִין דַּף עא] דִּכְתִיב קִיר וּכְתִיב קִירֹת, אֵיזֶהוּ קִיר שֶׁהוּא כְקִירוֹת, הֱוֵי אוֹמֵר זֶה קֶרֶן זָוִית, וּבְכָל קִיר בָּעִינַן שִׁעוּר נֶגַע, דְּהַיְנוּ כַּגְּרִיס הַקִּלְקִי מְרֻבָּע בְּכָל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


רבי אליעזר בר' שמעון אומר כו' – the reason of Rabbi Eliezer the son of Rabbi Shimon [Tractate Sanhedrin 71a] as it is written (Leviticus 14:37): "קיר" (i.e., "ומראיהן שפל מן-הקיר"/that appear to go deep in the wall) and it is written (Leviticus 14:37): "קירות" (i.e., "והנה הנגע בקירת הבית" /the plague in the walls of the house is found), What is a wall that is like multiple walls? He would say that this the corner, and in each wall, we require the measure of a plague, and that is like the split [Cilician] bean squared on each one. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiba.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:1)


**עֵצִים.** כִּדְאָמְרִינַן שֶׁאֵין הַבַּיִת מִטַּמֵּא עַד שֶׁיִּהְיוּ בוֹ עֵצִים, כַּמָּה יִהְיוּ בְכָל כֹּתֶל וְכֹתֶל, כְּדֵי לִתֵּן תַּחַת הַשְּׁקוֹף. כָּךְ צָרִיךְ לְכָל קִיר וְקִיר:


עצים – as we have stated (see Mishnah 2 of this chapter, that a house is not susceptible to be unclean until it has wood in it, how much [wood] will be in each and every wall. כדי ליתן תחת השקוף (as much wood as is needed to place under an arch/lintel – to keep it in shape)- thus it needs for each and every wall.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:2)


**שְׁקוֹף.** מָקוֹם שֶׁהַדֶּלֶת שׁוֹקֵף עָלָיו, וּרְגִילִין לִתֵּן עֵץ תַּחְתָּיו:


שקוף – place that the door arches/bends upon it, and they regularly place wood under it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:3)


**סַנְדָּל לַאֲחוֹרֵי הַשְּׁקוֹף.** רְגִילִים לְהַנִּיחַ עֵץ לַאֲחוֹרֵי הַשְּׁקוֹף לְהָגֵן, וְנִקְרָא סַנְדָּל, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהַסַּנְדָּל מֵגֵן עַל הָרֶגֶל. שְׁקוֹף, נִקְרָא בֵּין שֶׁל פֶּתַח בֵּין שֶׁל חַלּוֹן:


סנדל לאחורי השקוף – the regularly place wood for the back of the lintel to protect it, and it is called aסנדל /sandal [support] , like that the sandal protects/supports the foot. שקוף/arch or lintel, is called whether of the opening [of the house] or the window.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:4)


**פַּצִּים.** לְשׁוֹן סֶדֶק וְקֶרַע. כְּמוֹ (תהלים ס) הִרְעַשְׁתָּה אֶרֶץ פְּצַמְתָּהּ:


פצים (boards – the board nailed against the wall to form a door-frame/door-post) – it the language of chips and tears. Like (Psalms 60:4): “You have made the land quake; You have torn it open.”/"הרעשתה ארץ פצמתה".


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:5)


**קִירוֹת הָאֵבוּס.** שֶׁל בְּהֵמָה:


קירות האבוס (walls of cattle stall) – of cattle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:6)


**וְקִירוֹת הַמְּחִצָּה.** שֶׁל (חצרות) גַּנּוֹת וּפַרְדֵּסִים:


וקירות המחיצה – of [courtyards), gardens and orchards.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:7)


**אֵין מִטַּמְּאִין בַּנְּגָעִים.** דִּכְתִיב בְּקִירֹת הַבַּיִת, וְלֹא בְּקִירוֹת הָאֵבוּס וְלֹא בְּקִירוֹת הַמְּחִצָּה:


אין מיטמאין בנגעים – as it is written (Leviticus 14:37): "בקירת-הבית"/in the walls of the house, but not the walls of the cattle stall nor the walls of the partition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:4:8)


**יְרוּשָׁלַיִם וְחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ אֵינָן מִטַּמְּאִין בַּנְּגָעִים.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יד) בְּבֵית אֶרֶץ אֲחֻזַּתְכֶם, וְחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ אֵינָהּ אֲחֻזַּתְכֶם, וִירוּשָׁלַיִם נַמִּי לֹא נִתְחַלְּקָה לַשְּׁבָטִים:


ירושלים וחוצה לארץ אינן מיטמאין בנגעים – as it is written (Leviticus 14:34): "בבית ארץ אחוזתכם"/in the land you possess, but outside the Land is not your possession, and Jerusalem also was not divided among the tribes.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:5:1)


**לֹא יִגְזֹר.** לֹא יִפְסֹק הַדַּיָּן לוֹמַר שֶׁהוּא נֶגַע וַדַּאי, מִדִּכְתִיב כְּנֶגַע נִרְאָה לִי בַּבָּיִת, וְלֹא כְתִיב נֶגַע:


לא יגזור (will not decisively rule) –the judge will not make a legal ruling to state that it is a definitive plague, from as it is written (Leviticus 14:35): "[ובא אשר-לו בבית והיגד לכהן לאמר] כנגע נראה לי בבית"/ “[The owner of the house shall come and tell the priest, saying,] ‘Something like a plague has appeared upon my house,’” but it is not written [a definitive] נגע/plague (but rather, something like a plague).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:5:2)


**אֲפִלּוּ חֲבִילֵי עֵצִים.** שֶׁאֵינָן בִּכְלַל טֻמְאָה, צָרִיךְ לְפַנּוֹת. וְהָא דִכְתִיב וְלֹא יִטְמָא כָּל אֲשֶׁר בַּבָּיִת, דְּמַשְׁמַע דְּאַטֻּמְאָה קָפֵיד רַחֲמָנָא, סְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה דְּמִלְּתָא בְאַנְפֵּי נַפְשַׁהּ הִיא, לְאַשְׁמוֹעִינַן דְּכֵלִים דַּהֲווּ מֵעִקָּרָא נַמִּי מִטַּמּוּ אִי שָׁבֵיק לְהוּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אפילו חבילי עצים (and even bundles of wood) – which are not included in the uncleanness, one must remove. But as it is written (Leviticus 14:36): "ולא יטמא כל-אשר בבית" / “so that nothing in the house may become unclean,” that implies that the All-Merciful was stringent regarding uncleanness, Rabbi Yehuda holds that this is a matter on its own , to inform us that vessels that were initially also defiled, if he left them. But the Halakah is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:5:3)


**עֵסֶק זֶה לַפִּנּוּי.** בִּתְמִיָּה, וְכִי לְהַעֲסִיקוֹ לְפַנּוֹת אֶת הַבַּיִת הֻצְרַךְ הַכָּתוּב, שֶׁאַתָּה מַזְקִיקוֹ לְפַנּוֹת דְּבָרִים שֶׁאֵין מְקַבְּלִים טֻמְאָה, עַל כָּרְחָךְ לֹא בָא הַכָּתוּב לִדְרֹשׁ אֶלָּא כְּמוֹ שֶׁדּוֹרֵשׁ רַבִּי מֵאִיר שֶׁהַתּוֹרָה חָסָה עַל מָמוֹנָן שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


עסק זה לפינוי – in astonishment, and to busy himself to empty out the house, Scripture needed that you must remove things that are not susceptible to receive defilement, by force, Scripture does not come to expound other than like Rabbi Meir expounds that the Torah has compassion on the money of Israelites.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:5:4)


**פַּכּוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן פַּכִּים קְטַנִּים:


פכו (his cruse) – the language of small cruses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:5:5)


**תִּפְיוֹ.** מָקוֹם שֶׁאוֹפֶה וּמְבַשֵּׁל בּוֹ קְדֵרָתוֹ, כְּגוֹן תַּנּוּר וְכִירַיִם שֶׁל חֶרֶס הַמִּטַּלְטְלִין. וְיֵשׁ סְפָרִים שֶׁגּוֹרְסִים טִפְיוֹ בְּטֵי"ת, וְהוּא כְּלִי חֶרֶס קָטָן מְאֹד שֶׁאֵין הַמַּשְׁקֶה יוֹצֵא הֵימֶנּוּ אֶלָּא טִיף טִיף. וְכָל אֵלּוּ אֵין לָהֶם טָהֳרָה בְמִקְוֶה:


תפיו (to set upon for cooking/ewer) – a place that one makes and cooks on it his dishes, as for example, an earthenware oven and [an earthenware] double stove that ae susceptible to become unclean. But there are books that have the reading טפיו/his stand for a portable oven/dripping, and it is a very small earthenware vessel that the liquid does not leave from it other by constant dripping. And all of these do not have purification in a Mikveh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:5:6)


**אִם כָּךְ עַל שֶׁל רָשָׁע.** שֶׁהַנְּגָעִים בָּאִים עַל לָשׁוֹן הָרָע:


וא"כ על של רשע – that the plagues come on account of gossip/evil talk.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:6:1)


**אֵינוֹ הוֹלֵךְ לְתוֹךְ בֵּיתוֹ.** דִּכְתִיב וְיָצָא הַכֹּהֵן מִן הַבַּיִת. יָכוֹל יֵלֵךְ לְבֵיתוֹ וְיַסְגִּיר, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר אֶל פֶּתַח הַבָּיִת, אִי אֶל פֶּתַח הַבַּיִת, יָכוֹל יַעֲמֹד תַּחַת הַמַּשְׁקוֹף וְיַסְגִּיר, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר וְיָצָא מִן הַבַּיִת, עַד שֶׁיֵּעָקֵר מִכָּל הַבַּיִת:


אינו הולך לתוך ביתו – as it is written (Leviticus 14:38): “The priest shall come out of the house [to the entrance of the house, and close up the house for seven days].” It is possible that he would go to his house and shut it up, the inference teaches us (Leviticus 14:38): “to the entrance of the house”/"אל-פתח הבית", or on the entrance of the house, it is possible that he would stand underneath the lintel and shut it up, as the inference teaches us (Leviticus 14:38): “The priest shall come out of the house”/"ויצא הכהן מן-הבית" until he uproots from everything in the house (see also Tractate Nedarim 56b and Hullin 10b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:6:2)


**וְלֹא סִיד מִכָּל מָקוֹם.** דִּכְתִיב וְעָפָר אַחֵר יִקַּח, וְסִיד לֹא הָוֵי עָפָר אֶלָּא אֶבֶן שְׂרֵפָה:


ולא סיד מכל מקום (and not lime from any place) – as it is written (Leviticus 14:42): “and take other coating ]and plaster the house[”/"ועפר אחר יקח [וטח את-הבית]", but he lime is not dirt, but rather burnt stone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:6:3)


**לֹא אַחַת תַּחַת שְׁתַּיִם.** אֶבֶן גְּדוֹלָה שֶׁמְּמַלְּאָה מְקוֹם הַשְּׁתַיִם:


לא אחת תחת שתים – a large stone that replaces the place of the two [stones].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:6:4)


**מִכָּאן אָמְרוּ אוֹי לָרָשָׁע וְאוֹי לִשְׁכֵנוֹ.** שֶׁאִם כֹּתֶל מַפְסִיק בֵּין שְׁנֵי בָתִּים וְנִרְאָה נֶגַע בַּכֹּתֶל לְצַד בַּיִת זֶה, בַּעַל הַבַּיִת הָאַחֵר צָרִיךְ לְהִטַּפֵּל עִמּוֹ, דִּכְתִיב וְחִלְּצוּ אֶת הָאֲבָנִים, לְשׁוֹן רַבִּים. וְלָזֶה שֶׁהַנֶּגַע לְצַד בֵּיתוֹ קוֹרֵא רָשָׁע, שֶׁהַנְּגָעִים בָּאִים עַל לָשׁוֹן הָרָע כְּדַאֲמַרַן:


מכאן אמרו אוי לרשע ואוי לשכנו – if a wall interrupts between two houses and the plague appears on the wall on the side of this house, the owner of the other house has to deal with him on this, as it is written (Leviticus 14:40): “[The priest] shall order the stones with the plague in them to be pulled out”/"[וצוה הכהן] וחלצו את-האבנים אשר בהן הנגע", in the plural language (i.e., וחלצו את האבנים – they shall pull out the stones). And the one for whom the plague is on the side of his house is called an “evil” person, for plagues come on account of gossip/evil talk, as we have stated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:6:5)


**שְׁנֵיהֶם קוֹצְעִים.** לְשׁוֹן וְאֶת הַבַּיִת יַקְצִעַ:


שניהם קוצעים (both of the neighbors must bear the expense for tearing out – the leprous stone, and for scraping the wall) – it is the language of (Leviticus 14:41): “The house shall be scraped inside all around”/”ואת-הבית יקצע מבית סביב" .


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:6:6)


**יִקַּח וְטָח.** לְשׁוֹן יָחִיד, לְמַעוֹטֵי חֲבֵרוֹ שֶׁאֵין מִטַּפֵּל עִמּוֹ בַטִּיחָה:


יקח וטח – it is singular language (Leviticus 14:42): "ועפר איר יקח וטח את-הבית"/ “and take other coating and plaster the house,” to exclude his neighbor that does not deal with him in plastering.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:7:1)


**בָּא בְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ.** אַחֲרֵי הִטּוֹחַ הַבַּיִת, חוֹזֵר וּמַסְגִּירוֹ, וְאִם בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ חָזַר הַנֶּגַע, וְנָתַץ אֶת הַבַּיִת:


בא בסוף השבוע –(Leviticus 12:48): "אחרי הטח את-בית"/ “after the house was replastered,” he (i.e., the Kohen) returns and quarantines it/shuts it up, but if at the end of the week, the plague returns, “he tears down the house,”/"ונתץ את הבית" (Leviticus 12:45).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:7:2)


**הַפִּשְׂיוֹן הַסָּמוּךְ.** שֶׁפָּשָׂה סָמוּךְ לַנֶּגַע:


הפשיון הסמוך – that it spread close/adjacent to the plague.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 12:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/12:7:3)


**וְהַחוֹזֵר כִּשְׁנֵי גְרִיסִים.** דִּכְתִיב וְאִם יָשׁוּב הַנֶּגַע, כְּאָדָם הָאוֹמֵר חָזַר פְּלוֹנִי לִמְקוֹמוֹ, וְהַנֶּגַע שֶׁבַּבָּתִּים בַּתְּחִלָּה אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא אֶלָּא בִשְׁנֵי גְרִיסִים, כְּדַאֲמַרַן:


והחוזר כשני גריסין – as it is written (Leviticus 14:43): "ואם-ישוב הנגע"/ “if the plague again breaks out in the house,” like a person who states: “so-and-so returned to his place.” But the plague that is in the houses at the outset do not other defile other than with two split [Cilician] beans in size.



## Chapter 13


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:1)


**עֲשָׂרָה בָתִּים.** הַכֵּהֶה בָרִאשׁוֹן. בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן כָּהָה, אוֹ הָלַךְ הַנֶּגַע לְגַמְרֵי. הֲרֵי שְׁנֵי בָתִּים, דְּכִשְׁנַיִם הֵן חֲשׁוּבִים:


עשרה בתים. הכהה בראשון (ten conditions which develop concerning plagues in houses) – at the end of the first week it grows dim, or the plague goes away completely. These are two plagues, for they are considered as two.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:2)


**קוֹלְפוֹ.** קוֹלֵף מְקוֹם הַנֶּגַע בִּלְבַד, וְהַבַּיִת טָהוֹר:


קולפו – he scrapes it (between one stone and another) in the place of a plague alone, and the house is pure/clean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:3)


**הַכֵּהֶה.** בְּסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי, וְהַהוֹלֵךְ לְגַמְרֵי. שְׁנַיִם אֲחֵרִים, הֲרֵי אַרְבָּעָה:


הכהה – at the end of the second week, and it goes away completely. Two others, that makes four.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:4)


**הַפּוֹשֶׂה בְּשָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן: חוֹלֵץ וְקוֹצֶה וְטָח וְנוֹתֵן לוֹ שָׁבוּעַ.** וְאִם חָזַר, נוֹתֵץ. הֲרֵי חֲמִשָּׁה:


הפושה בשבוע ראשון חולץ וקוצה וטח ונותן לו שבוע (if it spreads in the first week, he must tear out – the leprous stone – and scrape – the wall – and plaster and give it a week) – but if it (i.e., the plague) returned, he tears it down by chipping off his part of the house. This is five.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:5)


**לֹא חָזַר טָעוּן צִפֳּרִים.** הֲרֵי שִׁשָּׁה:


לא חזר טעון צפרים – this makes six.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:6)


**עָמַד.** בְּעֵינָיו בְּשָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן: **וּפָשָׂה בַשֵּׁנִי, חוֹלֵץ וְקוֹצֶה וְטָח וְנוֹתֵן לוֹ שָׁבוּעַ.** חָזַר, נוֹתֵץ. הֲרֵי שִׁבְעָה:


עמד בעיניו בשבוע ראשון ופשה בשני חולץ וקוצה וטח ונותן לו שבוע – if it returned, he tears it down by chipping. This makes seven.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:7)


**לֹא חָזַר טָעוּן צִפֳּרִים.** הֲרֵי שְׁמֹנָה:


לא חזר טעון צפרים – this makes eight.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:8)


**עָמַד.** בְּעֵינָיו בְּשָׁבוּעַ רִאשׁוֹן וּבְשָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי: **חוֹלֵץ וְקוֹצֶה וְטָח וְנוֹתֵן לוֹ שָׁבוּעַ.** שֶׁדִּין הָעוֹמֵד בְּעֵינָיו בְּשָׁבוּעַ שֵׁנִי כְּדִין הַפּוֹשֶׂה. חָזַר, נוֹתֵץ. הֲרֵי תִשְׁעָה:


עמד בעיניו בשבוע ראשון ובשבוע שני, חולץ וקוצה וטח ונותן לו שבוע – that the law for remaining unchanged in the second week is like the law of spreading, if it returned, he tears it down by chipping. This is nine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:9)


**לֹא חָזַר טָעוּן צִפֳּרִים.** הֲרֵי עֲשָׂרָה:


לא חזר טעון צפרים – this makes ten.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:1:10)


**אִם עַד שֶׁלֹּא טִהֲרוֹ בַצִּפֳּרִים נִרְאָה בוֹ נֶגַע.** הֲרֵי הוּא כְּחוֹזֵר, וְטָעוּן נְתִיצָה. אֲבָל מִשֶּׁטִּהֲרוֹ, הָוֵי לֵיהּ כְּנֶגַע חָדָשׁ וּמַסְגִּירוֹ:


אם עד שלא טיהרו בצפרים נראה בו נגע – this is like [the plague] returns, and requires cutting out/breaking up, but from when he purifies it, it is for him like a new plague and he shuts it up/quarantines it.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:2:1)


**הָאֶבֶן שֶׁבַּזָּוִית.** שֶׁהִיא בֵינוֹ וּבֵין חֲבֵרוֹ בְּשֻׁתָּפוּת:


האבן שבזוית – that is between him and his neighbor jointly.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:2:2)


**חוֹלֵץ אֶת כֻּלּוֹ.** כִּדְדָרְשִׁינַן וְחִלְּצוּ, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם חוֹלְצִים:


חולץ את כולו – for when they (i.e., the Rabbis) expound "וחלצו /]“the priest shall order the stones with the plague in them] to be pulled out,” (Leviticus 13:40 – with a plural construction), teaching that both of them pull out/tear out [the stones].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:2:3)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהוּא נוֹתֵץ.** אֶת הַבַּיִת כֻּלּוֹ:


בזמן שהוא נותץ (and at the time when he has to tear down – see Leviticus 13:45) – the entire house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:2:4)


**נוֹתֵץ שֶׁלּוֹ.** דְּגַבֵּי נְתִיצָה כְּתִיב אֶת אֲבָנָיו וְאֶת עֵצָיו, אֲבָנָיו שֶׁלּוֹ וְעֵצָיו שֶׁלּוֹ:


נותץ שלו – for concerning cutting out/breaking up, it is written (Leviticus 13:45): “its stones and timber”/”את אבניו ואת עציו" , its stones that are his and its timber that is his.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:2:5)


**רֹאשׁ וּפִתִּין.** פֵּרֵשׁ הֶעָרוּךְ, אוֹתָהּ הָאֶבֶן אוֹ אוֹתוֹ הָעֵץ שֶׁתּוֹפֵס מִצַּד מַעֲרָב נִדְבָּךְ שֶׁלּוֹ, וּלְצַד מִזְרָח נִדְבָּךְ שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ, שֵׁם אוֹתָהּ הָאֶבֶן אוֹ אוֹתוֹ הָעֵץ, רֹאשׁ. וְאוֹתָם הָאֲבָנִים וְעֵצִים שֶׁבְּנִדְבָּךְ זֶה בַּמַּעֲרַב אַחַת, וּבְנִדְבָּךְ זֶה בַּמִּזְרָח אַחַת, שְׁמָן פִּתִּין:


ראש ופתין (if a [twin] house is built with cross-beam and king-beam/principal beam common to both compartments) – it explains in the Arukh, that particular stone or that piece of wood that takes hold from the western side of the course of stones is his, and of the eastern side, the course of stones is his neighbors, he put the stone or that piece of wood is the cross beam. And those stones and pieces of wood that are in this course of stones on the west is one, and that course of stones in the east is one, for they are from the principal beam common to both compartments.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:3:1)


**נוֹתֵן אֶת הַקּוֹרוֹת לָעֲלִיָּה.** וְסוֹמְכָן בָּעַמּוּדִין וְאֵינוֹ נוֹתְצָן עִם הַבַּיִת, דִּכְתִיב אֶת אֲבָנָיו וְאֶת עֵצָיו וְאֵת [כָּל] עֲפַר הַבָּיִת, וְלֹא אֲבָנִים וְעֵצִים וְעָפָר שֶׁל עֲלִיָּה:


נותן את הקורות לעלייה – and he supports them with pillars and does not tear them down with the house, as it is written (Leviticus 13:45): “its stones and timber and all the coating on the house”/את-אבניו ואת-עציו ואת כל-עפר הבית", but not the stones and timber and coating of the upper story.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:3:2)


**נִתָּצִין עִמּוֹ.** דְּדָרְשִׁינַן אֶת אֲבָנָיו וְאֶת עֵצָיו וְאֶת עֲפַר הַבָּיִת, כָּל אֲבָנִים שֶׁנִּבְנוּ עִמּוֹ וְכָל עֵצִים שֶׁנִּבְנוּ עִמּוֹ וְכָל עָפָר שֶׁנִּבְנָה עִמּוֹ. וְכֵיוָן דְּאֵין עֲלִיָּה עַל גַּבָּיו, הֲרֵי כֻלָּם נִבְנוּ עִמּוֹ:


נתצין עמו (are torn down with it) – for [the Rabbis] expound on: "את-אבניו ואת-עציו ואת כל-עפר הבית"/ “its stones and timber and all the coating on the house” (Leviticus 13:45), all the stones that were built with it and all the pieces of wood that were built with it and all of the coating that was built with it,” for since there is no upper story on top it, all of them were built with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:3:3)


**מַלְבְּנִים.** כְּמִין לְבֵנִים מְרֻבָּעוֹת שֶׁשּׁוֹטְחִים עַל הַגַּגּוֹת. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים, כְּעֵין מְזוּזוֹת שֶׁעוֹשִׂים בַּחַלּוֹנוֹת מִבַּחוּץ לְנוֹי, וְאֵין מְחֻבָּרִים בְּטִיט בַּחַלּוֹן:


מלבנים (a casing to protect that portion of the beam which rests on the wall) – kinds of square bricks that one spreads out/stretches on the roofs. But there are those who explain that they are similar to door-posts that they make in windows from the outside for beauty, but they are not attached with plaster on the window.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:3:4)


**שְׂרִיגוֹת.** כְּמִין סְבָכָה שֶׁעוֹשִׂים בַּחַלּוֹן שֶׁמִּסְתַּכְּלִים מִשָּׁם לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


שריגות (lattices) – like a kind of net-work that they make in the window that they look out from there to the public domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:3:5)


**מַלְבֵּן.** עֵץ אָרֹךְ שֶׁעַל הַקִּיר הֶעָשׂוּי לְהוֹשִׁיב עָלָיו רָאשֵׁי הַקּוֹרוֹת שֶׁלֹּא יֵרָקְבוּ מִלַּחְלוּחִית הַחוֹמָה:


מלבן (frame)– a long piece of wood that is on the wall that is made to hold upon it the heads of the beams that they should not rot from the moisture of the wall.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:3:6)


**בְּכַזָּיִת.** מִשּׁוּם דְּצָרַעַת אִתַּקַּשׁ לְמֵת, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יב) אַל נָא תְהִי כַּמֵּת:


בכזית – because leprosy is made an analogy to a corpse, as it is written (Numbers 12:12): “Let her (i.e., Miriam) not be as one dead, [who emerges from his mother’s womb with half his flesh eaten away].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:3:7)


**בְּמַשֶּׁהוּ.** כְּאֵבָרִים שֶׁל מֵת, דִּתְנַן בְּאֳהָלוֹת, הָאֵבָרִים אֵין לָהֶם שִׁעוּר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר:


במשהו – like the limbs of a corpse, as it is taught in [Tractate] Ahilot [Chapter 1, Mishnah 7]. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:4:1)


**בַּיִת הַמֻּסְגָּר מְטַמֵּא מִתּוֹכוֹ.** כְּדִכְתִיב וְהַבָּא אֶל הַבַּיִת כָּל יְמֵי הִסְגִּיר אֹתוֹ יִטְמָא עַד הָעָרֶב. וְהָאֲבָנִים הַמְנֻגָּעִים עַצְמָן, אֲפִלּוּ בְמֻסְגָּר מְטַמְּאִין מֵאֲחוֹרֵיהֶן:


בית המוסגר מטמא מתוכו – as it is written (Leviticus 14:46): “Whoever enters the house while it is closed up shall be impure until evening.” But the leprous stones themselves, even when locked up, defile from their outsides.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:4:2)


**וְהַמֻּחְלָט מִתּוֹכוֹ וּמֵאֲחוֹרָיו.** הַנּוֹגֵעַ בַּבַּיִת מֵאֲחוֹרָיו, טָמֵא, דִּכְתִיב בְּמֻחְלָט, צָרַעַת מַמְאֶרֶת בַּבַּיִת טָמֵא הוּא, מַה תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר טָמֵא הוּא, הוֹסִיף לוֹ שֶׁיְּהֵא טָמֵא בֵּין מִתּוֹכוֹ בֵּין מֵאֲחוֹרָיו:


והמוחלט מתוכו ומאחוריו – a person who touches/comes in contact with a house from its outside, is impure. As it is written regarding a [house] that is certified unclean (Leviticus 14:44): "צרעת ממארת היא בבית טמא הוא"/”it is a malignant eruption in the house; it is impure.” What does the inference of "טמא הוא" /”it is impure” teach us? It adds that a person is impure whether from the inside or whether from the outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:4:3)


**זֶה וָזֶה מְטַמְּאִין בְּבִיאָה.** לְטַמֵּא כָּל הַנִּכְנָס בְּתוֹכוֹ:


זה וזה מטמאין בביאה – to defile all who enters inside of it.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:5:1)


**הַבּוֹנֶה מִן הַמֻּסְגָּר בְּטָהוֹר.** לְאַחַר שֶׁחָלַץ וְקָצַע וְטָח וְנָתַן לוֹ שָׁבוּעַ, בְּתוֹךְ יְמֵי הֶסְגֵּרוֹ לָקַח מֵאוֹתוֹ בַיִת אֲבָנִים וּבְנָאָן בְּבַיִת אַחֵר:


הבונה מן המוסגר בטהור – after he had torn out and scraped and [re-] plastered and gave it a week, within the days of [the house] being shut up he took from that same house stones and built them in another house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:5:2)


**חָזַר נֶגַע לַבַּיִת.** הַמֻּסְגָּר:


וחזר נגע לבית – that was shut up/quarantined.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:5:3)


**חוֹלֵץ אֶת הָאֲבָנִים.** שֶׁלָּקַח מִמֶּנּוּ וּמְסַלְּקָן מִן הַטָּהוֹר, וְהֵן טְמֵאִים כְּטֻמְאַת הַבַּיִת שֶׁנְּטָלָן מִמֶּנּוּ:


חולץ את האבנים – that he took from it, and removes them from the pure [house], and they are impure like the uncleanness of the house that he took them from.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:5:4)


**חָזַר הַנֶּגַע לָאֲבָנִים.** שֶׁנִּבְנָה בְטָהוֹר:


חזר הנגע לאבנים – that he built in the pure [new] house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:5:5)


**הַבַּיִת.** הַמֻּסְגָּר יִנָּתֵץ, כְּאִלּוּ הָיוּ קְבוּעִים בּוֹ. וְהָנֵי אֲבָנִים נַמִּי בָּעוּ נְתִיצָה, אֶלָּא שֶׁמַּמְתִּין לָהֶם עַד שֶׁיְּשַׁמְּשׁוּ הַבַּיִת הַשֵּׁנִי בְּסִימָנִין, דְּטָעוּן הֶסְגֵּר, וְדִינוֹ לְכָל דָּבָר כְּכָל בַּיִת שֶׁנִּרְאָה בוֹ נֶגַע בַּתְּחִלָּה:


הבית – that is shut up, should be torn down/chipped off, as if they were fixed in it. And they stones also require tearing out, but that we wait for them until they will serve in the second house [for the purpose of inspecting whether] tokens [of uncleanness recur in the second house] , that require shutting up/quarantining , and its law for every manner like every house in which a plague appears at the outset.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:6:1)


**בַּיִת שֶׁהוּא מֵסֵךְ עַל גַּבֵּי בַיִת הַמְנֻגָּע.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁבַּיִת הַמְנֻגָּע בָּנוּי בְּתוֹךְ בַּיִת אַחֵר. וְכֵן אִילָן שֶׁבַּיִת הַמְנֻגָּע בָּנוּי תַּחְתָּיו:


בית שהוא מיסך על גבי בית המנוגע – as for example, that the house that is certified as leprous is built within another house. And similarly, a tree that the leprous house is built underneath it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:6:2)


**הַנִּכְנָס לַחִיצוֹן טָהוֹר.** דְּהַאי לְחוֹדֵיהּ קָאֵי וְהַאי לְחוֹדֵיהּ קָאֵי. וְדָמֵי לְבַיִת וַעֲלִיָּה, דְּאִי קָאֵי מְצֹרָע בַּבַּיִת, עֲלִיָּה טְהוֹרָה:


הנכנס לחיצון טהור – for this one stands alone and that one stands alone. And it is similar to a house and the upper story, for if leprosy is in the house, the upper story is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:6:3)


**וּמָה אִם אֶבֶן אַחַת.** שֶׁמִּבַּיִת הַמְנֻגָּע, מְטַמְּאָה כָּל אֲשֶׁר בָּאֹהֶל:


ומה אם אבן אחת – that is from the house with leprosy, which defiles all that is in the tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:6:4)


**הַבַּיִת כֻּלּוֹ.** שֶׁהוּא תַּחַת בַּיִת אַחֵר, לֹא כָל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁיְּטַמֵּא כָּל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹךְ הַחִיצוֹן. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


הבית כולו – that is underneath another house, all the more so that it would defile all that is within the outer house. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Eliezer (see also Tractate Negaim, Chapter 11, Mishnah 6).



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:7:1)


**וְהַטָּהוֹר עוֹבֵר טָמֵא.** דִּכְתִיב בִּמְצֹרָע, מִחוּץ לַמַּחֲנֶה מוֹשָׁבוֹ, מִכָּאן שֶׁמּוֹשָׁבוֹ טָמֵא. הִלְכָּךְ כְּשֶׁהַטָּמֵא עוֹמֵד תַּחַת הָאִילָן, נַעֲשֶׂה מְקוֹם הָאִילָן מוֹשָׁבוֹ וְהַטָּהוֹר הָעוֹבֵר שָׁם טָמֵא. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁהַטָּמֵא עוֹבֵר וְאֵינוֹ עוֹמֵד לָפוּשׁ שָׁם, אֵין זֶה קָרוּי מוֹשָׁבוֹ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַטָּהוֹר יוֹשֵׁב שָׁם, טָהוֹר וְאֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה:


והטהור עובר טמא – as it is written regarding the leper (Leviticus 13:46): “[Being impure, he shall dwell apart;] his dwelling shall be outside the camp.”/ "מחוץ למחנה מושבו" , from here that his dwelling is impure, therefore, when the impure individual stands underneath the tree, the place of the tree becomes his dwelling place and the pure person that passes there is impure. But when the impure person passes and doesn’t stand to rest himself, this is not called his dwelling, and even though that the pure person sits there, the pure person is not susceptible to receive defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:7:2)


**וְאִם עָמַד.** הַטָּמֵא, נִטְמָא מְקוֹם הָאִילָן וְנִטְמָא הַטָּהוֹר הָעוֹבֵר שָׁם:


ואם עמד – the impure person, the place of the tree is defiled and the pure person is defiled who passes there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:7:3)


**וְכֵן בְּאֶבֶן הַמְנֻגַּעַת.** אִם אָדָם הַנּוֹשְׂאָהּ עוֹבֵר תַּחַת הָאִילָן, הַטָּהוֹר הָעוֹמֵד שָׁם לֹא נִטְמָא:


וכן באבן המנוגע – if a person who carries it (i.e., a leprous stone) passes underneath the tree, the pure person standing there is not defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:7:4)


**וְאִם הִנִּיחָהּ טָמֵא.** וְהוּא הַדִּין אֲפִלּוּ לֹא הִנִּיחָהּ, אֶלָּא שֶׁעָמַד הַנּוֹשְׂאָהּ:


ואם הניחה טמא – and the same law applies even if he did not place it down, but rather that the person carrying it stood.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:8:1)


**טָהוֹר שֶׁהִכְנִיס רֹאשׁוֹ וְרֻבּוֹ.** דִּבְצִיר מֵרֹאשׁוֹ וְרֻבּוֹ לָא חֲשִׁיבָא בִיאָה:


טהור שהכניס ראשו ורובו – for less than one’s head and the majority of one’s body is not considered coming in.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:8:2)


**שֶׁהִכְנִיס מִמֶּנָּה שָׁלֹשׁ אֶצְבָּעוֹת עַל שָׁלֹשׁ אֶצְבָּעוֹת.** חֲשִׁיבָא כְּאִלּוּ נִכְנְסָה כָּל הַטַּלִּית כֻּלָּהּ. אֲבָל פָּחוֹת מִכֵּן לֹא, דִּבְצִיר מִשָּׁלֹשׁ עַל שָׁלֹשׁ אֵינָהּ מְקַבֶּלֶת טֻמְאָה וְלָא חֲשִׁיבָא:


שהכניס ממנה שלש אצבעות על שלש אצבעות – it is considered as if all of the cloak had entered. But not less than this, for less than three [fingerbreadths] by three [fingerbreadths] is not susceptible to receive defilement and is not considered.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:8:3)


**וּטְמֵאָה.** כְּגוֹן טַלִּית הַמְנֻגַּעַת שֶׁהִכְנִיס מִמֶּנָּה כַזַּיִת לְבַיִת טָהוֹר:


וטמאה – as for example, a cloak afflicted with leprosy that an olive’s bulk of it entered into a pure house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:8:4)


**טִמְּאַתּוּ.** דְּצָרַעַת אִתַּקַּשׁ לְמֵת. וְטַלִּית הַמְנֻגַּעַת דִּמְטַמְּאָה בְּבִיאָה נַפְקָא לָן מִקְּרָא דִּכְתִיב [וַיִּקְרָא יד] וּלְצָרַעַת הַבֶּגֶד וְלַבָּיִת, מַקִּישׁ בֶּגֶד לְבַיִת, מַה בַּיִת מְטַמֵּא בְּבִיאָה דְּהָא כְתִיב [שָׁם] וְהַבָּא אֶל הַבַּיִת וְגוֹ', אַף בֶּגֶד מְטַמֵּא בְּבִיאָה. מִנַּיִן לְרַבּוֹת אֶת כֻּלָּן כְּגוֹן שְׁתִי וָעֵרֶב וְעוֹרוֹת, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר [שָׁם יג] זֹאת תּוֹרַת נֶגַע צָרַעַת בֶּגֶד הַצֶּמֶר, מַקִּישׁ כֻּלָּן לְבֶגֶד, מַה בֶּגֶד מְטַמֵּא בְּבִיאָה, אַף כֻּלָּן מְטַמְּאִין בְּבִיאָה:


טמאתו – for an analogy is made between leprosy and a corpse. But a leprous cloak that became defiled through oming in, we derive if from Scripture, as it is written (Leviticus 14:55): “for an eruption on a cloth or a house.”/"ולצרעת הבגד והבית", an analogy is made from a cloth to a house, for just as aa house defiles when one enters it, for it is written (Leviticus 14:46): “Whoever enters the house”/"והבא אל הבית וגו' ", even a cloth defiles through entry. From where do we learn that this includes everything as for example warp and woof and hides, the inference teaches us (Leviticus 13:59): "זאת תורת נגע-צרעת בגד הצמר [או הפשתים או השתי או הערב או כל כלי-עור לטהרו או לטמאו]."/ “Such is the procedure for eruptive affections of cloth, [woolen or linen, in warp or in woof, or of any article of skin, for pronouncing it pure or impure.],” an analogy is made between all of them to a cloth, just as a cloth defiles through entry, so all of them defile through entry.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:9:1)


**וְכֵלָיו עַל כְּתֵפוֹ.** מְקֻפָּלִים וּמֻנָּחִים עַל כְּתֵפוֹ דֶּרֶךְ מַשְּׂאוֹי:


וכליו על כתפו – folded and placed on his shoulder in the manner of a burden [to carry].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:9:2)


**וְטַבְּעוֹתָיו בְּיָדָיו.** מֻנָּחִים עַל פַּס יָדוֹ שֶׁלֹּא כְדֶרֶךְ מַלְבּוּשׁ:


וטבעותיו בידיו – placed in the palm of his hand that is not according to the manner of dressing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:9:3)


**הוּא וָהֵן טְמֵאִין מִיָּד.** דְּקָרִינַן נַמִּי בְכֵלִים וְהַבָּא אֶל הַבַּיִת יִטְמָא:


הוא והן טמאין מיד – for we call them also with vessels (Leviticus 14:46): “Whoever enters the house [while it is closed up] shall be impure [until evening].”/"והבא אל-הבית...יטמא" .


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:9:4)


**הָיָה לָבוּשׁ בְּכֵלָיו.** דְּהַשְׁתָּא אֵין כֵּלָיו טְמֵאִים אֶלָּא מֵחֲמַת דִּידֵיהּ:


היה לבוש כליו – that now his vessels ae not impure, other than on account of himself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:9:5)


**וְהֵן טְהוֹרִים עַד שֶׁיִּשְׁהֶה בִּכְדֵי אֲכִילַת פְּרָס.** דִּכְתִיב וְהָאֹכֵל בַּבַּיִת יְכַבֵּס אֶת בְּגָדָיו וְהַשֹּׁכֵב בַּבַּיִת יְכַבֵּס אֶת בְּגָדָיו, וְתַנְיָא, אֵין לִי אֶלָּא אוֹכֵל וְשׁוֹכֵב, לֹא אוֹכֵל וְלֹא שׁוֹכֵב מִנַּיִן, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר יְכַבֵּס בְּגָדָיו, רִבָּה. אִם כֵּן לָמָּה נֶאֱמָר אוֹכֵל וְשׁוֹכֵב, לִתֵּן שִׁעוּר לַבָּא אֶל הַבַּיִת שֶׁאֵין טָעוּן כִּבּוּס בְּגָדִים עַד שֶׁיִּשְׁהֶה כְּדֵי שִׁעוּר אֲכִילָה כְּשֶׁהוּא שׁוֹכֵב, כְּלוֹמַר כְּשֶׁהוּא מֵסֵב. וְכַמָּה שִׁעוּר אֲכִילָה, כְּדֵי אֲכִילַת פְּרָס, שֶׁהוּא חֲצִי כִכָּר שֶׁל עֵרוּב, לְדִבְרֵי רַשִׁ"י הֵם אַרְבַּע בֵּיצִים, וּלְדִבְרֵי רַמְבַּ"ם שָׁלֹשׁ בֵּיצִים:


והן טהורים עד שישהה בכדי אכילת פרס – as it is written (Leviticus 14:47): “Whoever sleeps in the house must wash his clothes, and whoever eats in the house must wash his clothes.” And it is taught in a Baraita: I have established only eating and sleeping, but from where can I derive that not eating or not sleeping [is included]? The inference comes to teach us, "יכבס את-בגדיו"/he must wash his clothes – is an inclusion (i.e., widening the scope of the Halakha), if so, why does it state (in Leviticus 14:47): "השכב בבית...והאכל בבית"/one who sleeps in the house….and one who eats in the house? To give a measurement to (Leviticus 13:46): "והבא אל-הבית"/whoever enters the house, which does not require washing of clothes until he remains there long enough eats while he is lying down, meaning to say, when he is reclining. And how much is the measurement of eating? In order to eat a piece of bread, which is one-half of a loaf of an Eruv (see Tractate Eruvin 8b and the dispute between Rashi and Maimonides; according to Rabbi Yohanan ben Beroka, a full loaf is six eggs and half of it is three eggs; according to Rabbi Shimon, the loaf is eight eggs and half of it is four eggs; Maimonides decides according to Rabbi Yohanan ben Beroka, whereas Rashi decides like Rabbi Shimon). According to the words of Rashi, they are four eggs, and according to the words of Maimonides they are three eggs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:9:6)


**מֵסֵב וְאוֹכֵל בְּלִפְתָּן.** שֶׁשִּׁעוּרוֹ מֻעָט מִשֶּׁאִלּוּ [הָיָה] אוֹכְלָן בְּלֹא לִפְתָּן, שֶׁמִּתּוֹךְ טַעַם שֶׁל לִפְתָּן מְמַהֵר לֶאֱכֹל:


מיסב ואוכלן בלפתן (sits and eats them with their condiments) – that its measure is less than if he [would] eat them without their condiments, that through the taste of their condiments, he hurries to eat.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:10:1)


**הָיָה עוֹמֵד בִּפְנִים.** תּוֹךְ בַּיִת הַמְנֻגָּע:


היה עומד בפנים – within the leprous house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:10:2)


**פָּשַׁט יָדָיו לַחוּץ וְטַבְּעוֹתָיו בְּיָדָיו.** הָכָא מַיְרֵי כְּשֶׁטַּבְּעוֹתָיו בְּאֶצְבְּעוֹתָיו דֶּרֶךְ מַלְבּוּשׁ, וְאִם שָׁהָה כְּדֵי אֲכִילַת פְּרָס, טְמֵאוֹת, דְּבָתַר גּוּפוֹ גְּרִירִי. וְאִם הָיוּ בְּפַס יָדוֹ דֶּרֶךְ מַשְּׂאוֹי, מוֹכִיחַ בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא שֶׁהֵם טְמֵאוֹת מִיָּד:


ופשט ידיו לחוץ וטבעותיו בידיו – here we are speaking that his rings are on his fingers like he wears them, and if he delayed in order to eat a piece of bread, they are impure, for they are clinging to his body, but if they were in the palm of his hand in the manner of burden, the Tosefta (Tractate Negaim, Chapter 7, Halakha 9) proves that they are impure immediately.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:10:3)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁאֵין כָּל גּוּפוֹ טָמֵא.** כְּגוֹן הָכָא דְּהוּא עוֹמֵד בַּחוּץ וְלֹא נִטְמָא כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא הִכְנִיס רֹאשׁוֹ וְרֻבּוֹ:


בזמן שאין כל גופו טמא – as for example, here when he stands outside and he is not defiled all the while that he did not put his head and the greater part of his body inside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:10:4)


**אֵינוֹ דִין שֶׁלֹּא יְטַמֵּא אֶת מַה שֶּׁעָלָיו עַד שֶׁיִּשְׁהֶה כְדֵי אֲכִילַת פְּרָס.** וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר, מָצִינוּ שֶׁיָּפֶה כֹחַ הַטָּמֵא לְהַצִּיל, מִכֹּחַ הַטָּהוֹר לְהַצִּיל, שֶׁהֲרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁמְּקַבְּלִים טֻמְאָה מַצִּילִין כֵּלִים בַּבַּיִת הַמְנֻגָּע, שֶׁאֵין מִטַּמְּאִין עַד שֶׁיִּשְׁהֶה כְּדֵי אֲכִילַת פְּרָס, וְנָכְרִי וּבְהֵמָה שֶׁאֵין מְקַבְּלִים טֻמְאָה אֵין מַצִּילִין כֵּלִים בַּבַּיִת הַמְנֻגָּע, שֶׁהַבְּגָדִים שֶׁעֲלֵיהֶם טְמֵאִים מִיָּד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אינו דין שלא יטמא מה שעליו עד שישהה כדי אכילת פרס – And Rabbi Yehuda states, we found that the power of something impure to protect is greater than the power of something pure to protect, for an Israelite when they become susceptible to impurity, they protect vessels in the leprous house, for they don’t defile until he remains enough time to eat a piece of bread, but the heathen and cattle which are not susceptible to receive impurity, they do not protect vessels in a leprous house, for the clothing that is upon them are impure immediately. But the Halakha Is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:11:1)


**עַד אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת.** קָסָבַר רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן, לְמַעְלָה מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת רְשׁוּתָא אַחֲרִיתָא הוּא, דְּקוֹמָתוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם בֵּינוֹנִי שָׁלֹשׁ אַמּוֹת, וְאַמָּה אַחַת לְפִשּׁוּט יָדַיִם וְרַגְלַיִם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


עד ארבע אמות – Rabbi Shimon holds that above four cubits is another domain, for the height of an average person is three cubits, and one cubit is for the prostration accompanied by stretched of hands and feet. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:11:2)


**כֵּלִים מִיָּד טְמֵאִים.** כְּלוֹמַר כֵּלִים שֶׁאָמַרְנוּ עַד הַקּוֹרוֹת לְתַנָּא קַמָּא וְעַד אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת לְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן, מִיָּד הֵן מִטַּמְּאִין כְּשֶׁנִּכְנָס עִמָּהֶן לַבַּיִת הַמְנֻגָּע:


כלים מיד טמאים – meaning to say, vessels that we stated even up until the beams according to the first Tanna/teacher, and until four cubits according to Rabbi Shimon, immediately become susceptible to impurity when he enters with them into the leprous house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:11:3)


**כְּדֵי הַדְלָקַת הַנֵּר.** כְּשֶׁנִּכְנַס בִּרְשׁוּת, מוֹדֶה רַבִּי יְהוּדָה דְּמִטַּמְּאִין מִיָּד, וְלֹא פָלֵיג אֶלָּא כְּשֶׁנִּכְנַס שֶׁלֹּא בִרְשׁוּת. מִשּׁוּם דְּמוֹשָׁבוֹ שֶׁל מְצֹרָע מְטַמֵּא כְּדַאֲמַרַן, וּכְשֶׁנִּכְנַס בִּרְשׁוּת הָוֵי מוֹשָׁבוֹ מִיָּד, אֲבָל כְּשֶׁנִּכְנַס שֶׁלֹּא בִּרְשׁוּת לֹא הָוֵי מוֹשָׁבוֹ אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן שָׁהָה כְּדֵי הַדְלָקַת הַנֵּר וְלֹא אַפְּקֵיהּ. וְאִם לֹא שָׁהָה כְּדֵי הַדְלָקַת הַנֵּר, לֹא הָוֵי מוֹשָׁבוֹ. דְּהַאי דְּלֹא אַפְּקֵיהּ, מִשּׁוּם דְּטָרִיד בְּהַדְלָקַת הַנֵּר דְּבֵינֵי שִׁמְשֵׁי אוֹ דִּימוֹת הַחֹל וְלֹא מָצֵי מַפִּיק לֵיהּ:


כדי הדלקת הנר – when he enters with permission, Rabbi Yehuda agrees/admits that the become susceptible to impurity immediately, but he doesn’t dispute other than when he enters without permission, because the dwelling of a leper defiles as we stated (earlier in this Mishnah), and when he enters with permission it is his dwelling immediately, but when he enters without permission, it is not his dwelling unless he remains enough time to light a candle and it doesn’t go out. But if he didn’t remain enough time in order to light a candle, it is not his dwelling, and that fact that [the master] throw him out is because he was preoccupied in lighting the candle because it was the onset of the Sabbath or on weekdays, and he was not able to bring it forth.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:12:1)


**נִכְנַס לְבֵית הַכְּנֶסֶת.** מְצֹרָע שֶׁבָּא לִכָּנֵס לְבֵית הַכְּנֶסֶת:


נכנס בית הכנסת – a leper that comes to enter into a synagogue.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:12:2)


**עוֹשִׂים לוֹ מְחִצָּה גְבוֹהָה עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים.** דְּפָחוֹת מֵעֲשָׂרָה אֵינָהּ חֲשׁוּבָה מְחִצָּה לְהַצִּיל:


עושים לו מחיצה גבוהה עשרה טפחים – for less than ten [handbreadths] is not considered a partition to protect.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:12:3)


**בְּרֹחַב אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת.** כְּנֶגֶד אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת שֶׁל תְּפִלָּה. אִי נַמִּי, מִשּׁוּם דְּזֶהוּ שִׁעוּר מְקוֹמוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בְּעֵרוּבִין בְּפֶרֶק מִי שֶׁהוֹצִיאוּהוּ [דַּף מח]:


ברוחב ארבע אמות – corresponding to the four cubits of prayer (see Tractate Berakhot 31b). Alternatively, because this is the measurement of the place of a person, as we stated in [Tractate] Eruvin, in the chapter מי שהוציאוהו /He who took forth beyond the Sabbath limits [folio 48a].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:12:4)


**נִכְנָס רִאשׁוֹן וְיוֹצֵא אַחֲרוֹן.** כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִטַּמְּאוּ הָעוֹמְדִים בְּבֵית הַכְּנֶסֶת בִּכְנִיסָתוֹ וּבִיצִיאָתוֹ, כְּשֶׁהוּא עוֹבֵר מִן הַפֶּתַח וְעַד הַמְּחִצָּה. דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא יַעֲמֹד מְעַט בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁהוּא עוֹבֵר, וְיִטַּמְּאוּ כָּל הָעוֹמְדִים שָׁם:


נכנס ראשון ויוצא אחרון – in order that those who are standing in the synagogue do not become defiled through his entrance and his departure, when he passes from the entrance until the partition, for we are concerned lest he stand a bit at the time that he passes, and all of those standing there will be defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:12:5)


**כֹּל הַמַּצִּיל בְּצָמִיד פָּתִיל.** כְּגוֹן כְּלִי חֶרֶס וְכָל הַנְהוּ דַחֲשִׁיב בְּמַסֶּכֶת כֵּלִים פֶּרֶק וְאֵלּוּ כֵלִים מַצִּילִים:


כל המציל בצמיד פתיל – as for example an earthenware vessel and all of those are considered in Tractate Kelim in Chapter 10: “Which are the vessels that are protected by a tightly fitting lid?”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 13:12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/13:12:6)


**כֹּל הַמַּצִּיל מְכֻסֶּה בְאֹהֶל הַמֵּת.** כְּגוֹן בּוֹר וְדוּת דְּלֹא בָעֵי צָמִיד פָּתִיל אֶלָּא כִסּוּי, כְּדִתְנַן בְּמַסֶּכֶת אֳהָלוֹת פֶּרֶק הַתַּנּוּר (משנה ו). וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


כל המציל מכוסה אוהל המת – such as a pit and a cistern that don’t require a tightly fit lid but rather a covering, as is taught in Tractate Ahilot, Chapter 5 [The Oven] (Mishnah 6). And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.



## Chapter 14


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:1)


**כֵּיצַד מְטַהֲרִין.** פְיָלִי. מִזְרָק. הַשֹּׁתִים בְּמִזְרְקֵי יַיִן (עמוס ו) מְתַרְגְּמִינַן דְּשָׁתַיִן בִּפְיָלֵי חֲמַר:


כיצד מטהרין. פיילי (a broad, flat bowl) – a bowl (out of which the sprinkling is done). (Amos 6:6): “They drink [straight] from the wine bowls [And anoint themselves with the choicest oils – But they are not concerned about the ruin of Joseph],” which we translate into Aramaic, that they drink from bowls of wine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:2)


**חֲדָשָׁה.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יד) אֶל כְּלִי חֶרֶשׂ עַל מַיִם, מַקִּישׁ כְּלִי לְמַיִם, מַה מַּיִם שֶׁלֹּא נַעֲשָׂה בָהֶן מְלָאכָה, אַף כְּלִי שֶׁלֹּא נַעֲשָׂה בוֹ מְלָאכָה:


חדשה – as it is written (Leviticus 14:5): “[The priest shall order one of the birds of slaughtered] over fresh water in an earthen vessel.”/"[וצוה הכהן ושחט את-הצפור האחת] אל-כלי-חרש על-מים חיים. An analogy is made between a vessel to water. Just as water that no work had been done with it, even a vessel that no work had been done with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:3)


**רְבִיעִית.** מִשּׁוּם דִּכְתִיב (שם) בְּדַם הַצִּפֹּר הַשְּׁחֻטָה וּבַמַּיִם הַחַיִּים, מַיִם חַיִּים שֶׁדַּם הַצִּפּוֹר נִכָּר בָּהֶן. וְשִׁעֲרוּ חֲכָמִים רְבִיעִית:


רביעית – because it is written (Leviticus 14:6): “[and dip them together with the live bird] in the blood of the bird that was slaughtered over the fresh water,” living water that the blood of the bird is recognized in them. And the Sages estimated/measured it as a quarter-log [of living water].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:4)


**מַיִם חַיִּים.** שֶׁלְּעוֹלָם נוֹבְעִים וְאֵינָן פּוֹסְקִים:


מים חיים – that always flows and doesn’t stop.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:5)


**צִפֳּרִים דְּרוֹר.** שֶׁדָּרוֹת בַּבַּיִת כְּבַשָּׂדֶה:


צפרים דרור (undomesticated birds/free birds) – that live in the house as they do in the field (see also Psalms 84:4 and Proverbs 26:2).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:6)


**וְקוֹבְרָהּ בְּפָנָיו.** לְפִי שֶׁאֲסוּרָה בַהֲנָאָה:


וקוברה לפניו – because it is prohibited for deriving benefit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:7)


**וּשְׁנִי תוֹלַעַת.** לְשׁוֹן צֶמֶר צָבוּעַ שָׁנִי. כרמז"י בְּלַעַ"ז:


ושני תולעת (scarlet-dyed yarn) – it is the language of scarlet colored wool. KARMAZI in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:8)


**וּכְרָכָן בִּשְׁיָרֵי לָשׁוֹן.** עֵץ אֶרֶז וְאֵזוֹב וּשְׁנִי תוֹלַעַת הָיָה מַנִּיחָן יַחַד, וְהַלָּשׁוֹן יוֹצֵא וְעוֹדֵף עַל עֵץ אֶרֶז וְאֵזוֹב, וְהָיָה כוֹרְכָן בּוֹ דַּהֲווּ שְׁלָשְׁתָּן בַּאֲגֻדָּה אֶחָת:


וכרכן בשיירי לשון (and bound them together with the ends of the strip of wool) – the cedar wood, hyssop and crimson stuff (i.e., wool), he would place them together, and the strip would go out and be a surplus over the cedar wood and hyssop, and he would bind them together so that all three of them would be in one band.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:9)


**וְהִקִּיף.** לְשׁוֹן סְמִיכָה וְהַקְרָבָה. כְּמוֹ [בֵּיצָה דַּף לב] מַקִּיפִים שְׁתֵּי חָבִיּוֹת. לִתְרֹם שֶׁלֹּא מִן הַמֻּקָּף. כְּלוֹמַר הָיָה סוֹמֵךְ לָהֶן רָאשֵׁי אֲגַפִּים וְרֹאשׁ הַזָּנָב שֶׁל הַצִּפּוֹר הַחַיָּה, וְטוֹבֵל כֻּלָּן בְּיַחַד בַּדָּם וּבַמַּיִם שֶׁבַּכְּלִי חֶרֶס:


והקיף – the language of proximity and bringing close. Like [Tractate Betzah 32b, Chapter 4, Mishnah 5]: They bring two jars together, to give the heave-offering that is not from that which is surrounded, meaning to say that he would place adjacent to them the tips of the wings and the head of the tail of the living [second] bird, and immerse all of them together in blood and in water that is the earthenware vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:10)


**לְאַחַר יָדוֹ.** עַל גַּב יָדוֹ שֶׁל מְצֹרָע:


לאחר ידו – on the back of the hand of the leper.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:11)


**וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים עַל מִצְחוֹ.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים:


ויש אומרים על מצחו – but the Halakha is not according to those who say [to sprinkle it on his forehead].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:1:12)


**עַל הַשְּׁקוֹף.** שֶׁל בַּיִת שֶׁהוּא מְנֻגָּע:


על השקוף – of the house that is plagued/leprous.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:2:1)


**לֹא לַיָּם.** אִם הוּא בְעִיר הַיּוֹשֶׁבֶת עַל שְׂפַת הַיָּם:


לא לים – if he is in a city that sits on the border of the sea.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:2:2)


**וְלֹא לַמִּדְבָּר.** אִם הָעִיר יוֹשֶׁבֶת אֵצֶל הַמִּדְבָּר:


ולא למדבר – if the city sits near the wilderness/desert.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:2:3)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר וְשִׁלַּח אֶת הַצִּפֹּר הַחַיָּה אֶל מִחוּץ לָעִיר אֶל פְּנֵי הַשָּׂדֶה.** מִכָּאן שֶׁאֵינוֹ הוֹפֵךְ פָּנָיו לָעִיר:


שנאמר ושלח את הצפור החיה אל מחוץ לעיר אל פני השדה – from here that he does not turn his face towards the city.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:2:4)


**הֶעֱבִיר תַּעַר עַל כָּל בְּשָׂרוֹ.** לָאו דַּוְקָא. שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה מְגַלֵּחַ אֶלָּא כָּל מְקוֹם כִּנּוּס שְׂעָר, [וְנִרְאֶה, לַאֲפוֹקֵי שֵׂעָר] בֵּית הַסְּתָרִים, דְּאָמַר קְרָא (ויקרא יד) וְהָיָה בַיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי יְגַלַּח אֶת כָּל שְׂעָרוֹ, כְּלָל. אֶת רֹאשׁוֹ וְאֶת זְקָנוֹ וְאֵת גַּבֹּת עֵינָיו, פְּרָט. וְאֶת כָּל שְׂעָרוֹ יְגַלֵּחַ, חָזַר וְכָלַל. כְּלָל וּפְרָט וּכְלָל, אִי אַתָּה דָן אֶלָּא כְּעֵין הַפְּרָט. מָה הַפְּרָט מְפֹרָשׁ מְקוֹם כִּנּוּס שֵׂעָר וְנִרְאֶה, אַף כָּל מְקוֹם כִּנּוּס שֵׂעָר וְנִרְאֶה. וְתִגְלַחַת רִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁמְּגַלֵּחַ בִּזְמַן חִלּוּטוֹ, לְמָדוּהָ בִּגְזֵרָה שָׁוָה מִתִּגְלַחַת שְׁנִיָּה, שֶׁמְּגַלֵּחַ שְׁנִיָּה לְאַחַר שֶׁסָּפַר שִׁבְעַת יָמִים, דְּנֶאֱמָר בִּימֵי חִלּוּטוֹ וְגִלַּח אֶת כָּל שְׂעָרוֹ וְגוֹ', וְנֶאֱמָר בִּימֵי סָפְרוֹ יְגַלַּח אֶת כָּל שְׂעָרוֹ וְגוֹ', מַה תִּגְלַחַת הָאֲמוּרָה בִימֵי סָפְרוֹ פְּרָט לְבֵית הַסְּתָרִים, אַף תִּגְלַחַת הָאֲמוּרָה בִימֵי גָמְרוֹ פְּרָט לְבֵית הַסְּתָרִים:


העביר תער על כל בשרו – not exactly. For he would not shave other than every place where there is a gathering of hair, [and it appears, to exclude the hair of] the covered parts of the body/posterior, for Scripture states (Leviticus 14:9): “On the seventh day he shall shave off all his hair,” – a generalization; “of head, beard and eyebrows” – a detail; “When he has shaved off all his hair,” he returned and made a generalization, the application of the Halakha under discussion is limited to items somewhat similar to the detail, just as the detail is explained, a place where hair is gathered and appears, even every place where there is a gathering of hair and it appears. But the first shaving that he shaves at the time of his certification as a leper, we learn it through a verbal analogy from the second shaving, that he shaves a second time after he counted seven days, for it states during the days of his being a certified leper (Leviticus 14:8), “[The one to be purified shall wash his clothes,] shave off all his hair, [and bathe in water; then he shall be pure], etc., and it states in the days of his counting that he shall shave all his hair, etc. (see above). Just as shaving during the days of his counting, except in the covered parts of the body/posterior, even the shaving that is stated during the days of his completion, excluding the covered parts of the body/posterior.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:2:5)


**טָהוֹר מִלְּטַמֵּא בְּבִיאָה.** וּמִלְּטַמֵּא מִשְׁכָּב וּמוֹשָׁב, כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁהָיָה מְטַמֵּא בִימֵי חִלּוּטוֹ:


טהור מלטמא בביאה – and to defile through lying and sitting, in the manner that he would defile in the days of his being a certified leper.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:2:6)


**מְטַמֵּא כַשֶּׁרֶץ.** לְטַמֵּא אָדָם וּבְגָדִים בְּמַגָּע, אֲבָל לֹא בְמַשָּׂא:


מטמא כשרץ – to defile a person and clothing through contact, but not through carrying.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:2:7)


**מְנֻדֶּה בְּבֵיתוֹ.** לְעִנְיָן זֶה שֶׁאָסוּר בְּתַשְׁמִישׁ הַמִּטָּה, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יד) וְיָשַׁב מִחוּץ לְאָהֳלוֹ, וְאֵין אָהֳלוֹ אֶלָּא אִשְׁתּוֹ, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר (דברים ה) שׁוּבוּ לָכֶם לְאָהֳלֵיכֶם, כְּשֶׁהִתִּיר לָהֶם תַּשְׁמִישׁ הַמִּטָּה לְאַחַר מַעֲמַד הַר סִינַי:


מנודה בביתו – for this matter that he is prohibited to engage in sexual relations, as it is written (Leviticus 14:8): “but he must remain outside his tent [seven days],” but [the word], "אהלו"/his tent is none other than his wife. And similarly it states (Deuteronomy 5:27): “[Go, say to them,] ‘Return to your tents,’” that Moses permitted to them sexual relations after the Revelation of Mount Sinai.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:3:1)


**טָהוֹר מִלְּטַמֵּא כַּשֶּׁרֶץ.** וּפוֹסֵל תְּרוּמָה [וְתוּ לֹא]:


טהור מלטמא כשרץ – ‘but he invalidates heave-offering [and nothing more].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:3:2)


**וְשָׁלֹשׁ טְהָרוֹת בַּיּוֹלֶדֶת.** וְהוּא הַדִּין בְּזָב בַּעַל שָׁלֹשׁ רְאִיּוֹת, וּבְזָבָה. דִּבְכֻלְּהוּ טָבַל וְעָלָה אוֹכֵל בַּמַּעֲשֵׂר, הֶעֱרִיב שִׁמְשׁוֹ אוֹכֵל בַּתְּרוּמָה, הֵבִיא כַפָּרָתוֹ אוֹכֵל בַּקָּדָשִׁים, הֲרֵי שָׁלֹשׁ טְהָרוֹת. וְהַאי דְּנָקַט יוֹלֶדֶת טְפֵי מִזָּב וְזָבָה, מִשּׁוּם דִּבְיוֹלֶדֶת כְּתִיב בְּהֶדְיָא (שם יב) וְכִפֶּר עָלֶיהָ הַכֹּהֵן וְטָהֵרָה, לַאֲכִילַת קָדָשִׁים:


ושלש טהרות כיולדת – and the same law applies to a person with gonorrhea who had three attacks and concerning a woman who had a flux, that in all of them, he immersed [in a Mikveh] and came up, he eats [Second] Tithe. When his (i.e., the leper’s seventh day) sun has set, he eats heave offering. When he brought his atonement sacrifice, he eats Holy Things. These are three purifications. And that it (i.e., the Mishnah) took that of the woman in childbirth, it is written explicitly (Leviticus 12:8): “The priest shall make expiation on her behalf, and she shall be pure, [if married to a Kohen), for the eating of Holy Things.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:4:1)


**וְהַלְוִיִּם.** בְּעֵת שֶׁנִּבְחֲרוּ לְשֵׁרוּת בַּמִּדְבָּר, וְאֵינָהּ נוֹהֶגֶת לְדוֹרוֹת:


והלוים – at the time that they chosen for service in the wilderness (see Numbers 8:7: “[This is what you shall do to purify them: sprinkle on them water of purification,] and let them go over their whole body with a razor, [and wash their clothes; thus they shall be purified].”), but it does not apply for all generations.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Negaim 14:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Negaim/r/14:5:1)


**מִצְוָתָן שֶׁיִּהְיוּ שָׁווֹת.** דְּלִכְתּוֹב רַחֲמָנָא צִפֳּרִים, וּמִעוּט צִפֳּרִים שְׁתַּיִם. שְׁתֵּי דְכָתַב רַחֲמָנָא לָמָּה לִּי, אֶלָּא לְלַמֵּד שֶׁיִּהְיוּ שְׁתֵּיהֶם שָׁווֹת:
